<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\ProfileController;
use App\Http\Controllers\ProductController;
use App\Http\Controllers\PurchaseController;
use App\Http\Controllers\SalesHistoryController;
use App\Http\Controllers\DashboardController;
use App\Http\Controllers\ReportController;
use App\Http\Controllers\PrescriptionController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use Inertia\Inertia;

Route::redirect('/', '/login');

// Akses Dasar untuk Semua User yang Sudah Login
Route::middleware(['auth', 'verified'])->group(function () {
    // Rute Dashboard (Wajib ada agar Ziggy tidak error)
    Route::get('/dashboard', [DashboardController::class, 'index'])->name('dashboard');
    
    // Profile Management
    Route::get('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'edit'])->name('profile.edit');
    Route::patch('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'update'])->name('profile.update');
    Route::delete('/profile', [ProfileController::class, 'destroy'])->name('profile.destroy');

    // POS & Penjualan (Bisa diakses Superadmin, Apoteker, dan Kasir)
    // Menggunakan middleware auth biasa agar tidak bentrok dengan string role_id database
    Route::get('/pos', [App\Http\Controllers\PosController::class, 'index'])->name('pos.index');
    Route::post('/pos', [App\Http\Controllers\PosController::class, 'store'])->name('pos.store');
    Route::get('/pos/receipt/{invoice}', [App\Http\Controllers\PosController::class, 'printReceipt'])->name('pos.receipt');
    
    // Sales History & Bulk Actions
    Route::get('/sales-history', [SalesHistoryController::class, 'index'])->name('sales.history');
    Route::get('/sales-history/trash', [SalesHistoryController::class, 'trash'])->name('sales.trash');
    Route::delete('/sales-history/{id}', [SalesHistoryController::class, 'destroy'])->name('sales.destroy');
    Route::post('/sales-history/{id}/restore', [SalesHistoryController::class, 'restore'])->name('sales.restore');
    Route::delete('/sales-history/{id}/force-delete', [SalesHistoryController::class, 'forceDelete'])->name('sales.force-delete');
    Route::post('/sales/bulk-destroy', [SalesHistoryController::class, 'bulkDestroy'])->name('sales.bulk-destroy');
    Route::post('/sales/bulk-restore', [SalesHistoryController::class, 'bulkRestore'])->name('sales.bulk-restore');
    
    // Prescriptions & OCR
    Route::post('/api/prescriptions/analyze', [PrescriptionController::class, 'analyzeImage'])->name('api.prescriptions.analyze');
    Route::get('/api/prescriptions/{number}', [PrescriptionController::class, 'getForPos'])->name('api.prescriptions.get');
    Route::resource('prescriptions', PrescriptionController::class)->except(['show', 'edit', 'update']);
    Route::get('/prescriptions/{prescription}/edit', [PrescriptionController::class, 'edit'])->name('prescriptions.edit');
    Route::put('/prescriptions/{prescription}', [PrescriptionController::class, 'update'])->name('prescriptions.update');
});

FILE: app/Exports/ReportExport.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Exports;

use Illuminate\Contracts\View\View;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\FromView;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\ShouldAutoSize;

class ReportExport implements FromView, ShouldAutoSize
{
    protected $type;
    protected $data;
    protected $startDate;
    protected $endDate;
    protected $title;

    public function __construct($type, $data, $startDate, $endDate, $title)
    {
        $this->type = $type;
        $this->data = $data;
        $this->startDate = $startDate;
        $this->endDate = $endDate;
        $this->title = $title;
    }

    public function view(): View
    {
        return view('reports.print', [
            'type' => $this->type,
            'data' => $this->data,
            'startDate' => $this->startDate,
            'endDate' => $this->endDate,
            'title' => $this->title,
            'isExcel' => true
        ]);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\AuditLog;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;

class AuditLogController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
    $currentTab = $request->get('tab', 'akun');

    $logs = \App\Models\AuditLog::with(['user.role'])
        ->where('module', $currentTab)
        ->latest()
        ->get();

    return Inertia::render('AuditLogs/Index', [
        'logs' => $logs,
        'currentTab' => $currentTab,
    ]);
}
}

=========================================================
FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/AuthenticatedSessionController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Auth\LoginRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class AuthenticatedSessionController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the login view.
     */
    public function create(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Auth/Login', [
            'canResetPassword' => Route::has('password.request'),
            'status' => session('status'),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming authentication request.
     */
    public function store(LoginRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->authenticate();
        $request->session()->regenerate();

        $user = $request->user()->load('role');
        $roleSlug = $user->role?->slug;

        if ($roleSlug === 'apoteker') {
            return redirect()->route('products.index');
        } elseif ($roleSlug === 'kasir') {
            return redirect()->route('dashboard');
        }

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }

    /**
     * Destroy an authenticated session.
     */
    public function destroy(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        Auth::guard('web')->logout();

        $request->session()->invalidate();

        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return redirect('/');
    }
}


=========================================================
FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/ConfirmablePasswordController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class ConfirmablePasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Show the confirm password view.
     */
    public function show(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Auth/ConfirmPassword');
    }

    /**
     * Confirm the user's password.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if (! Auth::guard('web')->validate([
            'email' => $request->user()->email,
            'password' => $request->password,
        ])) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'password' => __('auth.password'),
            ]);
        }

        $request->session()->put('auth.password_confirmed_at', time());

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }
}

FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/EmailVerificationNotificationController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class EmailVerificationNotificationController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Send a new email verification notification.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
        }

        $request->user()->sendEmailVerificationNotification();

        return back()->with('status', 'verification-link-sent');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class EmailVerificationPromptController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the email verification prompt.
     */
    public function __invoke(Request $request): RedirectResponse|Response
    {
        return $request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()
                    ? redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false))
                    : Inertia::render('Auth/VerifyEmail', ['status' => session('status')]);
    }
}


=========================================================
FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/NewPasswordController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\PasswordReset;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class NewPasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the password reset view.
     */
    public function create(Request $request): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Auth/ResetPassword', [
            'email' => $request->email,
            'token' => $request->route('token'),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming new password request.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'token' => 'required',
            'email' => 'required|email',
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
        ]);

        // Here we will attempt to reset the user's password. If it is successful we
        // will update the password on an actual user model and persist it to the
        // database. Otherwise we will parse the error and return the response.
        $status = Password::reset(
            $request->only('email', 'password', 'password_confirmation', 'token'),
            function ($user) use ($request) {
                $user->forceFill([
                    'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
                    'remember_token' => Str::random(60),
                ])->save();

                event(new PasswordReset($user));
            }
        );

        // If the password was successfully reset, we will redirect the user back to
        // the application's home authenticated view. If there is an error we can
        // redirect them back to where they came from with their error message.
        if ($status == Password::PASSWORD_RESET) {
            return redirect()->route('login')->with('status', __($status));
        }

        throw ValidationException::withMessages([
            'email' => [trans($status)],
        ]);
    }
}


=========================================================
FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/PasswordController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;

class PasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Update the user's password.
     */
    public function update(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'current_password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
            'password' => ['required', Password::defaults(), 'confirmed'],
        ]);

        $request->user()->update([
            'password' => Hash::make($validated['password']),
        ]);

        return back();
    }
}

FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/PasswordResetLinkController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class PasswordResetLinkController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the password reset link request view.
     */
    public function create(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Auth/ForgotPassword', [
            'status' => session('status'),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming password reset link request.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'email' => 'required|email',
        ]);

        // We will send the password reset link to this user. Once we have attempted
        // to send the link, we will examine the response then see the message we
        // need to show to the user. Finally, we'll send out a proper response.
        $status = Password::sendResetLink(
            $request->only('email')
        );

        if ($status == Password::RESET_LINK_SENT) {
            return back()->with('status', __($status));
        }

        throw ValidationException::withMessages([
            'email' => [trans($status)],
        ]);
    }
}


=========================================================
FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/RegisteredUserController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Registered;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class RegisteredUserController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the registration view.
     */
    public function create(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Auth/Register');
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming registration request.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'email' => 'required|string|lowercase|email|max:255|unique:'.User::class,
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
        ]);

        $user = User::create([
            'name' => $request->name,
            'email' => $request->email,
            'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
        ]);

        event(new Registered($user));

        Auth::login($user);

        return redirect(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }
}


=========================================================
FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Auth/VerifyEmailController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Verified;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\EmailVerificationRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;

class VerifyEmailController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Mark the authenticated user's email address as verified.
     */
    public function __invoke(EmailVerificationRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
        }

        if ($request->user()->markEmailAsVerified()) {
            event(new Verified($request->user()));
        }

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
    }
}

FILE: app/Http/Controllers/DashboardController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use Inertia\Inertia;
use App\Models\Sale;
use App\Models\Medicine;
use App\Services\AlertService;
use Carbon\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class DashboardController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $expiringBatches = AlertService::getExpiringBatches(90);
        $lowStockMedicines = AlertService::getLowStockMedicines();
        
        $filter = $request->get('period', 'daily');
        $today = Carbon::today();
        
        $todayRevenue = Sale::whereDate('sale_date', $today)->sum('total_amount');
        $todayTransactions = Sale::whereDate('sale_date', $today)->count();
        
        $lowStockCount = Medicine::whereColumn('stock', '<=', 'min_stock')->count();
        $activeMedicines = Medicine::where('is_active', true)->count();

        $chartData = [];
        if ($filter == 'weekly') {
            for ($i = 3; $i >= 0; $i--) {
                $start = Carbon::now()->subWeeks($i)->startOfWeek();
                $end = Carbon::now()->subWeeks($i)->endOfWeek();
                $total = Sale::whereBetween('sale_date', [$start, $end])->sum('total_amount');
                $chartData[] = [
                    'name' => 'Minggu ' . (4 - $i), // Syntax error '-' diperbaiki
                    'total' => (float) $total
                ];
            }
        } elseif ($filter == 'monthly') {
            for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
                $month = Carbon::now()->subMonths($i);
                $total = Sale::whereYear('sale_date', $month->year)
                            ->whereMonth('sale_date', $month->month)
                            ->sum('total_amount');
                $chartData[] = [
                    'name' => $month->locale('id')->isoFormat('MMM YYYY'),
                    'total' => (float) $total
                ];
            }
        } elseif ($filter == 'yearly') {
            for ($i = 2; $i >= 0; $i--) {
                $year = Carbon::now()->subYears($i)->year;
                $total = Sale::whereYear('sale_date', $year)->sum('total_amount');
                $chartData[] = [
                    'name' => (string) $year,
                    'total' => (float) $total
                ];
            }
        } else {
            $last7Days = Sale::select(DB::raw('DATE(sale_date) as date'), DB::raw('SUM(total_amount) as total'))
                ->where('sale_date', '>=', Carbon::now()->subDays(6)->startOfDay())
                ->groupBy('date')
                ->orderBy('date')
                ->get();

            for ($i = 6; $i >= 0; $i--) {
                $dateStr = Carbon::now()->subDays($i)->format('Y-m-d');
                $match = $last7Days->firstWhere('date', $dateStr);
                $chartData[] = [
                    'name' => Carbon::now()->subDays($i)->locale('id')->isoFormat('ddd'),
                    'total' => $match ? (float) $match->total : 0
                ];
            }
        }

        $topProducts = DB::table('sale_details')
            ->join('sales', 'sale_details.sale_id', '=', 'sales.id')
            ->join('medicines', 'sale_details.medicine_id', '=', 'medicines.id')
            ->select('medicines.name', DB::raw('SUM(sale_details.quantity) as value'))
            ->groupBy('medicines.id', 'medicines.name')
            ->orderByDesc('value')
            ->limit(4)
            ->get();

        $totalCompleted = Sale::count();
        $paymentStats = DB::table('sales')
            ->select('payment_method', DB::raw('COUNT(*) as count'))
            ->groupBy('payment_method')
            ->get();

        $paymentDistribution = $paymentStats->map(function ($item) use ($totalCompleted) {
            return [
                'method' => strtoupper($item->payment_method),
                'count' => $item->count,
                'percentage' => $totalCompleted > 0 ? round(($item->count / $totalCompleted) * 100) : 0
            ];
        })->keyBy('method');

        $recentTransactions = Sale::with('user')
            ->orderBy('sale_date', 'desc')
            ->take(5)
            ->get();

        return Inertia::render('Dashboard/Index', [
            'stats' => [
                'expiringBatches' => $expiringBatches,
                'lowStockMedicines' => $lowStockMedicines,
                'todayRevenue' => (float) $todayRevenue,
                'todayTransactions' => $todayTransactions,
                'lowStockCount' => $lowStockCount,
                'activeMedicines' => $activeMedicines,
            ],
            'chartData' => $chartData,
            'topProducts' => $topProducts,
            'paymentDistribution' => $paymentDistribution,
            'recentTransactions' => $recentTransactions,
            'currentFilter' => $filter,
        ]);
    }
}

=========================================================
FILE: app/Http/Controllers/PosController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Medicine;
use App\Models\Sale;
use App\Models\SaleDetail;
use App\Models\Prescription;
use App\Services\InventoryService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Inertia\Inertia;

class PosController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $today = now()->toDateString();

        $medicines = Medicine::where('is_active', true)
            ->whereHas('batches', function ($query) use ($today) {
                $query->where('stock_quantity', '>', 0)
                    ->where('expired_date', '>=', $today);
            })
            ->withSum(['batches as real_stock' => function ($query) use ($today) {
                $query->where('stock_quantity', '>', 0)
                    ->where('expired_date', '>=', $today);
            }], 'stock_quantity')
            ->get()
            ->map(function ($medicine) {
                $medicine->stock = (int) ($medicine->real_stock ?? 0);
                return $medicine;
            })
            ->filter(fn($m) => $m->stock > 0)
            ->values();

        return Inertia::render('Pos/Index', [
            'medicines' => $medicines
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'cart' => 'required|array|min:1',
            'cart.*.quantity' => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'cart.*.price' => 'required|numeric',
            'paid_amount' => 'required|numeric',
            'change_amount' => 'required|numeric',
            'payment_method' => 'required|string',
            'prescription_id' => 'nullable|exists:prescriptions,id',
        ]);

        DB::beginTransaction();
        try {
            if ($request->filled('prescription_id')) {
                $prescription = Prescription::where('id', $request->prescription_id)
                    ->lockForUpdate()
                    ->first();

                if (!$prescription) {
                    throw new \Exception('Resep tidak ditemukan.');
                }

                if ($prescription->status !== 'DRAFT') {
                    throw new \Exception('Resep ini sudah pernah ditebus.');
                }

                $prescription->update(['status' => 'DISPENSED']);
            }

            $invoiceNumber = 'TRX-' . date('YmdHis') . '-' . rand(100, 999);
            $totalAmount = collect($request->cart)->sum(fn($i) => $i['quantity'] * $i['price']);

            $sale = Sale::create([
                'invoice_number' => $invoiceNumber,
                'user_id' => auth()->id(),
                'total_amount' => $totalAmount,
                'subtotal_amount'  => $totalAmount,
                'paid_amount'    => $request->paid_amount,
                'change_amount' => $request->change_amount,
                'payment_method' => $request->payment_method,
                'sale_date' => now(),
            ]);

            foreach ($request->cart as $item) {
                $medicineId = $item['medicine_id'] ?? $item['id'];
                $unitHpp = InventoryService::deductStockFEFO($medicineId, $item['quantity']);

                $activeBatch = \App\Models\ProductBatch::where('medicine_id', $medicineId)
                    ->where('stock_quantity', '>', 0)
                    ->orderBy('expired_date', 'asc')
                    ->first();

                SaleDetail::create([
                    'sale_id'        => $sale->id,
                    'medicine_id'    => $medicineId,
                    'batch_number'   => $activeBatch ? $activeBatch->batch_number : 'BATCH-AUTO',
                    'quantity'       => $item['quantity'],
                    'purchase_price' => $unitHpp,
                    'selling_price'  => $item['price'],
                    'subtotal'       => $item['quantity'] * $item['price'],
                ]);
            }

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->back()->with([
                'success' => 'Transaksi kasir berhasil diproses!',
                'invoice_number' => $invoiceNumber
            ]);
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => 'Gagal memproses transaksi: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Prescription;
use App\Models\PrescriptionItem;
use App\Models\Medicine;
use App\Services\InventoryService;
use App\Services\GoogleAIService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Inertia\Inertia;

class PrescriptionController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $prescriptions = Prescription::with(['user', 'items.medicine'])
            ->orderBy('created_at', 'desc')
            ->get();

        return Inertia::render('Prescription/Index', [
            'prescriptions' => $prescriptions
        ]);
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $medicines = Medicine::where('is_active', true)
            ->select('id', 'name', 'base_unit', 'sku', 'stock')
            ->get();

        return Inertia::render('Prescription/Create', [
            'medicines' => $medicines
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'patient_name'        => 'required|string|max:255',
            'doctor_name'         => 'required|string|max:255',
            'prescription_date'   => 'required|date',
            'notes'               => 'nullable|string',
            'attachment'          => 'nullable|file|mimes:jpg,jpeg,png,pdf|max:2048',
            'items'               => 'required|array|min:1',
            'items.*.medicine_id' => 'required|exists:medicines,id', 
            'items.*.quantity'    => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'items.*.signa'       => 'required|string|max:255',
        ]);

        DB::beginTransaction();
        try {
            $attachmentPath = null;
            if ($request->hasFile('attachment')) {
                $attachmentPath = $request->file('attachment')->store('prescriptions', 'public');
            }

            $randomNumber = str_pad(mt_rand(1, 999), 3, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
            $prescriptionNumber = 'RSP-' . date('Ymd') . '-' . $randomNumber;

            $prescription = Prescription::create([
                'prescription_number' => $prescriptionNumber,
                'patient_name'        => $request->patient_name,
                'doctor_name'         => $request->doctor_name,
                'prescription_date'   => $request->prescription_date,
                'notes'               => $request->notes,
                'attachment'          => $attachmentPath,
                'user_id'             => auth()->id(),
                'status'              => 'DRAFT',
            ]);

            foreach ($request->items as $item) {
                PrescriptionItem::create([
                    'prescription_id' => $prescription->id,
                    'medicine_id'     => $item['medicine_id'],
                    'quantity'        => $item['quantity'],
                    'signa'           => $item['signa'],
                ]);

                InventoryService::deductStockFEFO($item['medicine_id'], $item['quantity']);
            }

            DB::commit();
            return redirect()->route('prescriptions.index')->with('success', 'Resep berhasil dicatat dan stok batch telah diperbarui!');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => 'Gagal menyimpan resep: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
    }

    public function getForPos($prescription_number)
    {
        $prescription = Prescription::with('items.medicine')
            ->where('prescription_number', $prescription_number)
            ->where('status', 'DRAFT')
            ->first();

        if (!$prescription) {
            return response()->json(['message' => 'Resep tidak ditemukan atau sudah ditebus!'], 404);
        }

        return response()->json($prescription);
    }

    public function analyzeImage(Request $request)
    {
        try {
            $request->validate([
                'attachment' => 'required|file|mimes:jpg,jpeg,png,webp|max:5120'
            ]);
            
            $file = $request->file('attachment');
            $base64Image = base64_encode(file_get_contents($file->getPathname()));
            $mimeType = $file->getClientMimeType();

            $allMedicines = Medicine::where('is_active', true)->select('id', 'name')->get()->toArray();
            $medicineListJson = json_encode($allMedicines);

            $prompt = "Anda adalah sistem OCR apotek cerdas. Analisis gambar resep dokter ini. " .
                    "Daftar obat yang tersedia di database apotek kami: {$medicineListJson}. \n" .
                    "Ekstrak informasi berikut dan kembalikan HANYA format JSON murni tanpa markdown: \n" .
                    "{\n" .
                    "  \"patient_name\": \"Nama Pasien\",\n" .
                    "  \"doctor_name\": \"dr. Nama Dokter\",\n" .
                    "  \"prescription_date\": \"YYYY-MM-DD\",\n" .
                    "  \"items\": [\n" .
                    "    {\"medicine_id\": 1, \"name\": \"Nama Obat\", \"quantity\": 10, \"signa\": \"3x1 sesudah makan\"}\n" .
                    "  ]\n" .
                    "}";

            // Menggunakan GoogleAIService (bisa passing nama model apa saja di parameter ke-4)
            // Contoh: 'gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-3.8-flash', 'gemini-2.5-pro'
            $parsedData = GoogleAIService::generateContent($prompt, $base64Image, $mimeType, 'gemini-2.5-flash');

            return response()->json($parsedData);

        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            Log::error('Prescription OCR Error: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return response()->json(['message' => $e->getMessage()], 500);
        }
    }

    public function destroy(Prescription $prescription)
    {
        $prescription->delete();
        return redirect()->route('prescriptions.index')->with('success', 'Resep berhasil dihapus.');
    }

    public function edit(Prescription $prescription)
    {
        if ($prescription->status !== 'DRAFT') {
            return redirect()->route('prescriptions.index')->with('error', 'Resep yang sudah ditebus tidak dapat diubah.');
        }

        $prescription->load('items.medicine');

        $medicines = Medicine::where('is_active', true)
            ->select('id', 'name', 'base_unit', 'sku', 'stock')
            ->get();

        return Inertia::render('Prescription/Edit', [
            'prescription' => $prescription,
            'medicines'    => $medicines
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Prescription $prescription)
    {
        if ($prescription->status !== 'DRAFT') {
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => 'Resep yang sudah ditebus tidak dapat diubah.']);
        }

        $request->validate([
            'patient_name'        => 'required|string|max:255',
            'doctor_name'         => 'required|string|max:255',
            'prescription_date'   => 'required|date',
            'notes'               => 'nullable|string',
            'items'               => 'required|array|min:1',
            'items.*.medicine_id' => 'required|exists:medicines,id', 
            'items.*.quantity'    => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'items.*.signa'       => 'required|string|max:255',
        ]);

        DB::beginTransaction();
        try {
            $prescription->update([
                'patient_name'      => $request->patient_name,
                'doctor_name'       => $request->doctor_name,
                'prescription_date' => $request->prescription_date,
                'notes'             => $request->notes,
            ]);

            $prescription->items()->delete();

            foreach ($request->items as $item) {
                PrescriptionItem::create([
                    'prescription_id' => $prescription->id,
                    'medicine_id'     => $item['medicine_id'],
                    'quantity'        => $item['quantity'],
                    'signa'           => $item['signa'],
                ]);
            }

            DB::commit();
            return redirect()->route('prescriptions.index')->with('success', 'Resep berhasil diperbarui!');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => 'Gagal memperbarui resep: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/Http/Controllers/ProductController.php

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Medicine;
use App\Models\MedicineCategory;
use App\Models\Supplier;
use App\Models\Rack;
use App\Models\AuditLog;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Inertia\Inertia;

class ProductController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $products = Medicine::with(['category', 'supplier', 'rack'])->latest()->get();

        return Inertia::render('Products/Index', [
            'products' => $products
        ]);
    }

    public function create()
    {
        return Inertia::render('Products/Create', [
            'categories' => MedicineCategory::where('is_active', true)->get(),
            'suppliers' => Supplier::where('is_active', true)->get(),
            'racks' => Rack::where('is_active', true)->get(),
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        if (!$request->filled('sku')) {
            $request->merge(['sku' => 'MED-' . date('Ym') . '-' . strtoupper(Str::random(4))]);
        }

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'sku' => 'required|string|unique:medicines,sku',
            'barcode' => 'nullable|string|unique:medicines,barcode',
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'generic_name' => 'nullable|string|max:255',
            'medicine_category_id' => 'required|exists:medicine_categories,id',
            'supplier_id' => 'nullable|exists:suppliers,id',
            'rack_id' => 'nullable|exists:racks,id',
            'dosage_form' => 'nullable|string|max:100',
            'dosage_strength' => 'nullable|string|max:100',
            'requires_prescription' => 'boolean',
            'base_unit' => 'required|string|max:50',
            'selling_price' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'min_stock' => 'required|integer|min:0',
            'max_stock' => 'nullable|integer|min:0',
        ]);

        $medicine = Medicine::create($validated);
        
        AuditLog::record('inventaris', 'CREATE', "Menambahkan master obat baru: {$medicine->name}");

        return redirect()->route('products.index')->with('success', 'Data Obat berhasil ditambahkan ke master inventaris.');
    }

    public function destroy(Medicine $product)
    {
        $name = $product->name;
        $product->delete(); 
        
        AuditLog::record('inventaris', 'DELETE', "Menghapus data obat: {$name}");
        return back()->with('success', "Obat {$name} berhasil dihapus dari sistem.");
    }

    public function edit(Medicine $product)
    {
        return Inertia::render('Products/Edit', [
            'product' => $product,
            'categories' => MedicineCategory::where('is_active', true)->get(),
            'suppliers' => Supplier::where('is_active', true)->get(),
            'racks' => Rack::where('is_active', true)->get(),
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Medicine $product)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'sku' => 'required|string|unique:medicines,sku,' . $product->id,
            'barcode' => 'nullable|string|unique:medicines,barcode,' . $product->id,
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'generic_name' => 'nullable|string|max:255',
            'medicine_category_id' => 'required|exists:medicine_categories,id',
            'supplier_id' => 'nullable|exists:suppliers,id',
            'rack_id' => 'nullable|exists:racks,id',
            'dosage_form' => 'nullable|string|max:100',
            'dosage_strength' => 'nullable|string|max:100',
            'requires_prescription' => 'boolean',
            'base_unit' => 'required|string|max:50',
            'selling_price' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'min_stock' => 'required|integer|min:0',
            'max_stock' => 'nullable|integer|min:0',
        ]);

        $product->update($validated);
        
        AuditLog::record('inventaris', 'UPDATE', "Memperbarui data master obat: {$product->name}");

        return redirect()->route('products.index')->with('success', 'Perubahan Master Obat berhasil disimpan.');
    }
}

FILE: app/Http/Controllers/ProfileController.php

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Requests\ProfileUpdateRequest;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Auth\MustVerifyEmail;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Redirect;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class ProfileController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the user's profile form.
     */
    public function edit(Request $request): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('Profile/Edit', [
            'mustVerifyEmail' => $request->user() instanceof MustVerifyEmail,
            'status' => session('status'),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Update the user's profile information.
     */
    public function update(ProfileUpdateRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->user()->fill($request->validated());

        if ($request->user()->isDirty('email')) {
            $request->user()->email_verified_at = null;
        }

        $request->user()->save();

        return Redirect::route('profile.edit');
    }

    /**
     * Delete the user's account.
     */
    public function destroy(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
        ]);

        $user = $request->user();

        Auth::logout();

        $user->delete();

        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return Redirect::to('/');
    }
}

FILE: app/Http/Controllers/PurchaseController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use Barryvdh\DomPDF\Facade\Pdf;
use App\Models\Purchase;
use App\Models\Supplier;
use App\Models\Medicine;
use App\Models\AuditLog;
use App\Models\ProductBatch;
use App\Services\InventoryService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class PurchaseController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $purchases = Purchase::with(['supplier', 'user', 'details.medicine' => function($q) {
            $q->withTrashed();
        }])
        ->latest()
        ->get();

        return Inertia::render('Purchases/Index', [
            'purchases' => $purchases
        ]);
    }

    public function create()
    {
        return Inertia::render('Purchases/Create', [
            'suppliers' => Supplier::where('is_active', true)->get(),
            'medicines' => Medicine::select('id', 'name', 'sku', 'base_unit')->get(),
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'internal_receipt_number' => 'required|string|unique:purchases,internal_receipt_number',
            'invoice_number' => 'required|string',
            'supplier_id' => 'required|exists:suppliers,id',
            'purchase_date' => 'required|date',
            'notes' => 'nullable|string',
            'items' => 'required|array|min:1',
            'items.*.medicine_id' => 'required|exists:medicines,id',
            'items.*.batch_number' => 'required|string|max:100',
            'items.*.expired_date' => 'required|date|after_or_equal:' . now()->addMonths(6)->format('Y-m-d'),
            'items.*.quantity' => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'items.*.uom' => 'required|string|max:50',
            'items.*.purchase_price' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
        ]);

        DB::beginTransaction();
        try {
            $totalAmount = 0;
            foreach ($request->items as $item) {
                $totalAmount += ($item['quantity'] * $item['purchase_price']);
            }

            $purchase = Purchase::create([
                'internal_receipt_number' => $validated['internal_receipt_number'],
                'invoice_number' => strtoupper($validated['invoice_number']),
                'supplier_id' => $validated['supplier_id'],
                'user_id' => auth()->id(),
                'purchase_date' => $validated['purchase_date'],
                'total_amount' => $totalAmount,
                'status' => 'completed',
                'notes' => $validated['notes'],
            ]);

            foreach ($request->items as $item) {
                $detail = $purchase->details()->create([
                    'medicine_id' => $item['medicine_id'],
                    'batch_number' => strtoupper($item['batch_number']),
                    'expired_date' => $item['expired_date'],
                    'quantity' => $item['quantity'],
                    'purchase_price' => $item['purchase_price'],
                    'subtotal' => $item['quantity'] * $item['purchase_price'],
                ]);

                ProductBatch::create([
                    'medicine_id' => $item['medicine_id'],
                    'purchase_detail_id' => $detail->id,
                    'batch_number' => strtoupper($item['batch_number']),
                    'stock_quantity' => $item['quantity'],
                    'expired_date' => $item['expired_date'],
                    'purchase_price' => $item['purchase_price'],
                ]);

                InventoryService::syncMedicineStock($item['medicine_id']);
            }

            if (class_exists(AuditLog::class)) {
                AuditLog::record(
                    'pembelian', 
                    'CREATE', 
                    "Mencatat faktur pembelian PBF No: {$purchase->invoice_number} senilai Rp " . number_format($purchase->total_amount, 0, ',', '.')
                );
            }

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->route('purchases.index')
                ->with('success', "Faktur Pembelian {$purchase->invoice_number} berhasil disimpan.")
                ->with('print_url', route('purchases.print', $purchase->id));

        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => 'Gagal menyimpan transaksi: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
    }

    public function destroy(Purchase $purchase)
    {
        DB::beginTransaction();
        try {
            $purchase->load('details');
            $invoiceNumber = $purchase->invoice_number;
            
            foreach ($purchase->details as $detail) {
                // Hapus batch obat agar stok POS berkurang
                ProductBatch::where('purchase_detail_id', $detail->id)->delete();

                // Hitung ulang stok POS
                InventoryService::syncMedicineStock($detail->medicine_id);
            }

            // Hapus faktur (soft delete)
            $purchase->delete();

            if (class_exists(AuditLog::class)) {
                AuditLog::record(
                    'pembelian', 
                    'DELETE', 
                    "Menghapus faktur pembelian PBF No: {$invoiceNumber} senilai Rp " . number_format($purchase->total_amount, 0, ',', '.')
                );
            }

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->route('purchases.index')
                ->with('success', "Faktur pembelian {$invoiceNumber} berhasil dipindahkan ke tong sampah.");

        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => 'Gagal menghapus pembelian: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
    }

    public function bulkDestroy(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'ids' => 'required|array',
            'ids.*' => 'exists:purchases,id',
        ]);

        DB::beginTransaction();
        try {
            $purchases = Purchase::with('details')->whereIn('id', $validated['ids'])->get();

            foreach ($purchases as $purchase) {
                foreach ($purchase->details as $detail) {
                    ProductBatch::where('purchase_detail_id', $detail->id)->delete();
                    InventoryService::syncMedicineStock($detail->medicine_id);
                }
                $purchase->delete();
            }

            if (class_exists(AuditLog::class)) {
                AuditLog::record('pembelian', 'BULK_DELETE', 'Menghapus massal ' . count($purchases) . ' faktur pembelian.');
            }

            DB::commit();
            return redirect()->route('purchases.index')->with('success', count($purchases) . ' faktur pembelian berhasil dipindahkan ke tong sampah.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => 'Gagal menghapus massal: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
    }

    public function restore($id)
    {
        DB::beginTransaction();
        try {
            $purchase = Purchase::onlyTrashed()->with('details')->findOrFail($id);
            
            // 1. Pulihkan data faktur (purchases)
            $purchase->restore();

            // 2. Buat ulang ProductBatch untuk tiap detail obat jika belum ada
            foreach ($purchase->details as $detail) {
                $batch = ProductBatch::where('purchase_detail_id', $detail->id)->first();
                
                if (!$batch) {
                    ProductBatch::create([
                        'medicine_id'        => $detail->medicine_id,
                        'purchase_detail_id' => $detail->id,
                        'batch_number'       => $detail->batch_number,
                        'stock_quantity'     => $detail->quantity,
                        'expired_date'       => $detail->expired_date,
                        'purchase_price'     => $detail->purchase_price,
                    ]);
                }

                // 3. Kembalikan stok di POS
                InventoryService::syncMedicineStock($detail->medicine_id);
            }

            if (class_exists(AuditLog::class)) {
                AuditLog::record(
                    'pembelian', 
                    'RESTORE', 
                    "Memulihkan data faktur pembelian PBF No: {$purchase->invoice_number} dari tong sampah."
                );
            }

            DB::commit();
            return redirect()->route('purchases.trash')
                ->with('success', "Faktur pembelian {$purchase->invoice_number} berhasil dipulihkan dan stok POS telah kembali.");

        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => 'Gagal memulihkan data: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
    }

    public function forceDelete($id)
    {
        DB::beginTransaction();
        try {
            $purchase = Purchase::onlyTrashed()->with('details')->findOrFail($id);
            $invoiceNumber = $purchase->invoice_number;

            foreach ($purchase->details as $detail) {
                ProductBatch::where('purchase_detail_id', $detail->id)->delete();
                InventoryService::syncMedicineStock($detail->medicine_id);
            }

            $purchase->details()->delete();
            $purchase->forceDelete();

            if (class_exists(AuditLog::class)) {
                AuditLog::record(
                    'pembelian', 
                    'FORCE DELETE', 
                    "Menghapus PERMANEN faktur pembelian PBF No: {$invoiceNumber} dari database."
                );
            }

            DB::commit();
            return redirect()->route('purchases.trash')
                ->with('success', "Faktur pembelian {$invoiceNumber} berhasil dimusnahkan secara permanen.");

        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => 'Gagal menghapus permanen data: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
    }

    public function printPdf(Purchase $purchase)
    {
        $purchase->load(['supplier', 'user', 'details.medicine']);
        $pdf = Pdf::loadView('pdf.purchase-receipt', compact('purchase'));
        $pdf->setPaper('a4', 'portrait');

        return $pdf->stream('BPB-' . $purchase->internal_receipt_number . '.pdf', [
            'Content-Type' => 'application/pdf',
        ]);
    }

    public function trash()
    {
        $trashedPurchases = Purchase::onlyTrashed()
            ->with(['supplier', 'user'])
            ->latest()
            ->get();

        return Inertia::render('Purchases/Trash', [
            'purchases' => $trashedPurchases
        ]);
    }

    public function batchesIndex()
    {
        $batches = ProductBatch::with([
            'medicine' => function ($query) {
                $query->withTrashed();
            }, 
            'purchaseDetail.medicine' => function ($query) {
                $query->withTrashed();
            },
            'purchaseDetail.purchase.supplier'
        ])
        ->where('stock_quantity', '>', 0)
        ->orderBy('expired_date', 'asc')
        ->get()
        ->map(function ($batch) {
            if (!$batch->medicine || $batch->medicine->trashed()) {
                $fallbackMedicineName = $batch->purchaseDetail?->medicine?->name;
                if ($fallbackMedicineName) {
                    $batch->setRelation('medicine', new \App\Models\Medicine([
                        'name' => $fallbackMedicineName,
                        'sku'  => $batch->purchaseDetail?->medicine?->sku ?? 'SKU-LEGACY'
                    ]));
                }
            }
            return $batch;
        });

        return Inertia::render('Purchases/Batches', [
            'batches' => $batches
        ]);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Rack;
use App\Models\LocationType;
use App\Models\AuditLog;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;

class RackController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $racks = Rack::with('locationType')->latest()->get();
        return Inertia::render('Racks/Index', [
            'racks' => $racks
        ]);
    }

    public function create()
    {
        return Inertia::render('Racks/Create', [
            'locationTypes' => LocationType::all()
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'location_type_id' => 'required|exists:location_types,id',
            'code' => 'required|string|max:50|unique:racks',
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
        ]);
        
        $rack = Rack::create([
            'branch_id' => $request->user()->branch_id ?? 1,
            'location_type_id' => $validated['location_type_id'],
            'code' => strtoupper($validated['code']),
            'name' => $validated['name'],
            'description' => $validated['description'] ?? null,
            'is_active' => true,
        ]);

        AuditLog::record('rak', 'CREATE', "Menambahkan lokasi rak baru: {$rack->name} ({$rack->code})");

        return redirect()->route('racks.index');
    }

    public function edit(Rack $rack)
    {
        return Inertia::render('Racks/Edit', [
            'rackEdit' => $rack,
            'locationTypes' => LocationType::all()
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Rack $rack)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'location_type_id' => 'required|exists:location_types,id',
            'code' => 'required|string|max:50|unique:racks,code,' . $rack->id,
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
            'is_active' => 'boolean',
        ]);

        $rack->update([
            'branch_id' => $request->user()->branch_id ?? $rack->branch_id ?? 1,
            'location_type_id' => $validated['location_type_id'],
            'code' => strtoupper($validated['code']),
            'name' => $validated['name'],
            'description' => $validated['description'] ?? null,
            'is_active' => $request->is_active ?? false,
        ]);

        AuditLog::record('rak', 'UPDATE', "Memperbarui data lokasi rak: {$rack->name}");

        return redirect()->route('racks.index');
    }

    public function destroy(Rack $rack)
    {
        $rackName = $rack->name;
        $rack->delete();

        AuditLog::record('rak', 'DELETE', "Menghapus lokasi rak: {$rackName}");

        return redirect()->route('racks.index');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use App\Models\LocationType;
use App\Models\SupplierType;
use App\Models\MedicineCategory;
use App\Models\AuditLog;
use App\Models\Supplier;

class ReferenceController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $tab = $request->query('tab', 'tipe-lokasi');
        $data = [];

        if ($tab === 'tipe-lokasi') {
            $data['locationTypes'] = LocationType::latest()->get();
        } elseif ($tab === 'tipe-supplier') {
            $data['supplierTypes'] = SupplierType::latest()->get();
        } elseif ($tab === 'golongan-obat') {
            $data['medicineCategories'] = MedicineCategory::latest()->get();
        }

        return Inertia::render('References/Index', [
            'currentTab' => $tab,
            'data' => $data
        ]);
    }

    public function storeLocationType(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'prefix_code' => 'required|string|max:10',
            'intended_for' => 'nullable|string',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
        ]);
        $validated['prefix_code'] = strtoupper($validated['prefix_code']);
        
        $type = LocationType::create($validated);
        AuditLog::record('referensi', 'CREATE', "Menambahkan tipe lokasi: {$type->name}");
        return back()->with('success', 'Tipe Lokasi berhasil ditambahkan!');
    }

    public function updateLocationType(Request $request, LocationType $locationType)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'prefix_code' => 'required|string|max:10',
            'intended_for' => 'nullable|string',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
        ]);
        $validated['prefix_code'] = strtoupper($validated['prefix_code']);
        
        $locationType->update($validated);
        AuditLog::record('referensi', 'UPDATE', "Memperbarui tipe lokasi: {$locationType->name}");
        return back()->with('success', 'Tipe Lokasi berhasil diperbarui!');
    }

    public function destroyLocationType(LocationType $locationType)
    {
        $name = $locationType->name;
        $locationType->delete();
        AuditLog::record('referensi', 'DELETE', "Menghapus tipe lokasi: {$name}");
        return back()->with('success', "Tipe Lokasi {$name} berhasil dihapus.");
    }

    public function storeSupplierType(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'prefix_code' => 'required|string|max:10|unique:supplier_types,prefix_code',
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
        ]);
        $validated['prefix_code'] = strtoupper($validated['prefix_code']);
        
        $type = SupplierType::create($validated);
        AuditLog::record('referensi', 'CREATE', "Menambahkan tipe supplier: {$type->name}");
        return back()->with('success', 'Tipe Supplier berhasil ditambahkan!');
    }

    public function updateSupplierType(Request $request, SupplierType $supplierType)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'prefix_code' => 'required|string|max:10|unique:supplier_types,prefix_code,' . $supplierType->id,
            'description' => 'nullable|string',
        ]);
        $validated['prefix_code'] = strtoupper($validated['prefix_code']);
        
        $supplierType->update($validated);
        AuditLog::record('referensi', 'UPDATE', "Memperbarui tipe supplier: {$supplierType->name}");
        return back()->with('success', 'Tipe Supplier berhasil diperbarui!');
    }

    public function destroySupplierType(SupplierType $supplierType)
    {
        $usedCount = Supplier::where('supplier_type_id', $supplierType->id)->count();
        if ($usedCount > 0) {
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => "Tipe ini tidak bisa dihapus karena sedang digunakan oleh {$usedCount} pemasok."]);
        }

        $name = $supplierType->name;
        $supplierType->delete();
        AuditLog::record('referensi', 'DELETE', "Menghapus tipe supplier: {$name}");
        return back()->with('success', "Tipe Supplier {$name} berhasil dihapus.");
    }

    public function storeMedicineCategory(Request $request)

        ]);
        
        $medicineCategory->update($validated);
        AuditLog::record('referensi', 'UPDATE', "Memperbarui golongan obat: {$medicineCategory->name}");
        return back()->with('success', 'Golongan obat berhasil diperbarui!');
    }

    {
        $name = $medicineCategory->name;
        $medicineCategory->delete();
        
        AuditLog::record('referensi', 'DELETE', "Menghapus golongan obat: {$name}");
        return back()->with('success', "Golongan obat {$name} berhasil dihapus.");

=========================================================
FILE: app/Http/Controllers/ReportController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use Inertia\Inertia;
use App\Models\Sale;
use App\Models\SaleDetail;
use App\Models\Purchase;
use App\Models\PurchaseDetail;
use App\Models\Medicine;
use App\Exports\ReportExport;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Carbon\Carbon;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Facades\Excel;

class ReportController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $period = $request->get('period', 'monthly');
        $startDate = $request->get('start_date');
        $endDate = $request->get('end_date');
        $medicineId = $request->get('medicine_id');

        if (!$startDate || !$endDate) {
            if ($period === 'daily') {
                $startDate = Carbon::today()->format('Y-m-d');
                $endDate = Carbon::today()->format('Y-m-d');
            } elseif ($period === 'weekly') {
                $startDate = Carbon::now()->startOfWeek()->format('Y-m-d');
                $endDate = Carbon::now()->endOfWeek()->format('Y-m-d');
            } elseif ($period === 'yearly') {
                $startDate = Carbon::now()->startOfYear()->format('Y-m-d');
                $endDate = Carbon::now()->endOfYear()->format('Y-m-d');
            } else {
                $startDate = Carbon::now()->startOfMonth()->format('Y-m-d');
                $endDate = Carbon::now()->endOfMonth()->format('Y-m-d');
            }
        }

        $salesQuery = Sale::with(['user', 'details.medicine'])
            ->whereBetween('sale_date', [$startDate . ' 00:00:00', $endDate . ' 23:59:59'])
            ->orderBy('sale_date', 'desc');
            
        $salesList = $salesQuery->get();

        $totalSales = $salesList->sum('total_amount');
        $totalTransactions = $salesList->count();
        $totalHpp = 0;

        foreach ($salesList as $sale) {
            foreach ($sale->details as $detail) {
                // Mengambil purchase_price riil dari sale_details
                $hppUnit = $detail->purchase_price > 0 
                    ? $detail->purchase_price 
                    : ($detail->medicine->purchase_price ?? 0);
                    
                $totalHpp += ($detail->quantity * $hppUnit);
            }
        }
        
        $profit = $totalSales - $totalHpp;

        $lowStockCount = Medicine::where('stock', '<=', 10)->count();

        $stockMutations = $this->generateStockLedger($medicineId, $startDate, $endDate);

        $calMonth = Carbon::parse($startDate)->month;
        $calYear = Carbon::parse($startDate)->year;

        $calendarData = Sale::selectRaw('DATE(sale_date) as date, COUNT(id) as transactions, SUM(total_amount) as revenue')
            ->whereMonth('sale_date', $calMonth)
            ->whereYear('sale_date', $calYear)
            ->groupBy('date')
            ->get()
            ->keyBy('date');

        $monthSales = Sale::with('user')
            ->whereMonth('sale_date', $calMonth)
            ->whereYear('sale_date', $calYear)
            ->orderBy('sale_date', 'desc')
            ->get();

        $medicinesList = Medicine::where('is_active', true)->select('id', 'name', 'sku', 'stock', 'base_unit')->get();

        return Inertia::render('Reports/Index', [
            'filters' => [
                'start_date'  => $startDate,
                'end_date'    => $endDate,
                'period'      => $period,
                'medicine_id' => $medicineId
            ],
            'summary' => [
                'total_sales'        => (float) $totalSales,
                'total_hpp'          => (float) $totalHpp,
                'profit'             => (float) $profit,
                'total_transactions' => $totalTransactions,
                'low_stock'          => $lowStockCount,
            ],
            'salesList'      => $salesList,
            'calendarData'   => $calendarData,
            'monthSales'     => $monthSales,
            'stockMutations' => $stockMutations,
            'medicinesList'  => $medicinesList,
            'calendarMonth'  => Carbon::parse($startDate)->format('Y-m-d')
        ]);
    }

    private function generateStockLedger($medicineId = null, $startDate, $endDate)
    {
        $purchasesQuery = PurchaseDetail::with(['purchase.supplier', 'medicine'])
            ->whereHas('purchase', function($q) use ($startDate, $endDate) {
                $q->whereBetween('purchase_date', [$startDate, $endDate]);
            });

        if ($medicineId) {
            $purchasesQuery->where('medicine_id', $medicineId);
        }

        $purchases = $purchasesQuery->get()->map(function($item) {
            return [
                'date'          => $item->purchase->purchase_date,
                'medicine_id'   => $item->medicine_id,
                'medicine_name' => $item->medicine->name ?? 'Obat',
                'sku'           => $item->medicine->sku ?? '-',
                'type'          => 'IN',
                'reference'     => $item->purchase->invoice_number,
                'description'   => 'Pembelian dari Supplier: ' . ($item->purchase->supplier->name ?? 'PBF'),
                'quantity'      => (int) $item->quantity,
                'uom'           => $item->uom ?? 'Tablet',
            ];
        });

        $salesQuery = SaleDetail::with(['sale.user', 'medicine'])
            ->whereHas('sale', function($q) use ($startDate, $endDate) {
                $q->whereBetween('sale_date', [$startDate . ' 00:00:00', $endDate . ' 23:59:59']);
            });

        if ($medicineId) {
            $salesQuery->where('medicine_id', $medicineId);
        }

        $sales = $salesQuery->get()->map(function($item) {
            return [
                'date'          => $item->sale->sale_date,
                'medicine_id'   => $item->medicine_id,
                'medicine_name' => $item->medicine->name ?? 'Obat',
                'sku'           => $item->medicine->sku ?? '-',
                'type'          => 'OUT',
                'reference'     => $item->sale->invoice_number,
                'description'   => 'Penjualan Kasir (Operator: ' . ($item->sale->user->name ?? 'Kasir') . ')',
                'quantity'      => (int) $item->quantity,
                'uom'           => $item->medicine->base_unit ?? 'Tablet',
            ];
        });

        return $purchases->concat($sales)->sortByDesc('date')->values()->all();
    }

    public function export(Request $request)
    {
        $type = $request->get('type', 'sales');
        $format = $request->get('format', 'pdf');
        $startDate = $request->get('start_date', Carbon::now()->startOfMonth()->format('Y-m-d'));
        $endDate = $request->get('end_date', Carbon::now()->endOfMonth()->format('Y-m-d'));
        $medicineId = $request->get('medicine_id');

        $data = [];
        $title = '';

        if ($type === 'sales') {
            $title = 'Laporan Penjualan (Omzet & Struk)';
            $data = Sale::with(['user', 'details.medicine'])
                ->whereBetween('sale_date', [$startDate . ' 00:00:00', $endDate . ' 23:59:59'])
                ->orderBy('sale_date', 'desc')->get();
        } elseif ($type === 'purchase') {
            $title = 'Laporan Pembelian & Supplier';
            $data = Purchase::with(['user', 'supplier'])
                ->whereBetween('purchase_date', [$startDate, $endDate])
                ->orderBy('purchase_date', 'desc')->get();
        } elseif ($type === 'profit') {
            $title = 'Laporan Laba Rugi & HPP';
            $data = Sale::with(['details.medicine'])
                ->whereBetween('sale_date', [$startDate . ' 00:00:00', $endDate . ' 23:59:59'])
                ->orderBy('sale_date', 'desc')->get();
        } elseif ($type === 'stock') {
            $title = 'Laporan Stok Kritis';
            $data = Medicine::where('stock', '<=', 10)->get();
        } elseif ($type === 'mutations') {
            $title = 'Laporan Mutasi & Kartu Stok Obat';
            $data = $this->generateStockLedger($medicineId, $startDate, $endDate);
        }

        if ($format === 'excel') {
            $fileName = 'Laporan_' . strtoupper($type) . '_' . date('Ymd') . '.xlsx';
            return Excel::download(new ReportExport($type, $data, $startDate, $endDate, $title), $fileName);
        }

        return view('reports.print', compact('type', 'data', 'startDate', 'endDate', 'title'));
    }

    public function profitLoss(Request $request)
    {
        $startDate = $request->input('start_date', date('Y-m-01'));
        $endDate = $request->input('end_date', date('Y-m-t'));

        $sales = Sale::with('details')
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$startDate . ' 00:00:00', $endDate . ' 23:59:59'])
            ->get();

        $totalRevenue = 0;
        $totalHPP = 0;

        foreach ($sales as $sale) {
            $totalRevenue += $sale->total_amount;
            foreach ($sale->details as $detail) {
                $hppUnit = $detail->purchase_price > 0 
                    ? $detail->purchase_price 
                    : ($detail->medicine->purchase_price ?? 0);

                $totalHPP += ($hppUnit * $detail->quantity);
            }
        }

        $grossProfit = $totalRevenue - $totalHPP;

        return Inertia::render('Reports/ProfitLoss', [
            'startDate'    => $startDate,
            'endDate'      => $endDate,
            'totalRevenue' => $totalRevenue,
            'totalHPP'     => $totalHPP,
            'grossProfit'  => $grossProfit,
            'profitMargin' => $totalRevenue > 0 ? round(($grossProfit / $totalRevenue) * 100, 2) : 0,
        ]);
    }
}

=========================================================
FILE: app/Http/Controllers/SalesHistoryController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Sale;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Inertia\Inertia;

class SalesHistoryController extends Controller
{
    private function checkOwnerAccess()
    {
        $user = auth()->user();
        
        $role = strtolower($user->role ?? '');

        $roleName = '';
        try {
            if (method_exists($user, 'roles') && $user->roles) {
                $roleName = strtolower($user->roles->first()->name ?? '');
            }
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
        }

        $isOwnerOrSuper = in_array($role, ['owner', 'pemilik', 'superadmin', 'super-admin']) ||
                            in_array($roleName, ['owner', 'pemilik', 'superadmin', 'super-admin']) ||
                            (method_exists($user, 'hasRole') && ($user->hasRole('superadmin') || $user->hasRole('owner') || $user->hasRole('Superadmin'))) ||
                            ($user->role_id == 1);

        if (!$isOwnerOrSuper) {
            abort(403, 'Hanya Owner yang memiliki hak akses untuk menghapus atau memulihkan data transaksi.');
        }
    }

    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $sales = Sale::with(['user', 'details.medicine'])
            ->orderBy('sale_date', 'desc')
            ->paginate(15)
            ->withQueryString();

        return Inertia::render('Sales/Index', [
            'sales'   => $sales,
            'isTrash' => false,
        ]);
    }

    public function trash(Request $request)
    {
        $sales = Sale::onlyTrashed()
            ->with(['user', 'details.medicine'])
            ->orderBy('deleted_at', 'desc')
            ->paginate(15)
            ->withQueryString();

        return Inertia::render('Sales/Index', [
            'sales'   => $sales,
            'isTrash' => true,
        ]);
    }

    public function destroy($id)
    {
        $this->checkOwnerAccess();

        DB::transaction(function () use ($id) {
            $sale = Sale::findOrFail($id);
            $sale->delete(); 
        });

        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Transaksi penjualan berhasil dipindahkan ke sampah.');
    }

    public function bulkDestroy(Request $request)
    {
        $this->checkOwnerAccess();

        $request->validate([
            'ids'   => 'required|array',
            'ids.*' => 'exists:sales,id',
        ]);

        DB::transaction(function () use ($request) {
            $sales = Sale::whereIn('id', $request->ids)->get();

            foreach ($sales as $sale) {
                $sale->delete(); 
            }
        });

        return redirect()->back()->with('success', count($request->ids) . ' transaksi berhasil dipindahkan ke sampah.');
    }

    public function restore($id)
    {
        $this->checkOwnerAccess();

        DB::transaction(function () use ($id) {
            $sale = Sale::onlyTrashed()->findOrFail($id);
            $sale->restore();
        });

        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Transaksi berhasil dipulihkan.');
    }

    public function bulkRestore(Request $request)
    {
        $this->checkOwnerAccess();

        $request->validate([
            'ids'   => 'required|array',
            'ids.*' => 'exists:sales,id',
        ]);

        DB::transaction(function () use ($request) {
            Sale::onlyTrashed()->whereIn('id', $request->ids)->restore();
        });

        return redirect()->back()->with('success', count($request->ids) . ' transaksi berhasil dipulihkan.');
    }

    public function forceDelete($id)
    {
        $this->checkOwnerAccess();

        DB::transaction(function () use ($id) {
            $sale = Sale::onlyTrashed()->findOrFail($id);
            $sale->forceDelete();
        });

        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Transaksi berhasil dihapus secara permanen.');
    }
}

FILE: app/Http/Controllers/StockAdjustmentController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Medicine;
use App\Models\ProductBatch;
use App\Models\StockAdjustment;
use App\Services\InventoryService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;

class StockAdjustmentController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $adjustments = StockAdjustment::with(['medicine', 'productBatch', 'user'])
            ->latest()
            ->paginate(15);

        return Inertia::render('StockOpname/Index', [
            'adjustments' => $adjustments
        ]);
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $medicines = Medicine::with(['batches' => function ($q) {
            $q->orderBy('expiry_date', 'asc');
        }])->get();

        return Inertia::render('StockOpname/Create', [
            'medicines' => $medicines
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'product_batch_id' => 'required|exists:product_batches,id',
            'actual_stock' => 'required|integer|min:0',
            'type' => 'required|in:DAMAGE,EXPIRED,LOST,CORRECTION',
            'notes' => 'nullable|string|max:500',
        ]);

        try {
            InventoryService::adjustBatchStock(
                $request->product_batch_id,
                $request->actual_stock,
                $request->type,
                $request->notes
            );

            return redirect()->route('stock-adjustments.index')->with('success', 'Stock opname berhasil dicatat dan stok disesuaikan!');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            return back()->withErrors(['message' => 'Gagal memperbarui stok: ' . $e->getMessage()]);
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/Http/Controllers/SupplierController.php
=========================================================
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Supplier;
use App\Models\AuditLog;
use App\Models\SupplierType;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Inertia\Inertia;

class SupplierController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $suppliers = Supplier::with('supplierType')->latest()->get();
        return Inertia::render('Suppliers/Index', [
            'suppliers' => $suppliers
        ]);
    }

    public function create()
    {
        return Inertia::render('Suppliers/Create', [
            'supplierTypes' => SupplierType::all()
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'supplier_type_id' => 'required|exists:supplier_types,id', // Validasi Field Baru
            'code' => 'required|unique:suppliers,code',
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'contact_person' => 'nullable|string|max:255',
            'phone' => 'nullable|string|max:50',
            'email' => 'nullable|email|max:255',
            'address' => 'nullable|string',
        ]);

        $supplier = Supplier::create($validated);

        AuditLog::record('supplier', 'CREATE', "Menambahkan pemasok baru: {$supplier->name}");

        return redirect()->route('suppliers.index')->with('success', 'Data Pemasok berhasil ditambahkan!');
    }

    public function edit(Supplier $supplier)
    {
        return Inertia::render('Suppliers/Edit', [
            'supplierEdit' => $supplier,
            'supplierTypes' => SupplierType::all()
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Supplier $supplier)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'supplier_type_id' => 'required|exists:supplier_types,id', // Validasi Field Baru
            'code' => 'required|string|max:50|unique:suppliers,code,' . $supplier->id,
            'name' => 'required|string|max:255',
            'contact_person' => 'nullable|string|max:255',
            'phone' => 'nullable|string|max:50',
            'email' => 'nullable|email|max:255',
            'address' => 'nullable|string',
            'is_active' => 'boolean',
        ]);

        $supplier->update($validated);

        AuditLog::record('supplier', 'UPDATE', "Memperbarui data pemasok: {$supplier->name}");

        return redirect()->route('suppliers.index')->with('success', 'Data Pemasok berhasil diperbarui!');
    }

    public function destroy(Supplier $supplier)
    {
        $supplierName = $supplier->name;
        $supplier->delete();

        AuditLog::record('supplier', 'DELETE', "Menghapus data pemasok: {$supplierName}");
        return redirect()->route('suppliers.index')->with('success', "Data Pemasok {$supplierName} berhasil dihapus permanen.");
    }
}
