import React from 'react';

export default function ReceiptModal({ sale, onClose, onPrint, formatRupiah }) {
    if (!sale) return null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-900/70 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in">
            <div className="bg-white text-black p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-w-sm w-full mx-4 font-mono text-xs animate-in zoom-in-95">
                
                <div id="printable-receipt" className="bg-white text-black p-2">
                    <div className="text-center pb-3 border-b border-dashed border-gray-400">
                        <div className="font-bold text-sm">APOTEK MEDISYNC</div>
                        <div>Nota: {sale.invoice_number}</div>
                        <div>{sale.date}</div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="py-3 space-y-2 border-b border-dashed border-gray-400 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
                        {sale.items.map((i, idx) => (
                            <div key={idx} className="flex flex-col">
                                <div className="flex justify-between">
                                    <span className="font-bold">{i.name} ({i.quantity}x)</span>
                                    <span className="font-bold">{formatRupiah(i.price * i.quantity)}</span>
                                </div>
                                {i.signa && <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 mt-1">* {i.signa}</span>}
                            </div>
                        ))}
                    </div>

                    <div className="py-3 space-y-1 border-b border-dashed border-gray-400">
                        <div className="flex justify-between font-bold">
                            <span>Total Tagihan:</span>
                            <span>{formatRupiah(sale.subtotal)}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex justify-between">
                            <span>Metode Bayar:</span>
                            <span className="font-bold uppercase">{sale.method}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex justify-between">
                            <span>Total Dibayar:</span>
                            <span className="font-bold">{formatRupiah(sale.paid)}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex justify-between">
                            <span>Kembalian:</span>
                            <span className="font-bold">{formatRupiah(sale.change)}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="text-center py-3 text-[10px] font-bold">
                        TERIMA KASIH - LEKAS SEMBUH KEMBALI
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* TOMBOL AKSI */}
                <div className="flex gap-2 pt-4 border-t border-gray-200 mt-2">
                    <button 
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="flex-1 py-2 bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-800 rounded-xl font-sans font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"></path></svg>
                        Tutup
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={onPrint}
                        className="flex-1 py-2 bg-[#48CFA4] hover:bg-[#3AB890] text-white rounded-xl font-sans font-bold shadow-md flex items-center justify-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M17 17h2a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h6a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2zm8-12V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4h10z"></path></svg>
                        Cetak Struk
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

import React from 'react';

export default function SimulationModal({ 
    isOpen, 
    onClose, 
    simulationType, 
    payments, 
    formatRupiah, 
    executeFinalCheckout, 
    isProcessing 
}) {
    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in">
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-w-sm w-full mx-4 text-center border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 animate-in zoom-in-95">
                <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                    <svg className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 2A8.001 8.001 0 004.582 9m0 0H9m11 11v-5h-.581m0 0a8.003 8.003 0 01-15.357-2m15.357 2H15"></path>
                    </svg>
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 mb-1">Menunggu Pembayaran {simulationType}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-4">Silakan verifikasi pembayaran non-tunai pelanggan sebesar <strong className="text-emerald-500">{formatRupiah(payments.qris + payments.transfer)}</strong></p>
                
                <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] p-4 rounded-2xl mb-6 border border-dashed border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest font-bold mb-1">Gateway Pembayaran Aktif</div>
                    <div className="font-mono font-bold text-sm text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400">STATUS: MENUNGGU KONFIRMASI EDC</div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button 
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        Batalkan
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={executeFinalCheckout}
                        disabled={isProcessing}
                        className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#48CFA4] hover:bg-[#3AB890] text-white rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-lg shadow-[#48CFA4]/30 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg>
                        {isProcessing ? 'Memproses...' : 'Konfirmasi Sukses'}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

=========================================================
FILE: resources/js/Pages/Pos/Index.jsx
=========================================================
import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';

// Impor Komponen Anak
import ProductList from './Components/ProductList';
import CartSidebar from './Components/CartSidebar';
import PrescriptionModal from './Components/PrescriptionModal';
import SimulationModal from './Components/SimulationModal';
import ReceiptModal from './Components/ReceiptModal';

export default function Index({ medicines }) {
    // === STATE UTAMA ===
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);
    const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
    const [payments, setPayments] = useState({ cash: 0, qris: 0, transfer: 0 });
    const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);

    // === STATE MODAL & RESEP ===
    const [showSimulationModal, setShowSimulationModal] = useState(false);
    const [simulationType, setSimulationType] = useState('');
    const [completedSale, setCompletedSale] = useState(null);
    const [isPrescriptionModalOpen, setIsPrescriptionModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [prescriptionNumber, setPrescriptionNumber] = useState('');
    const [prescriptionError, setPrescriptionError] = useState('');
    const [isFetchingPrescription, setIsFetchingPrescription] = useState(false);

    const [activePrescriptionId, setActivePrescriptionId] = useState(null);

    // === PERHITUNGAN & FILTER ===
    const formatRupiah = (number) => new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', { style: 'currency', currency: 'IDR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(number || 0);

    const filteredMedicines = useMemo(() => {
        return medicines.filter(medicine => 
            medicine.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
            medicine.sku.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
            (medicine.barcode && medicine.barcode.includes(searchQuery))
        );
    }, [medicines, searchQuery]);

    const subtotal = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * item.quantity), 0);
    const totalPaid = (payments.cash || 0) + (payments.qris || 0) + (payments.transfer || 0);
    const changeAmount = totalPaid - subtotal;

    const addToCart = (medicine) => {
        const existingItem = cart.find(item => item.medicine_id === medicine.id);
        if (existingItem) {
            if (existingItem.quantity < medicine.stock) {
                setCart(cart.map(item => item.medicine_id === medicine.id ? { ...item, quantity: item.quantity + 1 } : item));
            } else {
                alert(`Stok ${medicine.name} tidak mencukupi!`);
            }
        } else {
            if (medicine.stock > 0) {
                setCart([...cart, { 
                    medicine_id: medicine.id, name: medicine.name, sku: medicine.sku,
                    base_unit: medicine.base_unit, quantity: 1, 
                    price: Number(medicine.selling_price), max_stock: medicine.stock
                }]);
            } else {
                alert(`Stok ${medicine.name} kosong!`);
            }
        }
    };

    const updateQuantity = (id, newQuantity) => {
        const item = cart.find(i => i.medicine_id === id);
        if (!item || newQuantity < 1 || newQuantity > item.max_stock) return;
        setCart(cart.map(i => i.medicine_id === id ? { ...i, quantity: newQuantity } : i));
    };

    const removeFromCart = (id) => setCart(cart.filter(item => item.medicine_id !== id));

    const handlePullPrescription = async () => {
        if (!prescriptionNumber) return;
        setIsFetchingPrescription(true);
        setPrescriptionError('');

        try {
            const response = await axios.get(`/api/prescriptions/${prescriptionNumber}`);
            const data = response.data;

            setActivePrescriptionId(data.id);

            const newCartItems = data.items.map(item => ({
                medicine_id: item.medicine_id,
                name: item.medicine.name,
                sku: item.medicine.sku,
                base_unit: item.medicine.base_unit,
                quantity: item.quantity,
                price: Number(item.medicine.selling_price),
                max_stock: item.medicine.stock || 999,
                signa: item.signa
            }));

            setCart([...cart, ...newCartItems]);
            setIsPrescriptionModalOpen(false);
            setPrescriptionNumber('');
        } catch (error) {
            setPrescriptionError(error.response?.data?.message || 'Gagal menarik resep. Pastikan nomor benar.');
        } finally {
            setIsFetchingPrescription(false);
        }
    };

    const handleProceedCheckout = () => {
        if (cart.length === 0 || changeAmount < 0) return;
        if ((payments.qris > 0 || payments.transfer > 0)) {
            setSimulationType(payments.qris > 0 && payments.transfer > 0 ? 'QRIS & Transfer' : (payments.qris > 0 ? 'QRIS' : 'Transfer'));
            setShowSimulationModal(true);
        } else {
            executeFinalCheckout();
        }
    };

    const executeFinalCheckout = () => {
        setIsProcessing(true);
        const usedMethods = [];
        if (payments.cash > 0) usedMethods.push('Cash');
        if (payments.qris > 0) usedMethods.push('QRIS');
        if (payments.transfer > 0) usedMethods.push('Transfer');

        const currentCartSnapshot = [...cart];
        const currentTotal = subtotal;
        const currentPaid = totalPaid;
        const currentChange = changeAmount;
        const currentMethod = usedMethods.join(' + ') || 'Cash';

        router.post(route('pos.store'), {
            cart: cart,
            paid_amount: totalPaid,
            change_amount: changeAmount >= 0 ? changeAmount : 0,
            payment_method: currentMethod,
            prescription_id: activePrescriptionId,
        }, {
            onSuccess: (page) => {
                setCompletedSale({
                    invoice_number: page.props.flash?.invoice_number || 'INV-SUCCESS',
                    date: new Date().toLocaleString('id-ID'),
                    items: currentCartSnapshot,
                    subtotal: currentTotal,
                    paid: currentPaid,
                    change: currentChange,
                    method: currentMethod
                });

                setCart([]);
                setActivePrescriptionId(null);
                setPayments({ cash: 0, qris: 0, transfer: 0 });
                setSearchQuery('');
                setIsProcessing(false);
                setShowSimulationModal(false);
            },
            onError: () => {
                setIsProcessing(false);
                setShowSimulationModal(false);
            }
        });
    };

    const handlePrintReceipt = () => {
        const printContents = document.getElementById('printable-receipt').innerHTML;
        const printIframe = document.createElement('iframe');
        printIframe.style.position = 'fixed';
        printIframe.style.right = '0';
        printIframe.style.bottom = '0';
        printIframe.style.width = '0px';
        printIframe.style.height = '0px';
        printIframe.style.border = 'none';

        document.body.appendChild(printIframe);

        const iframeDoc = printIframe.contentWindow || printIframe.contentDocument.document || printIframe.contentDocument;
        iframeDoc.document.open();
        iframeDoc.document.write(`
            <html>
                <head>
                    <title>Cetak Struk</title>
                    <style>
                        @page { size: 58mm auto; margin: 0; }
                        body { font-family: 'Courier New', Courier, monospace; font-size: 11px; width: 58mm; margin: 0; padding: 5px; color: #000; }
                        .text-center { text-align: center; }
                        .flex { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; }
                        .font-bold { font-weight: bold; }
                        .border-b { border-bottom: 1px dashed #000; }
                        .pb-3 { padding-bottom: 8px; }
                        .py-3 { padding-top: 8px; padding-bottom: 8px; }
                        .mt-1 { margin-top: 4px; }
                        .text-\\[10px\\] { font-size: 10px; }
                    </style>
                </head>
                <body>${printContents}</body>
            </html>
        `);
        iframeDoc.document.close();
        
        setTimeout(() => {
            printIframe.contentWindow.focus();
            printIframe.contentWindow.print();

            printIframe.contentWindow.onafterprint = () => {
                if (document.body.contains(printIframe)) {
                    document.body.removeChild(printIframe);
                }
            };
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (document.body.contains(printIframe)) {
                    document.body.removeChild(printIframe);
                }
            }, 2000);
        }, 300);
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Point of Sales (Kasir)">
            <Head title="Kasir - Penjualan" />

            <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-6 h-[calc(100vh-140px)]">
                {/* KOMPONEN KIRI */}
                <ProductList 
                    searchQuery={searchQuery}
                    setSearchQuery={setSearchQuery}
                    filteredMedicines={filteredMedicines}
                    addToCart={addToCart}
                    setIsPrescriptionModalOpen={setIsPrescriptionModalOpen}
                />

                {/* KOMPONEN KANAN */}
                <CartSidebar 
                    cart={cart}
                    removeFromCart={removeFromCart}
                    updateQuantity={updateQuantity}
                    subtotal={subtotal}
                    payments={payments}
                    setPayments={setPayments}
                    totalPaid={totalPaid}
                    changeAmount={changeAmount}
                    handleProceedCheckout={handleProceedCheckout}
                    isProcessing={isProcessing}
                    formatRupiah={formatRupiah}
                />
            </div>

            {/* KOMPONEN MODAL */}
            <PrescriptionModal 
                isOpen={isPrescriptionModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsPrescriptionModalOpen(false)}
                prescriptionNumber={prescriptionNumber}
                setPrescriptionNumber={setPrescriptionNumber}
                prescriptionError={prescriptionError}
                setPrescriptionError={setPrescriptionError}
                isFetchingPrescription={isFetchingPrescription}
                handlePullPrescription={handlePullPrescription}
            />

            <SimulationModal 
                isOpen={showSimulationModal}
                onClose={() => setShowSimulationModal(false)}
                simulationType={simulationType}
                payments={payments}
                formatRupiah={formatRupiah}
                executeFinalCheckout={executeFinalCheckout}
                isProcessing={isProcessing}
            />

            <ReceiptModal 
                sale={completedSale}
                onClose={() => setCompletedSale(null)}
                onPrint={handlePrintReceipt}
                formatRupiah={formatRupiah}
            />
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

=========================================================
FILE: resources/js/Pages/Prescription/Create.jsx
=========================================================
import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import PrescriptionFormHeader from '@/Components/Prescription/PrescriptionFormHeader';
import PrescriptionItemManager from '@/Components/Prescription/PrescriptionItemManager';
import CustomAlertModal from '@/Components/Prescription/CustomAlertModal';

export default function Create({ medicines }) {
    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors } = useForm({
        patient_name: '',
        doctor_name: '',
        prescription_date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
        notes: '',
        attachment: null,
        items: []
    });

    const [isScanning, setIsScanning] = useState(false);
    const [customAlert, setCustomAlert] = useState({ show: false, message: '', type: 'error' });
    const [unmatchedMedicines, setUnmatchedMedicines] = useState([]);
    const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState(null);

    const [selectedMedicine, setSelectedMedicine] = useState('');
    const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(1);
    const [signaFreq, setSignaFreq] = useState('3 x 1');
    const [signaForm, setSignaForm] = useState('Tablet');
    const [signaTime, setSignaTime] = useState('Sesudah Makan');

    useEffect(() => {
        return () => {
            if (previewUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(previewUrl);
        };
    }, [previewUrl]);

    const formatDoctorName = (name) => {
        let clean = name.replace(/^(dr\.|dr|Dr\.|Dr|drs\.|Drs\.)\s*/i, '').trim();
        return clean ? `dr. ${clean}` : '';
    };

    const handleDoctorChange = (e) => {
        setData('doctor_name', formatDoctorName(e.target.value));
    };

    // MURNI PILIH FILE & PREVIEW (TIDAK MEMANGGIL AI)
    const handleImageUpload = (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (!file) return;
        
        setData('attachment', file);
        setPreviewUrl(URL.createObjectURL(file));
    };

    // PROSES AI BERJALAN HANYA KETIKA TOMBOL "EKSTRAK AI" DIKLIK MANUSIA
    const handleScanAI = async (e) => {
        if (e) e.preventDefault(); 
        
        const file = data.attachment;
        if (!file) {
            return setCustomAlert({ 
                show: true, 
                message: 'Harap pilih file gambar resep terlebih dahulu!', 
                type: 'error' 
            });
        }

        const cacheKey = `medisync_ocr_cache_${file.name}_${file.size}`;
        const cachedData = localStorage.getItem(cacheKey);

        if (cachedData) {
            try {
                const parsedData = JSON.parse(cachedData);
                processAiResult(parsedData);
                setCustomAlert({ 
                    show: true, 
                    message: 'Data resep dimuat dari memori lokal (Cache aktif)!', 
                    type: 'success' 
                });
                return;
            } catch (err) {
                localStorage.removeItem(cacheKey);
            }
        }

        setIsScanning(true);

        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        reader.onload = async () => {
            try {
                const base64String = reader.result.split(',')[1];
                const mimeType = file.type;

                const apiKey = import.meta.env.VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY;
                if (!apiKey) {
                    throw new Error("Kunci VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY belum dipasang di file .env!");
                }

                const prompt = `Anda adalah sistem OCR apotek. Analisis gambar resep dokter ini. Daftar obat yang ada di database apotek kami: ${JSON.stringify(medicines.map(m => m.name))}.
                Tugas:
                1. Ekstrak patient_name, doctor_name (format 'dr. Nama'), prescription_date (YYYY-MM-DD).
                2. Ekstrak items [{name, quantity, signa}].
                KEMBALIKAN HANYA FORMAT JSON MENTAH TANPA MARKDOWN.`;

                let parsedData = null;
                let usedModelName = '';
                let lastErrorMessage = '';

                let activeModels = [];
                try {
                    const listRes = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=${apiKey}`);
                    if (listRes.ok) {
                        const listData = await listRes.json();
                        activeModels = (listData.models || [])
                            .filter(m => 
                                m.supportedGenerationMethods?.includes('generateContent') &&
                                (m.name.includes('flash') || m.name.includes('pro')) &&
                                !m.name.includes('tts') && 
                                !m.name.includes('audio') && 
                                !m.name.includes('gemma')
                            )
                            .map(m => m.name.replace('models/', ''));
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.warn("Gagal mengambil daftar model dinamis, memakai fallback bawaan.", e);
                }

                if (activeModels.length === 0) {
                    activeModels = ['gemini-1.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-flash-lite-latest', 'gemini-1.5-pro'];
                }

                for (const model of activeModels) {
                    const apiVersions = ['v1beta', 'v1'];
                    let success = false;

                    for (const apiVer of apiVersions) {
                        try {
                            const response = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/${apiVer}/models/${model}:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`, {
                                method: 'POST',
                                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                                body: JSON.stringify({
                                    contents: [{
                                        parts: [
                                            { text: prompt },
                                            { inlineData: { mimeType, data: base64String } }
                                        ]
                                    }]
                                })
                            });

                            const aiData = await response.json();

                            if (response.ok && aiData.candidates && aiData.candidates[0]?.content) {
                                const textRaw = aiData.candidates[0].content.parts[0].text;
                                const textClean = textRaw.replace(/```json/g, '').replace(/```/g, '').trim();
                                parsedData = JSON.parse(textClean);
                                usedModelName = `${model} (${apiVer})`;
                                success = true;
                                break;
                            } else {
                                lastErrorMessage = aiData.error?.message || `HTTP ${response.status}`;
                            }
                        } catch (err) {
                            lastErrorMessage = err.message;
                        }
                    }

                    if (success) break;
                }

                if (!parsedData) {
                    throw new Error(`Gagal terhubung ke AI. Rincian: ${lastErrorMessage}`);
                }

                localStorage.setItem(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(parsedData));

                processAiResult(parsedData);
                setCustomAlert({ 
                    show: true, 
                    message: `Berhasil memindai resep menggunakan ${usedModelName}!`, 
                    type: 'success' 
                });

            } catch (error) {
                setCustomAlert({ 
                    show: true, 
                    message: `Gagal memindai resep: ${error.message}. Silakan isi detail resep secara manual.`, 
                    type: 'error' 
                });
            } finally {
                setIsScanning(false);
            }
        };
    };

    const processAiResult = (parsedData) => {
        let autoItems = [];
        let notFoundItems = [];

        if (parsedData.items && parsedData.items.length > 0) {
            parsedData.items.forEach(aiItem => {
                const readName = (aiItem.name || aiItem.medicine_name || '').toLowerCase();
                const matchedMed = medicines.find(m => 
                    m.name.toLowerCase().includes(readName) || readName.includes(m.name.toLowerCase())
                );

                if (matchedMed) {
                    autoItems.push({
                        medicine_id: matchedMed.id,
                        name: matchedMed.name,
                        sku: matchedMed.sku,
                        stock: matchedMed.stock,
                        quantity: aiItem.quantity || 1,
                        signa: aiItem.signa || '3 x 1 Tablet Sesudah Makan'
                    });
                } else if (readName) {
                    notFoundItems.push(`${aiItem.name || aiItem.medicine_name} (Qty: ${aiItem.quantity || 1}, Signa: ${aiItem.signa || '-'})`);
                }
            });
        }

        setUnmatchedMedicines(notFoundItems);

        setData(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            patient_name: parsedData.patient_name || prev.patient_name,
            doctor_name: parsedData.doctor_name ? formatDoctorName(parsedData.doctor_name) : prev.doctor_name,
            prescription_date: parsedData.prescription_date || prev.prescription_date,
            items: autoItems.length > 0 ? autoItems : prev.items
        }));
    };

    const handleAddItem = () => {
        if (!selectedMedicine || !quantity) {
            return setCustomAlert({ show: true, message: 'Pilih obat dan isi jumlahnya terlebih dahulu!', type: 'error' });
        }
        const medicine = medicines.find(m => m.id === parseInt(selectedMedicine));
        if (!medicine) return;
        
        const finalSigna = `${signaFreq} ${signaForm} ${signaTime}`;
        setData('items', [...data.items, { 
            medicine_id: medicine.id, 
            name: medicine.name, 
            sku: medicine.sku, 
            stock: medicine.stock,
            quantity: parseInt(quantity), 
            signa: finalSigna 
        }]);
        setSelectedMedicine(''); setQuantity(1);
    };

    const handleRemoveItem = (index) => {
        const newItems = [...data.items];
        newItems.splice(index, 1);
        setData('items', newItems);
    };

    const handleSubmit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (data.items.length === 0) {
            return setCustomAlert({ show: true, message: 'Minimal harus ada 1 obat di dalam rincian resep!', type: 'error' });
        }
        post(route('prescriptions.store'), { forceFormData: true });
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Catat Resep Masuk">
            <Head title="Input Resep Baru" />

            <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6 relative">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Formulir Resep Dokter</h2>
                    <Link href={route('prescriptions.index')} title="Batal & Kembali" className="p-2.5 bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-xl transition-colors">
                        <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M10 19l-7-7m0 0l7-7m-7 7h18"></path></svg>
                    </Link>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
                    <PrescriptionFormHeader 
                        data={data} 
                        setData={setData} 
                        handleImageUpload={handleImageUpload} 
                        handleDoctorChange={handleDoctorChange} 
                        isScanning={isScanning} 
                        previewUrl={previewUrl}    
                        handleScanAI={handleScanAI}
                    />

                    <PrescriptionItemManager 
                        medicines={medicines}
                        data={data}
                        setData={setData}
                        unmatchedMedicines={unmatchedMedicines}
                        selectedMedicine={selectedMedicine}
                        setSelectedMedicine={setSelectedMedicine}
                        quantity={quantity}
                        setQuantity={setQuantity}
                        signaFreq={signaFreq}
                        setSignaFreq={setSignaFreq}
                        signaForm={signaForm}
                        setSignaForm={setSignaForm}
                        signaTime={signaTime}
                        setSignaTime={setSignaTime}
                        handleAddItem={handleAddItem}
                        handleRemoveItem={handleRemoveItem}
                    />

                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 shadow-sm space-y-4">
                        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#48CFA4] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 pb-2">Catatan Tambahan</h3>
                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">Keterangan / Catatan Resep</label>
                            <textarea 
                                className="w-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200" 
                                rows="3" 
                                value={data.notes} 
                                onChange={e => setData('notes', e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="Tulis instruksi tambahan resep di sini..."
                            ></textarea>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-end">
                        <button type="submit" disabled={processing} className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-[#48CFA4] hover:bg-[#3AB890] text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all duration-200 shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer">
                            {processing ? (
                                <svg className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 2A8.001 8.001 0 004.582 9m0 0H9m11 11v-5h-.581m0 0a8.003 8.003 0 01-15.357-2m15.357 2H15"></path></svg>
                            ) : (
                                <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M8 7H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v9a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V9a2 2 0 00-2-2h-3m-1 4l-3 3m0 0l-3-3m3 3V4"></path></svg>
                            )}
                            Simpan Resep Masuk
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>

            <CustomAlertModal 
                show={customAlert.show} 
                message={customAlert.message} 
                type={customAlert.type} 
                onClose={() => setCustomAlert({ ...customAlert, show: false })} 
            />
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

=========================================================
FILE: resources/js/Pages/Prescription/Edit.jsx
=========================================================
import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import PrescriptionItemManager from '@/Components/Prescription/PrescriptionItemManager';
import CustomAlertModal from '@/Components/Prescription/CustomAlertModal';

export default function Edit({ prescription, medicines }) {
    const { data, setData, put, processing, errors } = useForm({
        patient_name: prescription.patient_name || '',
        doctor_name: prescription.doctor_name || '',
        prescription_date: prescription.prescription_date || '',
        notes: prescription.notes || '',
        items: prescription.items.map(item => ({
            medicine_id: item.medicine_id,
            name: item.medicine?.name || 'Obat',
            sku: item.medicine?.sku || '',
            quantity: item.quantity,
            signa: item.signa
        }))
    });

    const [customAlert, setCustomAlert] = useState({ show: false, message: '', type: 'error' });
    const [selectedMedicine, setSelectedMedicine] = useState('');
    const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(1);
    const [signaFreq, setSignaFreq] = useState('3 x 1');
    const [signaForm, setSignaForm] = useState('Tablet');
    const [signaTime, setSignaTime] = useState('Sesudah Makan');

    const handleAddItem = () => {
        if (!selectedMedicine || !quantity) {
            return setCustomAlert({ show: true, message: 'Pilih obat dan isi jumlahnya terlebih dahulu!', type: 'error' });
        }
        const medicine = medicines.find(m => m.id === parseInt(selectedMedicine));
        if (!medicine) return;
        
        const finalSigna = `${signaFreq} ${signaForm} ${signaTime}`;
        setData('items', [...data.items, { medicine_id: medicine.id, name: medicine.name, sku: medicine.sku, quantity: parseInt(quantity), signa: finalSigna }]);
        setSelectedMedicine(''); setQuantity(1);
    };

    const handleRemoveItem = (index) => {
        const newItems = [...data.items];
        newItems.splice(index, 1);
        setData('items', newItems);
    };

    const handleSubmit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (data.items.length === 0) {
            return setCustomAlert({ show: true, message: 'Minimal harus ada 1 obat di dalam rincian resep!', type: 'error' });
        }
        put(route('prescriptions.update', prescription.id));
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Edit Resep Masuk">
            <Head title={`Edit Resep ${prescription.prescription_number}`} />

            <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6 relative">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                    <div>
                        <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Edit Resep: {prescription.prescription_number}</h2>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">Perbarui informasi pasien atau rincian obat resep jika terdapat kesalahan.</p>
                    </div>
                    <Link href={route('prescriptions.index')} className="p-2.5 bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-xl transition-colors">
                        <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M10 19l-7-7m0 0l7-7m-7 7h18"></path></svg>
                    </Link>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
                    {/* Header Data Pasien */}
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 shadow-sm space-y-4">
                        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#48CFA4] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 pb-2">1. Informasi Pasien & Dokter</h3>
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">Nama Pasien</label>
                                <input type="text" className="w-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200" value={data.patient_name} onChange={e => setData('patient_name', e.target.value)} required />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">Nama Dokter</label>
                                <input type="text" className="w-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm font-mono text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200" value={data.doctor_name} onChange={e => setData('doctor_name', e.target.value)} required />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">Tanggal</label>
                                <input type="date" className="w-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200" value={data.prescription_date} onChange={e => setData('prescription_date', e.target.value)} required />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">Catatan / Keterangan Tambahan</label>
                            <textarea className="w-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200" rows="2" value={data.notes} onChange={e => setData('notes', e.target.value)}></textarea>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Rincian Item Obat */}
                    <PrescriptionItemManager 
                        medicines={medicines}
                        data={data}
                        setData={setData}
                        unmatchedMedicines={[]}
                        selectedMedicine={selectedMedicine}
                        setSelectedMedicine={setSelectedMedicine}
                        quantity={quantity}
                        setQuantity={setQuantity}
                        signaFreq={signaFreq}
                        setSignaFreq={setSignaFreq}
                        signaForm={signaForm}
                        setSignaForm={setSignaForm}
                        signaTime={signaTime}
                        setSignaTime={setSignaTime}
                        handleAddItem={handleAddItem}
                        handleRemoveItem={handleRemoveItem}
                    />

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3">
                        <Link href={route('prescriptions.index')} className="px-5 py-3 bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm transition-colors flex items-center">Batal</Link>
                        <button type="submit" disabled={processing} className="px-6 py-3 bg-[#48CFA4] hover:bg-[#3AB890] text-white rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-lg disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors">
                            {processing ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Perbarui Resep'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>

            <CustomAlertModal 
                show={customAlert.show} 
                message={customAlert.message} 
                type={customAlert.type} 
                onClose={() => setCustomAlert({ ...customAlert, show: false })} 
            />
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function Index({ prescriptions }) {
    const [selectedPrint, setSelectedPrint] = useState(null);
    const [selectedDelete, setSelectedDelete] = useState(null);

    const executeDelete = () => {
        if (!selectedDelete) return;
        router.delete(route('prescriptions.destroy', selectedDelete.id), {
            onSuccess: () => setSelectedDelete(null)
        });
    };

    const executePrint = () => {
        let printIframe = document.getElementById('copyresep-print-iframe');
        if (!printIframe) {
            printIframe = document.createElement('iframe');
            printIframe.id = 'copyresep-print-iframe';
            Object.assign(printIframe.style, { position: 'fixed', right: '0', bottom: '0', width: '0', height: '0', border: 'none' });
            document.body.appendChild(printIframe);
        }
        
        const iframeDoc = printIframe.contentWindow || printIframe.contentDocument.document || printIframe.contentDocument;
        iframeDoc.document.open();
        
        let itemsHtml = selectedPrint.items.map(i => `<div style="border-bottom: 1px dashed #000; padding: 6px 0;"><b>${i.medicine?.name || 'Obat'}</b> (Qty: ${i.quantity})<br/><span style="font-size:10px;">Aturan: ${i.signa}</span></div>`).join('');
        
        iframeDoc.document.write(`<html><head><style>@page { size: 58mm auto; margin: 0; } body { font-family: monospace; font-size: 11px; width: 58mm; padding: 5px; margin: 0;}</style></head><body><div style="text-align:center; border-bottom:2px dashed #000; margin-bottom:10px;"><b>SALINAN RESEP</b><br/>APOTEK MEDISYNC</div><div>No: ${selectedPrint.prescription_number}<br/>Dr: ${selectedPrint.doctor_name}<br/>Pasien: ${selectedPrint.patient_name}<br/>Tgl: ${selectedPrint.prescription_date}</div><div style="border-top:2px dashed #000; margin-top:5px;">${itemsHtml}</div><div style="text-align:center; margin-top:15px; font-weight:bold; font-size:10px;">LEKAS SEMBUH</div></body></html>`);
        iframeDoc.document.close();
        
        setTimeout(() => { printIframe.contentWindow.focus(); printIframe.contentWindow.print(); setSelectedPrint(null); }, 300);
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Manajemen Resep Masuk">
            <Head title="Daftar Resep" />
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Riwayat Resep Masuk</h2>
                <Link 
                    href={route('prescriptions.create')} 
                    title="Catat Resep Baru" 
                    className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-md text-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all duration-200 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white hover:bg-[#3bb890] shadow-sm dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#2d4747] dark:border dark:border-[#30413B] dark:shadow-none"
                >
                    <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M12 4v16m8-8H4"></path></svg>
                    <span>Input Resep Baru</span>
                </Link>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 overflow-hidden">
                <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                    <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                        <thead className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-500 text-xs uppercase">
                            <tr>
                                <th className="px-6 py-4">Nomor & Lampiran</th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-4">Pasien & Dokter</th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-4">Status</th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center">Aksi</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100 dark:divide-gray-800">
                            {(!prescriptions || prescriptions.length === 0) ? (
                                <tr><td colSpan="4" className="px-6 py-8 text-center text-gray-400 font-bold">Belum ada resep masuk.</td></tr>
                            ) : (
                                prescriptions.map((resep) => (
                                    <tr key={resep.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24]">
                                        <td className="px-6 py-4">
                                            <div className="font-mono font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{resep.prescription_number}</div>
                                            {resep.attachment && (
                                                <a href={`/storage/${resep.attachment}`} target="_blank" className="text-[10px] text-blue-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 hover:underline">
                                                    <svg className="w-3 h-3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M15.172 7l-6.586 6.586a2 2 0 102.828 2.828l6.414-6.586a4 4 0 00-5.656-5.656l-6.415 6.585a6 6 0 108.486 8.486L20.5 13"></path></svg> Lampiran Resep
                                                </a>
                                            )}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-4">
                                            <div className="font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{resep.patient_name}</div>
                                            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">{resep.doctor_name}</div>
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-4">
                                            <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold ${resep.status === 'DRAFT' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700' : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700'}`}>
                                                {resep.status === 'DRAFT' ? 'BELUM DITEBUS' : 'SUDAH DITEBUS'}
                                            </span>
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                            <div className="flex justify-center gap-2">
                                                {/* Tombol Cetak Struk */}
                                                <button 
                                                    title="Cetak Salinan Resep" 
                                                    onClick={() => setSelectedPrint(resep)} 
                                                    className="p-2.5 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 dark:bg-[#25333D] dark:hover:bg-[#2E3F4C] text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                                >
                                                    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M17 17h2a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h6a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2zm8-12V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4h10z"></path></svg>
                                                </button>

                                                {/* Tombol Edit */}
                                                {resep.status === 'DRAFT' && (
                                                    <Link 
                                                        href={route('prescriptions.edit', resep.id)} 
                                                        title="Edit Resep"
                                                        className="p-2.5 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/20 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/40 text-blue-600 rounded-xl transition-colors"
                                                    >
                                                        <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z"></path></svg>
                                                    </Link>
                                                )}

                                                {/* Tombol Hapus (Memicu Modal Kustom) */}
                                                {resep.status === 'DRAFT' && (
                                                    <button 
                                                        title="Hapus Resep" 
                                                        onClick={() => setSelectedDelete(resep)} 
                                                        className="p-2.5 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/20 dark:hover:bg-red-900/40 text-red-600 rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                                    >
                                                        <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path></svg>
                                                    </button>
                                                )}
                                            </div>
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                ))
                            )}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Modal Konfirmasi Hapus Kustom */}
            {selectedDelete && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in">
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-w-sm w-full mx-4 text-center border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/30 text-red-500 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                            <svg className="w-8 h-8" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path></svg>
                        </div>
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 mb-1">Konfirmasi Hapus</h3>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-6">
                            Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus resep <span className="font-mono font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">{selectedDelete.prescription_number}</span> untuk pasien <span className="font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">{selectedDelete.patient_name}</span>? Tindakan ini tidak dapat dibatalkan.
                        </p>
                        <div className="flex gap-2">
                            <button onClick={() => setSelectedDelete(null)} className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-800 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 font-bold rounded-xl text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                                Batal
                            </button>
                            <button onClick={executeDelete} className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-xs transition-colors shadow-md cursor-pointer">
                                Ya, Hapus
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Modal Cetak Thermal */}
            {selectedPrint && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in">
                    <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-w-sm w-full mx-4 font-mono text-xs text-black">
                        <div className="text-center pb-3 border-b border-dashed border-gray-400">
                            <div className="font-bold text-sm">SALINAN RESEP</div>
                            <div>APOTEK MEDISYNC</div>
                            <div className="mt-2 text-left">No: {selectedPrint.prescription_number}</div>
                            <div className="text-left">Tgl: {selectedPrint.prescription_date}</div>
                            <div className="text-left">Dr: {selectedPrint.doctor_name}</div>
                            <div className="text-left">Pasien: {selectedPrint.patient_name}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="py-3 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">
                            {selectedPrint.items.map((i, idx) => (
                                <div key={idx} className="border-b border-dashed border-gray-400 py-2">
                                    <div className="font-bold">{i.medicine?.name || 'Obat'} (Qty: {i.quantity})</div>
                                    <div className="text-[10px]">Aturan: {i.signa}</div>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-center py-3 text-[10px] font-bold">LEKAS SEMBUH</div>
                        <div className="flex gap-2 pt-4 mt-2">
                            <button onClick={() => setSelectedPrint(null)} className="flex-1 py-2 bg-gray-200 font-sans font-bold rounded-xl text-gray-800 cursor-pointer">Batal</button>
                            <button onClick={executePrint} className="flex-1 py-2 bg-emerald-600 font-sans font-bold rounded-xl text-white cursor-pointer">Cetak Struk Thermal</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

FILE: resources/js/Pages/Products/Create.jsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import BarcodeScannerModal from '@/Components/BarcodeScannerModal';

export default function Create({ categories, suppliers, racks }) {
    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors } = useForm({
        sku: '',
        barcode: '',
        name: '',
        generic_name: '',
        medicine_category_id: '',
        supplier_id: '',
        rack_id: '',
        dosage_form: '',
        dosage_strength: '',
        requires_prescription: false,
        base_unit: '',
        selling_price: '',
        min_stock: 0,
        max_stock: '',
    });
    const [isScannerOpen, setIsScannerOpen] = useState(false);

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        post(route('products.store'));
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Tambah Obat Baru">
            <Head title="Tambah Obat" />

            <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto">
                <div className="mb-8">
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Informasi Master Obat</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Pastikan data medis yang dimasukkan akurat sesuai kemasan pabrik.</p>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-8">
                    
                    {/* BAGIAN 1: IDENTITAS PRODUK */}
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-6 flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span className="w-2 h-6 bg-[#48CFA4] rounded-full"></span> Identitas Obat
                        </h4>
                        
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nama Obat <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                                <input type="text" value={data.name} onChange={e => setData('name', e.target.value)} required placeholder="Misal: Panadol Extra" className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100" />
                                <InputError message={errors.name} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nama Generik (Kandungan)</label>
                                <input type="text" value={data.generic_name} onChange={e => setData('generic_name', e.target.value)} placeholder="Misal: Paracetamol 500mg, Caffeine 65mg" className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100" />
                                <InputError message={errors.generic_name} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">SKU (Kode Internal)</label>
                                <input type="text" value={data.sku} onChange={e => setData('sku', e.target.value)} placeholder="Kosongkan untuk Auto-Generate" className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 uppercase" />
                                <InputError message={errors.sku} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Barcode Pabrik</label>
                                <div className="relative">
                                    <input 
                                        type="text" 
                                        value={data.barcode} 
                                        onChange={e => setData('barcode', e.target.value)} 
                                        placeholder="Ketik manual atau scan..." 
                                        className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 pr-12" 
                                    />
                                    <button 
                                        type="button" 
                                        onClick={() => setIsScannerOpen(true)}
                                        className="absolute right-2 top-2 p-1.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-[#48CFA4] bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 transition-colors"
                                        title="Scan via Kamera"
                                    >
                                        <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M3 9a2 2 0 012-2h.93a2 2 0 001.664-.89l.812-1.22A2 2 0 0110.07 4h3.86a2 2 0 011.664.89l.812 1.22A2 2 0 0018.07 7H19a2 2 0 012 2v9a2 2 0 01-2 2H5a2 2 0 01-2-2V9z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="13" r="4"></circle></svg>
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                                <InputError message={errors.barcode} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* BAGIAN 2: KLASIFIKASI & PENYIMPANAN */}
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-6 flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span className="w-2 h-6 bg-[#6FD8E8] rounded-full"></span> Klasifikasi & Penyimpanan
                        </h4>
                        
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Golongan Obat <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                                <select value={data.medicine_category_id} onChange={e => setData('medicine_category_id', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">
                                    <option value="">-- Pilih Golongan --</option>
                                    {categories.map(cat => <option key={cat.id} value={cat.id}>{cat.name}</option>)}
                                </select>
                                <InputError message={errors.medicine_category_id} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Pemasok Utama (Opsional)</label>
                                <select value={data.supplier_id} onChange={e => setData('supplier_id', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">
                                    <option value="">-- Pilih Supplier --</option>
                                    {suppliers.map(sup => <option key={sup.id} value={sup.id}>{sup.name}</option>)}
                                </select>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Lokasi Rak Fisik</label>
                                <select value={data.rack_id} onChange={e => setData('rack_id', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">
                                    <option value="">-- Pilih Lokasi --</option>
                                    {racks.map(rack => <option key={rack.id} value={rack.id}>{rack.name} ({rack.code})</option>)}
                                </select>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center mt-8">
                                <input type="checkbox" id="resep" checked={data.requires_prescription} onChange={e => setData('requires_prescription', e.target.checked)} className="w-5 h-5 text-[#48CFA4] bg-gray-100 border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-[#48CFA4] dark:focus:ring-[#48CFA4] dark:ring-offset-gray-800 focus:ring-2 dark:bg-gray-700 dark:border-gray-600" />
                                <label htmlFor="resep" className="ml-3 text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-300">Wajib Resep Dokter</label>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* SPESIFIKASI MEDIS, HARGA & STOK */}
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-6 flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span className="w-2 h-6 bg-[#F6A84B] rounded-full"></span> Spesifikasi, Harga & Stok

                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Satuan Terkecil <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                                <input type="text" value={data.base_unit} onChange={e => setData('base_unit', e.target.value)} required placeholder="Misal: Strip, Botol, Pcs" className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100" />
                                <InputError message={errors.base_unit} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Harga Jual (HJA) <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                                <input type="number" min="0" value={data.selling_price} onChange={e => setData('selling_price', e.target.value)} required placeholder="Misal: 15000" className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 font-mono" />
                                <InputError message={errors.selling_price} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>

                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Batas Stok Maksimal</label>
                                <input type="number" min="0" value={data.max_stock} onChange={e => setData('max_stock', e.target.value)} placeholder="Opsional" className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100" />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-4 pt-4 pb-12">
                        <Link href={route('products.index')} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center">Batal</Link>
                        <PrimaryButton type="submit" disabled={processing} className="px-8 py-3 text-base">
                            {processing ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan Obat'}
                        </PrimaryButton>
                    </div>

                    <BarcodeScannerModal 
                        isOpen={isScannerOpen} 
                        onClose={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)} 
                        onScan={(code) => {
                            setData('barcode', code);
                            setIsScannerOpen(false);
                        }} 
                    />

                </form>
            </div>
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import BarcodeScannerModal from '@/Components/BarcodeScannerModal';

export default function Edit({ product, categories, suppliers, racks }) {
    const { data, setData, put, processing, errors } = useForm({
        sku: product.sku || '',
        barcode: product.barcode || '',
        name: product.name || '',
        generic_name: product.generic_name || '',
        medicine_category_id: product.medicine_category_id || '',
        supplier_id: product.supplier_id || '',
        rack_id: product.rack_id || '',
        dosage_form: product.dosage_form || '',
        dosage_strength: product.dosage_strength || '',
        requires_prescription: product.requires_prescription || false,
        base_unit: product.base_unit || '',
        selling_price: product.selling_price || '',
        min_stock: product.min_stock || 0,
        max_stock: product.max_stock || '',
    });
    
    const [isScannerOpen, setIsScannerOpen] = useState(false);

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        put(route('products.update', product.id));
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Edit Obat">
            <Head title={`Edit ${product.name}`} />

            <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto">
                <div className="mb-8">
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Edit Data: {product.name}</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Pastikan pembaruan data selaras dengan stok fisik yang ada di apotek.</p>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-8">
                    
                    {/* IDENTITAS PRODUK */}
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-6 flex items-center gap-2">

                        </h4>
                        
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nama Obat <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                                <input type="text" value={data.name} onChange={e => setData('name', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100" />
                                <InputError message={errors.name} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nama Generik (Kandungan)</label>
                                <input type="text" value={data.generic_name} onChange={e => setData('generic_name', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100" />
                                <InputError message={errors.generic_name} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">SKU (Kode Internal)</label>
                                <input type="text" value={data.sku} onChange={e => setData('sku', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 uppercase" />
                                <InputError message={errors.sku} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Barcode Pabrik</label>
                                <div className="relative">
                                    <input 
                                        type="text"

FILE: resources/js/Pages/Products/Index.jsx
