import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import ConfirmModal from '@/Components/ConfirmModal';
import { useState } from 'react';

const EditIcon = () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z"></path></svg>;
const DeleteIcon = () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path></svg>;

export default function Index({ products }) {
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [productToDelete, setProductToDelete] = useState(null);

    const confirmDelete = (product) => {
        setProductToDelete(product);
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    const executeDelete = () => {
        router.delete(route('products.destroy', productToDelete.id), {
            preserveScroll: true,
            onFinish: () => setIsModalOpen(false)
        });
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Katalog Produk & Obat">
            <Head title="Katalog Produk" />

            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Daftar Master Data Obat</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Pusat data SKU, kategori, dan spesifikasi medis produk.</p>
                </div>
                <PrimaryButton as="link" href={route('products.create')}>
                    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M12 4v16m8-8H4"></path></svg>
                    Tambah Obat Baru
                </PrimaryButton>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors duration-300">
                <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                    <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 tracking-wider">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">SKU / Nama Obat</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Golongan</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Dosis & Satuan</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Rak (Lokasi)</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Resep</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Aksi</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        {!products || products.length === 0 ? (
                            <tr>
                                <td colSpan="6" className="px-6 py-16 text-center text-gray-400">Katalog obat masih kosong.</td>
                            </tr>
                        ) : (
                            products.map((product) => (
                                <tr key={product.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 dark:border-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24] transition-colors">
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5">
                                        <div className="font-mono text-xs text-gray-500 mb-1">{product.sku}</div>
                                        <div className="font-bold text-blue-600 dark:text-[#6FD8E8]">{product.name}</div>
                                        {product.generic_name && <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-400">{product.generic_name}</div>}
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5">
                                        <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
                                            {product.category?.name || '-'}
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">
                                        <span className="font-bold">{product.dosage_strength || '-'}</span> {product.dosage_form} 
                                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Satuan: <span className="font-bold">{product.base_unit}</span></div>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-bold text-[#48CFA4]">{product.rack?.code || '-'}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                        {product.requires_prescription ? 
                                            <span className="text-red-500 font-bold text-xs bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/30 px-2 py-1 rounded-md">Wajib</span> : 
                                            <span className="text-gray-400 text-xs">-</span>
                                        }
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                        <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-3">
                                            <Link href={route('products.edit', product.id)} className="p-2 text-[#48CFA4] bg-[#EAF5F0] hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#48CFA4] dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200"><EditIcon /></Link>
                                            <button onClick={() => confirmDelete(product)} className="p-2 text-red-500 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-500 dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200"><DeleteIcon /></button>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            ))
                        )}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            <ConfirmModal 
                isOpen={isModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={executeDelete}
                title="Hapus Obat?"
                message={<>Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus <span className="font-bold">{productToDelete?.name}</span>?</>}
            />
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

// Form Ganti Profil
const UpdateProfileForm = () => {

                </button>

            </div>
        </form>
    );
};

// Form Ganti Password
const UpdatePasswordForm = () => {
    const { data, setData, put, errors, processing, recentlySuccessful, reset } = useForm({
        current_password: '',
        password: '',
        password_confirmation: '',
    });

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        put(route('password.update'), {
            preserveScroll: true,
            onSuccess: () => reset(),
        });
    };

    return (
        <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-6">
            <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Password Saat Ini</label>
                <input 
                    type="password" 
                    value={data.current_password} 
                    onChange={e => setData('current_password', e.target.value)} 
                    className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                />
                <InputError message={errors.current_password} className="mt-2" />
            </div>
            <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Password Baru</label>
                <input 
                    type="password" 
                    value={data.password} 
                    onChange={e => setData('password', e.target.value)} 
                    className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                />
                <InputError message={errors.password} className="mt-2" />
            </div>
            <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Konfirmasi Password Baru</label>
                <input 
                    type="password" 
                    value={data.password_confirmation} 
                    onChange={e => setData('password_confirmation', e.target.value)} 
                    className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                />
                <InputError message={errors.password_confirmation} className="mt-2" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <button disabled={processing} className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-gray-800 dark:bg-gray-200 text-white dark:text-gray-900 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-gray-700 dark:hover:bg-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-50">
                    {processing ? 'Memperbarui...' : 'Perbarui Password'}
                </button>
                {recentlySuccessful && <p className="text-sm text-green-600 dark:text-green-400 font-medium">Password diperbarui.</p>}
            </div>
        </form>
    );
};

FILE: resources/js/Pages/Profile/Partials/DeleteUserForm.jsx
=========================================================
import DangerButton from '@/Components/DangerButton';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import InputLabel from '@/Components/InputLabel';
import Modal from '@/Components/Modal';
import SecondaryButton from '@/Components/SecondaryButton';
import TextInput from '@/Components/TextInput';
import { useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';

export default function DeleteUserForm({ className = '' }) {
    const [confirmingUserDeletion, setConfirmingUserDeletion] = useState(false);
    const passwordInput = useRef();

    const {
        data,
        setData,
        delete: destroy,
        processing,
        reset,
        errors,
        clearErrors,
    } = useForm({
        password: '',
    });

    const confirmUserDeletion = () => {
        setConfirmingUserDeletion(true);
    };

    const deleteUser = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        destroy(route('profile.destroy'), {
            preserveScroll: true,
            onSuccess: () => closeModal(),
            onError: () => passwordInput.current.focus(),
            onFinish: () => reset(),
        });
    };

    const closeModal = () => {
        setConfirmingUserDeletion(false);

        clearErrors();
        reset();
    };

    return (
        <section className={`space-y-6 ${className}`}>
            <header>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900">
                    Delete Account
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">
                    Once your account is deleted, all of its resources and data
                    will be permanently deleted. Before deleting your account,
                    please download any data or information that you wish to
                    retain.
                </p>
            </header>

            <DangerButton onClick={confirmUserDeletion}>
                Delete Account
            </DangerButton>

            <Modal show={confirmingUserDeletion} onClose={closeModal}>
                <form onSubmit={deleteUser} className="p-6">
                    <h2 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900">
                        Are you sure you want to delete your account?
                    </h2>

                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">
                        Once your account is deleted, all of its resources and
                        data will be permanently deleted. Please enter your
                        password to confirm you would like to permanently delete
                        your account.
                    </p>

                    <div className="mt-6">
                        <InputLabel
                            htmlFor="password"
                            value="Password"
                            className="sr-only"
                        />

                        <TextInput
                            id="password"
                            type="password"
                            name="password"
                            ref={passwordInput}
                            value={data.password}
                            onChange={(e) =>
                                setData('password', e.target.value)
                            }
                            className="mt-1 block w-3/4"
                            isFocused
                            placeholder="Password"
                        />

                        <InputError
                            message={errors.password}
                            className="mt-2"
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end">
                        <SecondaryButton onClick={closeModal}>
                            Cancel
                        </SecondaryButton>

                        <DangerButton className="ms-3" disabled={processing}>
                            Delete Account
                        </DangerButton>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </Modal>
        </section>
    );
}


=========================================================
FILE: resources/js/Pages/Profile/Partials/UpdatePasswordForm.jsx
=========================================================
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import InputLabel from '@/Components/InputLabel';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import TextInput from '@/Components/TextInput';
import { Transition } from '@headlessui/react';
import { useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useRef } from 'react';

export default function UpdatePasswordForm({ className = '' }) {
    const passwordInput = useRef();
    const currentPasswordInput = useRef();

    const {
        data,
        setData,
        errors,
        put,
        reset,
        processing,
        recentlySuccessful,
    } = useForm({
        current_password: '',
        password: '',
        password_confirmation: '',
    });

    const updatePassword = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        put(route('password.update'), {
            preserveScroll: true,
            onSuccess: () => reset(),
            onError: (errors) => {
                if (errors.password) {
                    reset('password', 'password_confirmation');
                    passwordInput.current.focus();
                }

                if (errors.current_password) {
                    reset('current_password');
                    currentPasswordInput.current.focus();
                }
            },
        });
    };

    return (
        <section className={className}>
            <header>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900">
                    Update Password
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">
                    Ensure your account is using a long, random password to stay
                    secure.
                </p>
            </header>

            <form onSubmit={updatePassword} className="mt-6 space-y-6">
                <div>
                    <InputLabel
                        htmlFor="current_password"
                        value="Current Password"
                    />

                    <TextInput
                        id="current_password"
                        ref={currentPasswordInput}
                        value={data.current_password}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                            setData('current_password', e.target.value)
                        }
                        type="password"
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="current-password"
                    />

                    <InputError
                        message={errors.current_password}
                        className="mt-2"
                    />
                </div>

                <div>
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="password" value="New Password" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="password"
                        ref={passwordInput}
                        value={data.password}
                        onChange={(e) => setData('password', e.target.value)}
                        type="password"
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="new-password"
                    />

                    <InputError message={errors.password} className="mt-2" />
                </div>

                <div>
                    <InputLabel
                        htmlFor="password_confirmation"
                        value="Confirm Password"
                    />

                    <TextInput
                        id="password_confirmation"
                        value={data.password_confirmation}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                            setData('password_confirmation', e.target.value)
                        }
                        type="password"
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        autoComplete="new-password"
                    />

                    <InputError
                        message={errors.password_confirmation}
                        className="mt-2"
                    />
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <PrimaryButton disabled={processing}>Save</PrimaryButton>

                    <Transition
                        show={recentlySuccessful}
                        enter="transition ease-in-out"
                        enterFrom="opacity-0"
                        leave="transition ease-in-out"
                        leaveTo="opacity-0"
                    >
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                            Saved.
                        </p>
                    </Transition>
                </div>
            </form>
        </section>
    );
}

import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import InputLabel from '@/Components/InputLabel';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import TextInput from '@/Components/TextInput';
import { Transition } from '@headlessui/react';
import { Link, useForm, usePage } from '@inertiajs/react';

export default function UpdateProfileInformation({
    mustVerifyEmail,
    status,
    className = '',
}) {
    const user = usePage().props.auth.user;

    const { data, setData, patch, errors, processing, recentlySuccessful } =
        useForm({
            name: user.name,
            email: user.email,
        });

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        patch(route('profile.update'));
    };

    return (
        <section className={className}>
            <header>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900">
                    Profile Information
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">
                    Update your account's profile information and email address.
                </p>
            </header>

            <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-6 space-y-6">
                <div>
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="name" value="Name" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="name"
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        value={data.name}
                        onChange={(e) => setData('name', e.target.value)}
                        required
                        isFocused
                        autoComplete="name"
                    />

                    <InputError className="mt-2" message={errors.name} />
                </div>

                <div>
                    <InputLabel htmlFor="email" value="Email" />

                    <TextInput
                        id="email"
                        type="email"
                        className="mt-1 block w-full"
                        value={data.email}
                        onChange={(e) => setData('email', e.target.value)}
                        required
                        autoComplete="username"
                    />

                    <InputError className="mt-2" message={errors.email} />
                </div>

                {mustVerifyEmail && user.email_verified_at === null && (
                    <div>
                        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-800">
                            Your email address is unverified.
                            <Link
                                href={route('verification.send')}
                                method="post"
                                as="button"
                                className="rounded-md text-sm text-gray-600 underline hover:text-gray-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:ring-offset-2"
                            >
                                Click here to re-send the verification email.
                            </Link>
                        </p>

                        {status === 'verification-link-sent' && (
                            <div className="mt-2 text-sm font-medium text-green-600">
                                A new verification link has been sent to your
                                email address.
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                )}

                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <PrimaryButton disabled={processing}>Save</PrimaryButton>

                    <Transition
                        show={recentlySuccessful}
                        enter="transition ease-in-out"
                        enterFrom="opacity-0"
                        leave="transition ease-in-out"
                        leaveTo="opacity-0"
                    >
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                            Saved.
                        </p>
                    </Transition>
                </div>
            </form>
        </section>
    );
}

FILE: resources/js/Pages/Purchases/Batches.jsx

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link } from '@inertiajs/react';

export default function Batches({ batches }) {
    const getExpiredStatus = (expiredDate) => {
        const today = new Date();
        const exp = new Date(expiredDate);
        const diffTime = exp - today;
        const diffDays = Math.ceil(diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));

        if (diffDays < 0) {
            return <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-red-100 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase">Sudah Kedaluwarsa</span>;
        } else if (diffDays <= 90) { // Kurang dari 3 bulan
            return <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-900/30 dark:text-amber-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase">Kritis (&lt; 3 Bulan)</span>;
        } else {
            return <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 dark:text-emerald-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase">Aman</span>;
        }
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Monitoring Batch & Kedaluwarsa (FEFO)">
            <Head title="Monitoring FEFO" />

            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:justify-between md:items-center gap-4 mb-8">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Daftar Stok Batch Aktif</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Sistem otomatis memprioritaskan pengeluaran obat berdasarkan tanggal kedaluwarsa terdekat.</p>
                </div>
                <Link 
                    href={route('purchases.index')}
                    className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors text-center"
                >
                    &larr; Kembali ke Faktur Pembelian
                </Link>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                    <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-300 uppercase bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 tracking-wider">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Nama Obat (SKU)</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Nomor Batch</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Supplier / PBF</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Sisa Stok Batch</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Tanggal Kedaluwarsa (ED)</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Status FEFO</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-50 dark:divide-gray-800">
                        {!batches || batches.length === 0 ? (
                            <tr>
                                <td colSpan="6" className="px-6 py-16 text-center text-gray-400 font-bold">Belum ada data stok batch aktif di gudang.</td>
                            </tr>
                        ) : (
                            batches.map((batch) => (
                                <tr key={batch.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24] transition-colors">
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5">
                                        {/* Diubah dari batch.product menjadi batch.medicine */}
                                        <div className="font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">{batch.medicine?.name || 'Nama Obat Tidak Tersedia'}</div>
                                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-mono">{batch.medicine?.sku}</div>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-mono font-bold text-[#48CFA4]">{batch.batch_number}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 font-medium">
                                        {batch.purchase_detail?.purchase?.supplier?.name || 'Pembelian Langsung'}
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-mono font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">
                                        {batch.stock_quantity} <span className="text-xs font-normal text-gray-400">{batch.medicine?.base_unit}</span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-mono text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 font-bold">{batch.expired_date}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                        {getExpiredStatus(batch.expired_date)}
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            ))
                        )}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

import React from 'react';
import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';

export default function Create({ suppliers, medicines }) {
    const generateReceiptNo = () => {
        const date = new Date();
        const ymd = date.getFullYear().toString() + (date.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0') + date.getDate().toString().padStart(2, '0');
        const timeSlug = date.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0') + date.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0') + date.getSeconds().toString().padStart(2, '0');
        const rnd = Math.floor(100 + Math.random() * 900);
        return `RCV-${ymd}-${timeSlug}-${rnd}`;
    };

    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors } = useForm({
        internal_receipt_number: generateReceiptNo(),
        invoice_number: '',
        supplier_id: '',
        purchase_date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
        notes: '',
        total_amount: 0,
        items: [
            { medicine_id: '', batch_number: '', expired_date: '', quantity: 1, uom: 'Tablet', purchase_price: 0, subtotal: 0 }
        ],
    });

    const formatRupiah = (number) => {
        return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', { style: 'currency', currency: 'IDR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(number);
    };

    const addItem = () => {
        setData('items', [
            ...data.items, 
            { medicine_id: '', batch_number: '', expired_date: '', quantity: 1, uom: 'Tablet', purchase_price: 0, subtotal: 0 }
        ]);
    };

    const removeItem = (index) => {
        const newItems = data.items.filter((_, i) => i !== index);
        const total = newItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.subtotal, 0);
        setData({ ...data, items: newItems, total_amount: total });
    };

    const handleItemChange = (index, field, value) => {
        const newItems = [...data.items];
        newItems[index][field] = value;
        
        if (field === 'medicine_id') {
            const selectedMed = medicines.find(m => m.id == value);
            if (selectedMed) {
                newItems[index].uom = selectedMed.base_unit || 'Tablet';
            }
        }
        
        if (field === 'quantity' || field === 'purchase_price' || field === 'medicine_id') {
            const qty = parseFloat(newItems[index].quantity) || 0;
            const price = parseFloat(newItems[index].purchase_price) || 0;
            newItems[index].subtotal = qty * price;
        }
        
        const total = newItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.subtotal, 0);
        setData({ ...data, items: newItems, total_amount: total });
    };

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        post(route('purchases.store'));
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Catat Pembelian & Barang Masuk">
            <Head title="Faktur Masuk" />

            <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto pb-12">
                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-6">
                    
                    {/* NOTIFIKASI ERROR UMUM DARI BACKEND */}
                    {errors.message && (
                        <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/30 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-800 rounded-2xl text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 text-xs font-bold shadow-sm">
                            ⚠️ {errors.message}
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* KARTU HEADER DOKUMEN */}
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                            <div>
                                <h4 className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <span className="w-2.5 h-6 bg-[#48CFA4] rounded-full"></span> Informasi Dokumen Pembelian (PBF)
                                </h4>
                                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Nomor sistem dibuat otomatis, sesuaikan data fisik dari faktur distributor.</p>
                            </div>
                            <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-[#EAF5F0] dark:bg-[#253939] text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#67E2B4] rounded-full text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                                Modul Gudang
                            </span>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-2">No. Sistem (Internal)</label>
                                <input type="text" value={data.internal_receipt_number} disabled className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-2xl text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 font-mono font-bold cursor-not-allowed" />
                                <InputError message={errors.internal_receipt_number} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">No. Faktur PBF Asli <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                                <input type="text" value={data.invoice_number} onChange={e => setData('invoice_number', e.target.value)} required placeholder="Cth: INV/2026/001" className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-2xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 uppercase font-bold" />
                                <InputError message={errors.invoice_number} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Pilih Supplier PBF <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                                <select value={data.supplier_id} onChange={e => setData('supplier_id', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-2xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 font-medium">
                                    <option value="">-- Pilih Supplier --</option>
                                    {suppliers.map(sup => <option key={sup.id} value={sup.id}>{sup.name}</option>)}
                                </select>
                                <InputError message={errors.supplier_id} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Tanggal di Faktur <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                                <input type="date" value={data.purchase_date} onChange={e => setData('purchase_date', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-2xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 font-medium" />
                                <InputError message={errors.purchase_date} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* KARTU RINCIAN OBAT */}
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors">
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                            <div>
                                <h4 className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <span className="w-2.5 h-6 bg-[#F6A84B] rounded-full"></span> Rincian Item Obat Masuk
                                </h4>
                                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Masukkan nomor batch dan tanggal kadaluarsa (minimal 6 bulan ke depan dari hari ini).</p>
                            </div>
                            <button type="button" onClick={addItem} className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-[#EAF5F0] text-[#246A55] dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] rounded-2xl text-xs font-extrabold hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white transition-all shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                                + Tambah Baris Obat
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div className="overflow-x-auto pb-2">
                            <table className="w-full text-sm text-left min-w-[950px]">
                                <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase bg-gray-50/70 dark:bg-[#151E24] tracking-wider">
                                    <tr>
                                        <th className="px-4 py-3.5 rounded-l-xl w-[26%]">Pilih Obat Master</th>
                                        <th className="px-4 py-3.5 w-[16%]">No. Batch Pabrik</th>
                                        <th className="px-4 py-3.5 w-[16%]">Expired Date (Min. 6 Bln)</th>
                                        <th className="px-4 py-3.5 w-[18%] text-center">Jumlah & Satuan</th>
                                        <th className="px-4 py-3.5 w-[14%]">Harga Beli</th>
                                        <th className="px-4 py-3.5 w-[10%] text-right">Subtotal</th>
                                        <th className="px-2 py-3.5 rounded-r-xl text-center w-12">Aksi</th>
                                    </tr>
                                </thead>
                                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-50 dark:divide-gray-800/50">
                                    {data.items.map((item, index) => (
                                        <tr key={index} className="hover:bg-gray-50/30 dark:hover:bg-gray-800/10 transition-colors align-top">
                                            <td className="px-2 py-3">
                                                <select value={item.medicine_id} onChange={e => handleItemChange(index, 'medicine_id', e.target.value)} required className="w-full text-xs px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 font-medium">
                                                    <option value="">-- Cari Obat --</option>
                                                    {medicines.map(med => <option key={med.id} value={med.id}>{med.sku} — {med.name}</option>)}
                                                </select>
                                                <InputError message={errors[`items.${index}.medicine_id`]} className="mt-1" />
                                            </td>

                                            </td>

                                                <div className="flex gap-2">
                                                    <input type="number" min="1" value={item.quantity} onChange={e => handleItemChange(index, 'quantity', e.target.value)} required className="w-16 text-xs text-center px-2 py-2.5 bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 font-bold" />

                                                        <option value="Pcs">Pcs</option>
                                                    </select>

                                            <td className="px-2 py-3">
                                                <div className="relative">
                                                    <span className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400 text-xs">Rp</span>
                                                    <input type="number" min="0" value={item.purchase_price} onChange={e => handleItemChange(index, 'purchase_price', e.target.value)} required className="w-full text-xs pl-8 pr-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 font-mono font-bold" />
                                                </div>
                                                <InputError message={errors[`items.${index}.purchase_price`]} className="mt-1" />
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="px-2 py-3 text-right font-mono font-bold text-[#48CFA4]">
                                                {formatRupiah(item.subtotal)}
                                            </td>

                                                <button type="button" onClick={() => removeItem(index)} disabled={data.items.length === 1} className="p-2 text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 dark:hover:text-red-400 disabled:opacity-20 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                                                    <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path></svg>

                                    ))}

                            </table>

                        {/* CATATAN & TOTAL KESELURUHAN */}
                        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-end border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 pt-6">
                            <div className="w-full md:w-1/2 mb-6 md:mb-0">
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-2">Catatan Keterangan Faktur</label>

                                <InputError message={errors.notes} className="mt-2" />
                            </div>
                            
                            <div className="text-right w-full md:w-auto bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                                <p className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400 mb-1">Total Nilai Faktur:</p>
                                <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 mb-6 font-mono">
                                    {formatRupiah(data.total_amount)}
                                </h2>
                                
                                <div className="flex justify-end gap-3">
                                    <Link href={route('purchases.index')} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors">Batal</Link>
                                    <PrimaryButton type="submit" disabled={processing || data.items.length === 0} className="px-8 py-3 text-sm shadow-lg shadow-[#48CFA4]/20 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer">
                                        {processing ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan & Cetak BPB'}
                                    </PrimaryButton>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

=========================================================

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, usePage, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export default function Index({ purchases }) {
    const { flash } = usePage().props;
    const [confirmingDeletion, setConfirmingDeletion] = useState(false);
    const [selectedPurchase, setSelectedPurchase] = useState(null);
    const [printModalOpen, setPrintModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [activePurchaseToPrint, setActivePurchaseToPrint] = useState(null);

    // STATE BARU: Modal Rincian Detail Item Pembelian
    const [detailModalOpen, setDetailModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [activePurchaseForDetail, setActivePurchaseForDetail] = useState(null);

    // STATE: Untuk pengelolaan pilihan checkbox (bulk delete)
    const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState([]);
    const [confirmingBulkDeletion, setConfirmingBulkDeletion] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (flash?.print_url) {
        }
    }, [flash]);

    const formatRupiah = (number) => {
        return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', { style: 'currency', currency: 'IDR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(number || 0);
    };

    // Fungsi Pilih Semua Checkbox
    const handleSelectAll = (e) => {
        if (e.target.checked) {
            setSelectedIds(purchases.map(p => p.id));
        } else {
            setSelectedIds([]);
        }
    };

    // Fungsi Pilih Satuan Checkbox
    const handleSelectOne = (id) => {
        if (selectedIds.includes(id)) {
            setSelectedIds(selectedIds.filter(item => item !== id));
        } else {
            setSelectedIds([...selectedIds, id]);
        }
    };

    const confirmDelete = (purchase) => {
        setSelectedPurchase(purchase);
        setConfirmingDeletion(true);
    };

    const deletePurchase = () => {
        if (selectedPurchase) {
            router.delete(route('purchases.destroy', selectedPurchase.id), {
                onSuccess: () => {
                    setConfirmingDeletion(false);
                    setSelectedPurchase(null);
                },
            });
        }
    };

    // Fungsi Hapus Massal (Bulk Delete)
    const deleteBulkPurchases = () => {
        router.post(route('purchases.bulk-destroy'), { ids: selectedIds }, {
            onSuccess: () => {
                setConfirmingBulkDeletion(false);
                setSelectedIds([]);
            },
        });
    };

    const getStatusBadge = (status) => {
        switch(status) {
            case 'completed': return <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-green-100 text-green-700 dark:bg-green-900/30 dark:text-green-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Selesai</span>;
            case 'draft': return <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700 dark:bg-yellow-900/30 dark:text-yellow-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Draft</span>;
            case 'cancelled': return <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-red-100 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Batal</span>;
            default: return status || <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-green-100 text-green-700 dark:bg-green-900/30 dark:text-green-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Selesai</span>;
        }
    };

    const handleOpenPrintModal = (purchase) => {
        setActivePurchaseToPrint(purchase);
        setPrintModalOpen(true);
    };

    const handleOpenDetailModal = (purchase) => {
        setActivePurchaseForDetail(purchase);
        setDetailModalOpen(true);
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Pembelian & Barang Masuk">
            <Head title="Data Pembelian" />

            {/* HEADER & KELOMPOK TOMBOL AKSI */}
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4 mb-6">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Riwayat Faktur Pembelian</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">Kelola data pembelian dan pantau masa kedaluwarsa obat.</p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    {/* Tombol Hapus Massal */}
                    {selectedIds.length > 0 && (
                        <button 
                            onClick={() => setConfirmingBulkDeletion(true)}
                            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 shadow-md shadow-red-600/20 font-bold text-xs tracking-wide cursor-pointer animate-in fade-in"
                        >
                            <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
                            </svg>
                            <span>Hapus ({selectedIds.length})</span>
                        </button>
                    )}

                    <Link 
                        href={route('batches.index')}
                        title="Cek Masa Kedaluwarsa (FEFO)"
                        className="p-2.5 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 hover:text-[#48CFA4] dark:hover:text-[#67E2B4] rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700/60 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer shadow-sm"
                    >
                        <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M12 8v4l3 3m6-3a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path>
                        </svg>
                    </Link>

                    <Link 
                        href={route('purchases.trash')}
                        title="Lihat Tong Sampah"
                        className="p-2.5 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500 dark:hover:text-red-400 rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700/60 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer shadow-sm"
                    >
                        <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
                        </svg>
                    </Link>

                    <Link 
                        href={route('purchases.create')} 
                        title="Catat Faktur Baru"
                        className="p-2.5 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white hover:bg-[#3bb890] rounded-md transition-colors shadow-sm dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#2d4747] dark:border dark:border-[#30413B] flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M12 4v16m8-8H4"></path>
                        </svg>
                    </Link>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors duration-300">
                <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                    <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-300 uppercase bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 tracking-wider">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-4 py-4 w-10 text-center">
                                <input 
                                    type="checkbox" 
                                    onChange={handleSelectAll}
                                    checked={purchases.length > 0 && selectedIds.length === purchases.length}
                                    className="rounded border-gray-300 text-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-[#48CFA4] dark:bg-gray-800 dark:border-gray-700 cursor-pointer"
                                />
                            </th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">No. Faktur PBF</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Tanggal</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Supplier</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-right">Total Nilai (Rp)</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Status</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Aksi</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        {!purchases || purchases.length === 0 ? (
                            <tr>
                                <td colSpan="7" className="px-6 py-16 text-center text-gray-400">Belum ada data faktur pembelian.</td>
                            </tr>
                        ) : (
                            purchases.map((purchase) => (
                                <tr key={purchase.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 dark:border-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24] transition-colors">
                                    <td className="px-4 py-5 text-center">
                                        <input 
                                            type="checkbox" 
                                            checked={selectedIds.includes(purchase.id)}
                                            onChange={() => handleSelectOne(purchase.id)}
                                            className="rounded border-gray-300 text-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-[#48CFA4] dark:bg-gray-800 dark:border-gray-700 cursor-pointer"
                                        />
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-bold text-[#48CFA4]">
                                        {purchase.invoice_number}
                                        <span className="block text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-normal">Internal: {purchase.internal_receipt_number}</span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200">{purchase.purchase_date}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{purchase.supplier?.name || '-'}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-right font-mono font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200">
                                        {formatRupiah(purchase.total_amount)}
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                        {getStatusBadge(purchase.status)}
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                                            {/* TOMBOL LIHAT DETAIL ITEM OBAT */}
                                            <button 
                                                onClick={() => handleOpenDetailModal(purchase)} 
                                                title="Lihat Detail Item Obat"
                                                className="p-2 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 dark:text-emerald-400 rounded-xl hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                            >
                                                <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M15 12a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z" /><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M2.458 12C3.732 7.943 7.523 5 12 5c4.478 0 8.268 2.943 9.542 7-1.274 4.057-5.064 7-9.542 7-4.477 0-8.268-2.943-9.542-7z" /></svg>
                                            </button>

                                            {/* TOMBOL CETAK BPB / PDF */}
                                            <button 
                                                onClick={() => handleOpenPrintModal(purchase)} 
                                                title="Cetak BPB"
                                                className="p-2 bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 dark:bg-blue-900/30 dark:text-blue-400 rounded-xl hover:bg-blue-500 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                            >
                                                <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M17 17h2a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h6a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2zm8-12V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4h10z"></path></svg>
                                            </button>

                                            {/* TOMBOL HAPUS */}
                                            <button 
                                                onClick={() => confirmDelete(purchase)}
                                                title="Hapus Faktur"
                                                className="p-2 bg-red-50 text-red-600 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 rounded-xl hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                            >
                                                <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path></svg>
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            ))
                        )}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            {/* MODAL POP-UP RINCIAN ITEM OBAT FAKTUR */}
            {detailModalOpen && activePurchaseForDetail && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200 p-4">
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-3xl max-w-2xl w-full p-6 space-y-4 shadow-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 pb-3">
                            <div>
                                <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-[#EAF5F0] dark:bg-[#253939] text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#67E2B4] rounded-full text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider">
                                    Item Obat Terdaftar
                                </span>
                                <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 text-lg mt-1 font-mono">
                                    {activePurchaseForDetail.invoice_number}
                                </h3>
                                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Supplier: {activePurchaseForDetail.supplier?.name || '-'}</p>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setDetailModalOpen(false)}
                                className="p-2 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 dark:hover:text-gray-200 rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"></path></svg>
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        {/* TABEL ITEM OBAT */}
                        <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
                            <table className="w-full text-left text-xs border-collapse">
                                <thead>
                                    <tr className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase">
                                        <th className="p-3">Nama Obat</th>
                                        <th className="p-3">No. Batch</th>
                                        <th className="p-3">Expired Date</th>
                                        <th className="p-3 text-center">Qty</th>
                                        <th className="p-3 text-right">Harga Beli</th>
                                        <th className="p-3 text-right">Subtotal</th>
                                    </tr>
                                </thead>
                                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100 dark:divide-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">
                                    {activePurchaseForDetail.details && activePurchaseForDetail.details.length > 0 ? (
                                        activePurchaseForDetail.details.map((detail) => (
                                            <tr key={detail.id}>
                                                <td className="p-3 font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">
                                                    {detail.medicine?.name || 'Obat Dihapus'}
                                                </td>
                                                <td className="p-3 font-mono text-[#48CFA4]">{detail.batch_number}</td>
                                                <td className="p-3">{detail.expired_date}</td>
                                                <td className="p-3 text-center font-bold">{detail.quantity}</td>
                                                <td className="p-3 text-right">{formatRupiah(detail.purchase_price)}</td>
                                                <td className="p-3 text-right font-bold text-emerald-500">
                                                    {formatRupiah(detail.subtotal)}
                                                </td>
                                            </tr>
                                        ))
                                    ) : (
                                        <tr>
                                            <td colSpan="6" className="p-4 text-center text-gray-400 italic">
                                                Tidak ada rincian item obat.
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    )}
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 pt-3">
                            <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold">Total Nilai Pembelian:</span>
                            <span className="text-lg font-black text-emerald-500">{formatRupiah(activePurchaseForDetail.total_amount)}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* MODAL KONFIRMASI HAPUS SATUAN */}
            {confirmingDeletion && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full mx-4 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 text-center">
                        <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/30 text-red-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 flex-shrink-0">
                            <svg className="w-8 h-8 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
                            </svg>
                        </div>

                        <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 mb-2">Hapus Faktur Pembelian?</h3>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-6">
                            Anda akan menghapus faktur <strong className="text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{selectedPurchase?.invoice_number}</strong>. Stok obat di POS akan otomatis terkurangi.
                        </p>
                        <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
                            <button 
                                onClick={() => setConfirmingDeletion(false)}
                                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Batal
                            </button>
                            <button 
                                onClick={deletePurchase}
                                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors shadow-lg shadow-red-600/30 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Ya, Hapus
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* MODAL KONFIRMASI HAPUS MASSAL (BULK DELETE) */}
            {confirmingBulkDeletion && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full mx-4 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 text-center">
                        <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/30 text-red-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 flex-shrink-0">
                            <svg className="w-8 h-8 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
                            </svg>
                        </div>

                        <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 mb-2">Hapus {selectedIds.length} Faktur Terpilih?</h3>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-6">
                            Semua faktur pembelian yang dicentang akan dipindahkan ke tong sampah secara bersamaan dan stok POS dikurangi.
                        </p>
                        <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
                            <button 
                                onClick={() => setConfirmingBulkDeletion(false)}
                                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Batal
                            </button>
                            <button 
                                onClick={deleteBulkPurchases}
                                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors shadow-lg shadow-red-600/30 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Ya, Hapus Semua
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* MODAL PRATINJAU & CETAK DOKUMEN */}
            {printModalOpen && activePurchaseToPrint && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200 p-4 overflow-y-auto">
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-6 md:p-7 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-w-4xl w-full border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 flex flex-col my-auto">
                        
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-center pb-4 mb-3 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                            <div>
                                <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-[#EAF5F0] dark:bg-[#253939] text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#67E2B4] rounded-full text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider">
                                    Pratinjau Dokumen BPB
                                </span>
                                <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 mt-1 font-mono">
                                    {activePurchaseToPrint.invoice_number}
                                </h3>
                            </div>
                            <button 
                                onClick={() => setPrintModalOpen(false)}
                                className="p-2.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 dark:hover:text-gray-200 rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"></path></svg>
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="w-full h-[62vh] bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#151E24] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 shadow-inner">
                            <iframe 
                                id="pdf-print-iframe"
                                src={route('purchases.print', activePurchaseToPrint.id)} 
                                className="w-full h-full border-0"
                                title="Pratinjau PDF BPB"
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-4 mt-3 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-mono">
                                No. Sistem: <span className="font-bold">{activePurchaseToPrint.internal_receipt_number}</span>
                            </p>
                            <div className="flex gap-3">
                                <button 
                                    onClick={() => setPrintModalOpen(false)}
                                    className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                >
                                    Tutup
                                </button>

                                <button 
                                    onClick={() => {
                                        const iframe = document.getElementById('pdf-print-iframe');
                                        if (iframe && iframe.contentWindow) {
                                            iframe.contentWindow.print();
                                        }
                                    }}
                                    className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity shadow-lg shadow-[#48CFA4]/20 flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
                                >
                                    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M17 17h2a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h6a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2zm8-12V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4h10z"></path></svg>
                                    Cetak Dokumen
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

=========================================================
FILE: resources/js/Pages/Purchases/Trash.jsx
