import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function Trash({ purchases }) {
    const [confirmingRestore, setConfirmingRestore] = useState(false);
    const [confirmingForceDelete, setConfirmingForceDelete] = useState(false);
    const [selectedPurchase, setSelectedPurchase] = useState(null);

    const formatRupiah = (number) => {
        return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', { style: 'currency', currency: 'IDR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(number);
    };

    const confirmRestore = (purchase) => {
        setSelectedPurchase(purchase);
        setConfirmingRestore(true);
    };

    const restorePurchase = () => {
        if (selectedPurchase) {
            router.post(route('purchases.restore', selectedPurchase.id), {}, {
                onSuccess: () => {
                    setConfirmingRestore(false);
                    setSelectedPurchase(null);
                },
            });
        }
    };

    const confirmForceDelete = (purchase) => {
        setSelectedPurchase(purchase);
        setConfirmingForceDelete(true);
    };

    const forceDeletePurchase = () => {
        if (selectedPurchase) {
            router.delete(route('purchases.force-delete', selectedPurchase.id), {
                onSuccess: () => {
                    setConfirmingForceDelete(false);
                    setSelectedPurchase(null);
                },
            });
        }
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Pembelian & Barang Masuk">
            <Head title="Keranjang Sampah Pembelian" />

            {/* HEADER */}
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4 mb-6">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span>🗑️</span> Keranjang Sampah (Recycle Bin)
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">Data faktur yang dihapus akan masuk ke sini. Pulihkan atau hapus permanen.</p>
                </div>

                <Link 
                    href={route('purchases.index')}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700/60 font-bold text-xs tracking-wide cursor-pointer shadow-sm"
                >
                    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M10 19l-7-7m0 0l7-7m-7 7h18"></path>
                    </svg>
                    <span>Kembali ke Data Aktif</span>
                </Link>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors duration-300">
                <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                    <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-300 uppercase bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 tracking-wider">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">No. Faktur PBF</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Waktu Dihapus</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Supplier</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-right">Total Nilai (Rp)</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Aksi Manajemen</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        {!purchases || purchases.length === 0 ? (
                            <tr>
                                <td colSpan="5" className="px-6 py-16 text-center text-gray-400">Keranjang sampah kosong.</td>
                            </tr>
                        ) : (
                            purchases.map((purchase) => (
                                <tr key={purchase.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 dark:border-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24] transition-colors">
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-bold text-[#48CFA4]">{purchase.invoice_number}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 text-xs font-mono">{purchase.deleted_at}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{purchase.supplier?.name || '-'}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-right font-mono font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200">
                                        {formatRupiah(purchase.total_amount)}
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                                            {/* Tombol Pulihkan (Restore) dengan Ikon SVG */}
                                            <button 
                                                onClick={() => confirmRestore(purchase)}
                                                title="Pulihkan Faktur"
                                                className="p-2 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 dark:text-emerald-400 rounded-xl hover:bg-emerald-500 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                            >
                                                <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 2A8.001 8.001 0 004.582 9m0 0H9m11 11v-5h-.581m0 0a8.003 8.003 0 01-15.357-2m15.357 2H15"></path>
                                                </svg>
                                            </button>

                                            {/* Tombol Hapus Permanen (Force Delete) dengan Ikon SVG */}
                                            <button 
                                                onClick={() => confirmForceDelete(purchase)}
                                                title="Hapus Permanen"
                                                className="p-2 bg-red-50 text-red-600 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 rounded-xl hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                            >
                                                <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
                                                </svg>
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            ))
                        )}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            {/* MODAL KONFIRMASI PULIHKAN */}
            {confirmingRestore && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full mx-4 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 text-center">
                        <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 flex-shrink-0">
                            <svg className="w-8 h-8 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 2A8.001 8.001 0 004.582 9m0 0H9m11 11v-5h-.581m0 0a8.003 8.003 0 01-15.357-2m15.357 2H15"></path>
                            </svg>
                        </div>

                        <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 mb-2">Pulihkan Faktur Pembelian?</h3>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-6">
                            Faktur <strong className="text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{selectedPurchase?.invoice_number}</strong> akan dikembalikan ke data aktif sistem.
                        </p>
                        <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
                            <button 
                                onClick={() => setConfirmingRestore(false)}
                                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Batal
                            </button>
                            <button 
                                onClick={restorePurchase}
                                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-emerald-700 transition-colors shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/30 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Ya, Pulihkan
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* MODAL KONFIRMASI HAPUS PERMANEN */}
            {confirmingForceDelete && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full mx-4 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 text-center">
                        <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/30 text-red-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 flex-shrink-0">
                            <svg className="w-8 h-8 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
                            </svg>
                        </div>

                        <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 mb-2">Hapus Permanen Faktur?</h3>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-6">
                            Faktur <strong className="text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{selectedPurchase?.invoice_number}</strong> akan dimusnahkan secara permanen dari database dan tidak dapat dikembalikan lagi.
                        </p>
                        <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
                            <button 
                                onClick={() => setConfirmingForceDelete(false)}
                                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Batal
                            </button>
                            <button 
                                onClick={forceDeletePurchase}
                                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors shadow-lg shadow-red-600/30 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Ya, Hapus Permanen
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';

export default function Create({ locationTypes }) {
    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors } = useForm({
        location_type_id: '',
        code: '',
        name: '',
        description: '',
    });

    const handleTypeChange = (e) => {
        const typeId = e.target.value;
        setData('location_type_id', typeId);

        const selected = locationTypes.find(t => t.id == typeId);
        if (selected) {
            const randomNum = Math.floor(100 + Math.random() * 900);
            setData(prev => ({
                ...prev,
                location_type_id: typeId,
                code: `${selected.prefix_code}-${randomNum}`,
                name: `${selected.name} - `
            }));
        }
    };

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        post(route('racks.store'));
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Tambah Lokasi Rak Baru">
            <Head title="Tambah Rak Penyimpanan" />

            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 p-8 max-w-2xl mx-auto transition-colors duration-300">
                <div className="mb-8">
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Informasi Lokasi</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Pilih dari Data Referensi terpusat untuk menjaga konsistensi kode dan penamaan.</p>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-6">
                    
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Pilih Tipe Titik Simpan (Dari Data Referensi)</label>
                        <select 
                            value={data.location_type_id} 
                            onChange={handleTypeChange}
                            required
                            className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors"
                        >
                            <option value="">-- Pilih Tipe Lokasi --</option>
                            {locationTypes.map((type) => (
                                <option key={type.id} value={type.id}>
                                    {type.name} ({type.prefix_code}) - Peruntukan: {type.intended_for || 'Umum'}
                                </option>
                            ))}
                        </select>
                        <InputError message={errors.location_type_id} className="mt-2" />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Kode Rak / Etalase (Auto-Generated)</label>
                        <input 
                            type="text" 
                            value={data.code} 
                            onChange={e => setData('code', e.target.value)} 
                            required 
                            className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors uppercase font-mono font-bold text-[#48CFA4]" 
                        />
                        <InputError message={errors.code} className="mt-2" />
                    </div>
                    
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nama Detail Lokasi</label>
                        <input 
                            type="text" 
                            value={data.name} 
                            onChange={e => setData('name', e.target.value)} 
                            required 
                            placeholder="Misal: Etalase Depan Sisi Kanan"
                            className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                        />
                        <InputError message={errors.name} className="mt-2" />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Keterangan Tambahan (Opsional)</label>
                        <textarea 
                            value={data.description} 
                            onChange={e => setData('description', e.target.value)} 
                            rows="3"
                            placeholder="Detail posisi rak..."
                            className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                        ></textarea>
                        <InputError message={errors.description} className="mt-2" />
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-6 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 mt-8">
                        <Link href={route('racks.index')} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors">
                            Batal
                        </Link>
                        <button type="submit" disabled={processing} className="px-6 py-3 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-[#3bb890] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50">
                            {processing ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan Lokasi'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';

export default function Edit({ rackEdit, locationTypes }) {
    const { data, setData, put, processing, errors } = useForm({
        location_type_id: rackEdit.location_type_id || '',
        code: rackEdit.code || '',
        name: rackEdit.name || '',
        description: rackEdit.description || '',
        is_active: rackEdit.is_active,
    });

    const handleTypeChange = (e) => {
        const typeId = e.target.value;
        const selectedType = locationTypes.find(t => t.id == typeId);

        if (selectedType) {
            const parts = data.code.split('-');
            const uniqueNumber = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1] : data.code;
            
            setData(prev => ({
                ...prev,
                location_type_id: typeId,
                code: `${selectedType.prefix_code}-${uniqueNumber}`
            }));
        } else {
            setData('location_type_id', typeId);
        }
    };

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        put(route('racks.update', rackEdit.id));
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Perbarui Lokasi Rak">
            <Head title="Edit Rak Penyimpanan" />

            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 p-8 max-w-2xl mx-auto transition-colors duration-300">
                <div className="mb-8">
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Informasi Lokasi</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Pindahkan rak ke tipe lokasi lain atau ubah status keaktifannya.</p>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-6">
                    <div className="p-4 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 mb-6">
                        <div>
                            <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Status Penggunaan Rak</h4>
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Matikan jika rak ini sedang direnovasi atau tidak digunakan.</p>
                        </div>
                        <label className="relative inline-flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                            <input type="checkbox" checked={data.is_active} onChange={e => setData('is_active', e.target.checked)} className="sr-only peer" />
                            <div className="w-11 h-6 bg-gray-200 peer-focus:outline-none rounded-full peer dark:bg-gray-700 peer-checked:after:translate-x-full peer-checked:after:border-white after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-[2px] after:left-[2px] after:bg-white after:border-gray-300 after:border after:rounded-full after:h-5 after:w-5 after:transition-all peer-checked:bg-[#48CFA4]"></div>
                        </label>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Tipe Titik Simpan (Referensi)</label>
                        <select 
                            value={data.location_type_id} 
                            onChange={handleTypeChange}
                            required
                            className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors"
                        >
                            <option value="">-- Pilih Tipe Lokasi --</option>
                            {locationTypes.map((type) => (
                                <option key={type.id} value={type.id}>
                                    {type.name} ({type.prefix_code}) - Peruntukan: {type.intended_for || 'Umum'}
                                </option>
                            ))}
                        </select>
                        <InputError message={errors.location_type_id} className="mt-2" />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Kode Rak / Etalase</label>
                        <input 
                            type="text" 
                            value={data.code} 
                            onChange={e => setData('code', e.target.value)} 
                            required 
                            className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#11181C] border-transparent rounded-xl text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors uppercase font-mono font-bold text-[#48CFA4]" 
                        />
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">Kode otomatis menyesuaikan jika Tipe Lokasi diubah.</p>
                        <InputError message={errors.code} className="mt-2" />
                    </div>
                    
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nama Detail Lokasi</label>
                        <input 
                            type="text" 
                            value={data.name} 
                            onChange={e => setData('name', e.target.value)} 
                            required 
                            className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                        />
                        <InputError message={errors.name} className="mt-2" />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Keterangan Tambahan</label>
                        <textarea 
                            value={data.description} 
                            onChange={e => setData('description', e.target.value)} 
                            rows="3"
                            className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                        ></textarea>
                        <InputError message={errors.description} className="mt-2" />
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-6 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 mt-8">
                        <Link href={route('racks.index')} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors">
                            Batal
                        </Link>
                        <button type="submit" disabled={processing} className="px-6 py-3 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-[#3bb890] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50">
                            {processing ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan Perubahan'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import ConfirmModal from '@/Components/ConfirmModal';
import { useState } from 'react';

const EditIcon = () => (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z"></path>
    </svg>
);

const DeleteIcon = () => (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
    </svg>
);

export default function Index({ racks }) {
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [rackToDelete, setRackToDelete] = useState(null);
    const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);

    const confirmDelete = (rack) => {
        setRackToDelete(rack);
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    const executeDelete = () => {
        setIsDeleting(true);
        router.delete(route('racks.destroy', rackToDelete.id), {
            preserveScroll: true,
            onSuccess: () => {
                setIsDeleting(false);
                setIsModalOpen(false);
            },
            onError: () => setIsDeleting(false),
        });
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Lokasi Rak Penyimpanan">
            <Head title="Data Rak" />

            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Daftar Etalase & Gudang</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Kelola titik lokasi penyimpanan fisik obat di apotek Anda.</p>
                </div>
                <PrimaryButton as="link" href={route('racks.create')}>
                    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M12 4v16m8-8H4"></path></svg>
                    Tambah Lokasi
                </PrimaryButton>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors duration-300">
                <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                    <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 tracking-wider">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Kode Lokasi</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Nama Rak / Etalase</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Keterangan</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Status</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Aksi</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        {racks.length === 0 ? (
                            <tr>
                                <td colSpan="5" className="px-6 py-16 text-center text-gray-400">Belum ada data rak penyimpanan.</td>
                            </tr>
                        ) : (
                            racks.map((rack) => (
                                <tr key={rack.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 dark:border-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24] transition-colors">
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">{rack.code}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-bold text-blue-600 dark:text-[#6FD8E8]">{rack.name}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">{rack.description || '-'}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                        {rack.is_active ? 
                                            <span className="bg-[#EAF5F0] dark:bg-[#253939] text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#67E2B4] text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">Aktif</span> : 
                                            <span className="bg-red-50 text-red-600 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">Nonaktif</span>
                                        }
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                        <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-3">
                                            <Link href={route('racks.edit', rack.id)} className="p-2 text-[#48CFA4] bg-[#EAF5F0] hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#48CFA4] dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200">
                                                <EditIcon />
                                            </Link>
                                            <button onClick={() => confirmDelete(rack)} className="p-2 text-red-500 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-500 dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200">
                                                <DeleteIcon />
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            ))
                        )}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            <ConfirmModal 
                isOpen={isModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={executeDelete}
                isProcessing={isDeleting}
                title="Hapus Lokasi Rak?"
                message={
                    <>
                        Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus lokasi <span className="font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200">{rackToDelete?.name}</span>? Tindakan ini tidak dapat dibatalkan. Pastikan tidak ada stok obat yang terkait dengan lokasi ini.
                    </>
                }
            />
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, router, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import ConfirmModal from '@/Components/ConfirmModal';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';

const EditIcon = () => (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z"></path>
    </svg>
);      

const DeleteIcon = () => (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
    </svg>
);

export default function Index({ currentTab, data }) {
    const tabs = [
        { id: 'tipe-lokasi', label: 'Tipe Lokasi & Rak' },
        { id: 'tipe-supplier', label: 'Tipe Supplier' },
        { id: 'golongan-obat', label: 'Golongan Obat' },
    ];

    const handleTabChange = (tabId) => {
        router.get(route('references.index'), { tab: tabId }, { preserveState: true, replace: true });
    };

    // --- STATE HAPUS GLOBAL ---
    const [itemToDelete, setItemToDelete] = useState(null);
    const [deleteType, setDeleteType] = useState(''); // 'location', 'supplier', atau 'category'
    const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);

    // --- STATE & FORM: LOKASI ---
    const [isLocationModalOpen, setIsLocationModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [editingLocation, setEditingLocation] = useState(null);
    const locationForm = useForm({ name: '', prefix_code: '', intended_for: '', description: '' });

    const openLocationModal = (item = null) => {
        setEditingLocation(item);
        if (item) {
            locationForm.setData({ name: item.name, prefix_code: item.prefix_code, intended_for: item.intended_for || '', description: item.description || '' });
        } else {
            locationForm.reset();
        }
        setIsLocationModalOpen(true);
    };

    const submitLocation = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const options = {
            onSuccess: () => {
                locationForm.reset();
                setIsLocationModalOpen(false);
                setEditingLocation(null);
            }
        };
        if (editingLocation) {
            locationForm.put(route('references.location-types.update', editingLocation.id), options);
        } else {
            locationForm.post(route('references.location-types.store'), options);
        }
    };

    // --- STATE & FORM: SUPPLIER ---
    const [isSupplierModalOpen, setIsSupplierModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [editingSupplierType, setEditingSupplierType] = useState(null);
    const supplierForm = useForm({ name: '', prefix_code: '', description: '' });

    const openSupplierModal = (item = null) => {
        setEditingSupplierType(item);
        if (item) {
            supplierForm.setData({ name: item.name, prefix_code: item.prefix_code, description: item.description || '' });
        } else {
            supplierForm.reset();
        }
        setIsSupplierModalOpen(true);
    };

    const submitSupplierType = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const options = {
            onSuccess: () => {
                supplierForm.reset();
                setIsSupplierModalOpen(false);
                setEditingSupplierType(null);
            }
        };
        if (editingSupplierType) {
            supplierForm.put(route('references.supplier-types.update', editingSupplierType.id), options);
        } else {
            supplierForm.post(route('references.supplier-types.store'), options);
        }
    };

    // --- STATE & FORM: GOLONGAN OBAT ---
    const [isCategoryModalOpen, setIsCategoryModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [editingCategory, setEditingCategory] = useState(null);
    const categoryForm = useForm({ name: '', description: '' });

    const openCategoryModal = (item = null) => {
        setEditingCategory(item);
        if (item) {
            categoryForm.setData({ name: item.name, description: item.description || '' });
        } else {
            categoryForm.reset();
        }
        setIsCategoryModalOpen(true);
    };

    const submitCategory = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const options = {
            onSuccess: () => {
                categoryForm.reset();
                setIsCategoryModalOpen(false);
                setEditingCategory(null);
            }
        };
        if (editingCategory) {
            categoryForm.put(route('references.medicine-categories.update', editingCategory.id), options);
        } else {
            categoryForm.post(route('references.medicine-categories.store'), options);
        }
    };

    // --- FUNGSI HAPUS GLOBAL ---
    const confirmDelete = (item, type) => {
        setItemToDelete(item);
        setDeleteType(type);
        setIsDeleteModalOpen(true);
    };

    const executeDelete = () => {
        let routeName;
        if (deleteType === 'location') {
            routeName = route('references.location-types.destroy', itemToDelete.id);
        } else if (deleteType === 'supplier') {
            routeName = route('references.supplier-types.destroy', itemToDelete.id);
        } else if (deleteType === 'category') {
            routeName = route('references.medicine-categories.destroy', itemToDelete.id);
        }

        router.delete(routeName, {
            preserveScroll: true,
            onFinish: () => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)
        });
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Data Referensi Sistem">
            <Head title="Data Referensi" />

            <div className="mb-6">
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Parameter & Referensi</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Kelola master data pendukung yang akan digunakan pada seluruh dropdown fitur utama.</p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex overflow-x-auto gap-2 mb-6 pb-2 custom-scrollbar">
                {tabs.map((tab) => (
                    <button
                        key={tab.id}
                        onClick={() => handleTabChange(tab.id)}
                        className={`px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full text-sm font-bold whitespace-nowrap transition-all duration-200 
                            ${currentTab === tab.id 
                                ? 'bg-[#48CFA4] text-white shadow-sm dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:border dark:border-[#48CFA4]/30' 
                                : 'bg-white text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 dark:bg-[#1E272E] dark:text-gray-400 dark:border-gray-800 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24]'
                            }`}
                    >
                        {tab.label}
                    </button>
                ))}
            </div>

            {/* TAB: LOKASI */}
            {currentTab === 'tipe-lokasi' && (
                <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors">
                    <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 flex justify-between items-center">
                        <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 uppercase tracking-wide text-sm">Daftar Tipe Titik Simpan</h4>
                        <button onClick={() => openLocationModal()} className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#EAF5F0] text-[#246A55] dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] rounded-lg font-bold text-xs hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white transition-colors">
                            + Tambah Tipe Baru
                        </button>
                    </div>
                    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                            <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                                <tr>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Prefix Kode</th>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Nama Tipe</th>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Peruntukan</th>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Aksi</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                {!data.locationTypes || data.locationTypes.length === 0 ? (
                                    <tr><td colSpan="4" className="px-6 py-16 text-center text-gray-400">Belum ada data referensi.</td></tr>
                                ) : (
                                    data.locationTypes.map((type) => (
                                        <tr key={type.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 dark:border-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24]">
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-[#48CFA4]">{type.prefix_code}</td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{type.name}</td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{type.intended_for || 'Umum'}</td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                                <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-3">
                                                    <button onClick={() => openLocationModal(type)} className="p-2 text-[#48CFA4] bg-[#EAF5F0] hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#48CFA4] dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200"><EditIcon /></button>
                                                    <button onClick={() => confirmDelete(type, 'location')} className="p-2 text-red-500 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-500 dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200"><DeleteIcon /></button>
                                                </div>
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    ))
                                )}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* TAB: SUPPLIER */}
            {currentTab === 'tipe-supplier' && (
                <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors">
                    <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 flex justify-between items-center">
                        <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 uppercase tracking-wide text-sm">Daftar Tipe Pemasok</h4>
                        <button onClick={() => openSupplierModal()} className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#EAF5F0] text-[#246A55] dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] rounded-lg font-bold text-xs hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white transition-colors">
                            + Tambah Tipe Pemasok
                        </button>
                    </div>
                    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                            <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                                <tr>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Prefix Kode</th>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Kategori Pemasok</th>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Keterangan</th>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Aksi</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                {!data.supplierTypes || data.supplierTypes.length === 0 ? (
                                    <tr><td colSpan="4" className="px-6 py-16 text-center text-gray-400">Belum ada tipe pemasok terdaftar.</td></tr>
                                ) : (
                                    data.supplierTypes.map((type) => (
                                        <tr key={type.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 dark:border-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24]">
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-blue-500">{type.prefix_code}</td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{type.name}</td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{type.description || '-'}</td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                                <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-3">
                                                    <button onClick={() => openSupplierModal(type)} className="p-2 text-[#48CFA4] bg-[#EAF5F0] hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#48CFA4] dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200"><EditIcon /></button>
                                                    <button onClick={() => confirmDelete(type, 'supplier')} className="p-2 text-red-500 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-500 dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200"><DeleteIcon /></button>
                                                </div>
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    ))
                                )}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* TAB: GOLONGAN OBAT */}
            {currentTab === 'golongan-obat' && (
                <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors">
                    <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 flex justify-between items-center">
                        <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 uppercase tracking-wide text-sm">Daftar Golongan Obat</h4>
                        <button onClick={() => openCategoryModal()} className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#EAF5F0] text-[#246A55] dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] rounded-lg font-bold text-xs hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white transition-colors">
                            + Tambah Golongan Obat
                        </button>
                    </div>
                    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                            <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                                <tr>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Nama Golongan</th>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Keterangan</th>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Status</th>
                                    <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Aksi</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                {!data.medicineCategories || data.medicineCategories.length === 0 ? (
                                    <tr><td colSpan="4" className="px-6 py-16 text-center text-gray-400">Belum ada golongan obat terdaftar.</td></tr>
                                ) : (
                                    data.medicineCategories.map((cat) => (
                                        <tr key={cat.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 dark:border-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24]">
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{cat.name}</td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{cat.description || '-'}</td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                                <span className="bg-[#EAF5F0] dark:bg-[#253939] text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#67E2B4] text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full">Aktif</span>
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                                <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-3">
                                                    <button onClick={() => openCategoryModal(cat)} className="p-2 text-[#48CFA4] bg-[#EAF5F0] hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#48CFA4] dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200"><EditIcon /></button>
                                                    <button onClick={() => confirmDelete(cat, 'category')} className="p-2 text-red-500 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-500 dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200"><DeleteIcon /></button>
                                                </div>
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    ))
                                )}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- MODAL LOKASI --- */}
            {isLocationModalOpen && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
                    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-gray-900/40 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setIsLocationModalOpen(false)}></div>
                    <div className="relative bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-3xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-md p-6 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white mb-4">{editingLocation ? 'Edit Tipe Lokasi' : 'Tambah Tipe Lokasi Baru'}</h3>
                        <form onSubmit={submitLocation} className="space-y-4">
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Prefix Kode</label>
                                <input type="text" value={locationForm.data.prefix_code} onChange={e => locationForm.setData('prefix_code', e.target.value)} placeholder="Misal: KKS" className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-lg text-sm dark:text-white uppercase font-bold" required />
                                <InputError message={locationForm.errors.prefix_code} />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Nama Tipe</label>
                                <input type="text" value={locationForm.data.name} onChange={e => locationForm.setData('name', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-lg text-sm dark:text-white" />
                                <InputError message={locationForm.errors.name} />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Peruntukan</label>
                                <input type="text" value={locationForm.data.intended_for} onChange={e => locationForm.setData('intended_for', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-lg text-sm dark:text-white" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-4">
                                <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsLocationModalOpen(false)} className="flex-1 px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#151E24] rounded-lg font-bold text-sm">Batal</button>
                                <button type="submit" disabled={locationForm.processing} className="flex-1 px-4 py-2 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white rounded-lg font-bold text-sm">Simpan</button>
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- MODAL SUPPLIER --- */}
            {isSupplierModalOpen && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
                    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-gray-900/40 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setIsSupplierModalOpen(false)}></div>
                    <div className="relative bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-3xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-md p-6 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white mb-4">{editingSupplierType ? 'Edit Tipe Pemasok' : 'Tambah Tipe Pemasok Baru'}</h3>
                        <form onSubmit={submitSupplierType} className="space-y-4">
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Prefix Kode</label>
                                <input type="text" value={supplierForm.data.prefix_code} onChange={e => supplierForm.setData('prefix_code', e.target.value)} placeholder="Misal: PBF" className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-lg text-sm dark:text-white uppercase font-bold" required />
                                <InputError message={supplierForm.errors.prefix_code} />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Kategori Pemasok</label>
                                <input type="text" value={supplierForm.data.name} onChange={e => supplierForm.setData('name', e.target.value)} placeholder="Misal: Pedagang Besar Farmasi" required className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-lg text-sm dark:text-white" />
                                <InputError message={supplierForm.errors.name} />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Keterangan Tambahan</label>
                                <textarea value={supplierForm.data.description} onChange={e => supplierForm.setData('description', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-lg text-sm dark:text-white"></textarea>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-4">
                                <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsSupplierModalOpen(false)} className="flex-1 px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#151E24] rounded-lg font-bold text-sm">Batal</button>
                                <button type="submit" disabled={supplierForm.processing} className="flex-1 px-4 py-2 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white rounded-lg font-bold text-sm">Simpan</button>
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- MODAL GOLONGAN OBAT --- */}
            {isCategoryModalOpen && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
                    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-gray-900/40 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setIsCategoryModalOpen(false)}></div>
                    <div className="relative bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-3xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-md p-6 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white mb-4">{editingCategory ? 'Edit Golongan Obat' : 'Tambah Golongan Baru'}</h3>
                        <form onSubmit={submitCategory} className="space-y-4">
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Nama Golongan Obat</label>
                                <input type="text" value={categoryForm.data.name} onChange={e => categoryForm.setData('name', e.target.value)} placeholder="Misal: Obat Bebas, Obat Keras" className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-lg text-sm dark:text-white font-bold" required />
                                <InputError message={categoryForm.errors.name} />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Keterangan (Opsional)</label>
                                <textarea value={categoryForm.data.description} onChange={e => categoryForm.setData('description', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-lg text-sm dark:text-white"></textarea>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-4">
                                <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsCategoryModalOpen(false)} className="flex-1 px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#151E24] rounded-lg font-bold text-sm">Batal</button>
                                <button type="submit" disabled={categoryForm.processing} className="flex-1 px-4 py-2 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white rounded-lg font-bold text-sm">Simpan</button>
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- MODAL HAPUS GLOBAL --- */}
            <ConfirmModal 
                isOpen={isDeleteModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={executeDelete}
                title="Hapus Parameter?"
                message={<>Data referensi <span className="font-bold">{itemToDelete?.name}</span> akan dihapus permanen.</>}
            />
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

FILE: resources/js/Pages/Reports/Components/ExportModal.jsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export default function ExportModal({ 
    isOpen, 
    onClose, 
    selectedReportType, 
    setSelectedReportType, 
    startDate, 
    setStartDate, 
    endDate, 
    setEndDate, 
    exportFormat, 
    setExportFormat, 
    handleExecuteExport,
    setPresetDate
}) {
    const [activePreset, setActivePreset] = useState('bulan_ini');

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) setActivePreset('bulan_ini');
    }, [isOpen]);

    const handlePresetClick = (preset) => {
        setActivePreset(preset);
        if (preset !== 'kustom') {
            setPresetDate(preset);
        }
    };

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-gray-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-[24px] w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700/60 flex flex-col overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">

                <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24]/50">
                    <h3 className="font-extrabold text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-white">Pengaturan Ekspor</h3>
                    <button onClick={onClose} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-md bg-white dark:bg-[#2A343D] border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500 hover:text-red-500 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                        ✕
                    </button>
                </div>

                <div className="p-6 space-y-5">
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2">Pilih Jenis Laporan</label>
                        <select 
                            value={selectedReportType} 
                            onChange={(e) => setSelectedReportType(e.target.value)}
                            className="w-full px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold rounded-md border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 focus:ring-[#48CFA4] focus:border-[#48CFA4] cursor-pointer"
                        >
                            <option value="sales">Laporan Penjualan (Omzet & Struk)</option>
                            <option value="purchase">Laporan Pembelian & Supplier</option>
                            <option value="profit">Laporan Laba Rugi & HPP</option>
                            <option value="stock">Laporan Stok Kritis & Kedaluwarsa</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>

                    {/* Periode Data */}
                    {selectedReportType !== 'stock' && (
                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2">Periode Data</label>

                            <div className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-1.5">
                                {[
                                    { id: 'hari_ini', label: 'Hari Ini' },
                                    { id: 'minggu_ini', label: 'Minggu' },
                                    { id: 'bulan_ini', label: 'Bulan' },
                                    { id: 'tahun_ini', label: 'Tahun' },
                                    { id: 'kustom', label: 'Kustom' }
                                ].map((preset) => {
                                    const isActive = activePreset === preset.id;
                                    return (
                                        <button 
                                            key={preset.id}
                                            onClick={() => handlePresetClick(preset.id)}
                                            className={`py-2 px-1 text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold rounded-md border transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                                                isActive 
                                                ? 'border-[#48CFA4]/50 bg-[#EAF5F0] dark:bg-[#1C332B] text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#48CFA4] shadow-sm' 
                                                : 'border-transparent bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#2A343D] text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-[#32404A]'
                                            }`}
                                        >
                                            {preset.label}
                                        </button>
                                    );
                                })}
                            </div>

                            {activePreset === 'kustom' && (
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-3 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-300">
                                    <div className="relative w-full">
                                        <input 
                                            type="date" 
                                            value={startDate} 
                                            onChange={e => setStartDate(e.target.value)} 
                                            className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-md border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-white dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 focus:ring-[#48CFA4] focus:border-[#48CFA4] dark:[color-scheme:dark] cursor-pointer" 
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                    <span className="text-gray-400 text-xs font-bold">s/d</span>
                                    <div className="relative w-full">
                                        <input 
                                            type="date" 
                                            value={endDate} 
                                            onChange={e => setEndDate(e.target.value)} 
                                            className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-md border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-white dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 focus:ring-[#48CFA4] focus:border-[#48CFA4] dark:[color-scheme:dark] cursor-pointer" 
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2">Format Berkas Ekspor</label>
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                            {[
                                { id: 'pdf', label: 'PDF / Cetak A4', icon: '📄' },
                                { id: 'excel', label: 'Excel (.xlsx)', icon: '📊' }
                            ].map(fmt => (
                                <button
                                    key={fmt.id}
                                    onClick={() => setExportFormat(fmt.id)}
                                    className={`p-4 rounded-md border text-center font-bold text-sm transition-all flex flex-col items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer ${
                                        exportFormat === fmt.id 
                                        ? 'border-[#48CFA4] bg-[#EAF5F0]/50 dark:bg-[#1C332B]/60 text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#48CFA4] shadow-sm' 
                                        : 'border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700/80 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#2A343D] hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-[#32404A]'
                                    }`}
                                >
                                    <span className="text-2xl">{fmt.icon}</span>
                                    {fmt.label}
                                </button>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-6 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24]/50 flex justify-end gap-3">
                    <button 
                        onClick={onClose} 
                        className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-md text-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all duration-200 bg-white text-gray-700 border border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50 shadow-sm dark:bg-transparent dark:text-gray-300 dark:hover:bg-[#2A343D] dark:border dark:border-[#30413B] cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        Batal
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={handleExecuteExport} 
                        className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-md text-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all duration-200 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white hover:bg-[#3bb890] shadow-sm dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#2d4747] dark:border dark:border-[#30413B] dark:shadow-none cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M4 16v1a3 3 0 003 3h10a3 3 0 003-3v-1m-4-4l-4 4m0 0l-4-4m4 4V4"></path></svg>
                        Unduh / Ekspor
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: resources/js/Pages/Reports/Components/ReportCalendar.jsx

import React from 'react';

const getDaysInMonth = (year, month) => new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();
const getFirstDayOfMonth = (year, month) => new Date(year, month, 1).getDay();

export default function ReportCalendar({ 
    calendarMonth, 
    calendarData, 
    selectedDate, 
    setSelectedDate, 
    monthSales, 
    formatRupiah 
}) {
    const calDate = calendarMonth ? new Date(calendarMonth) : new Date();
    const currentYear = isNaN(calDate.getFullYear()) ? new Date().getFullYear() : calDate.getFullYear();
    const currentMonth = isNaN(calDate.getMonth()) ? new Date().getMonth() : calDate.getMonth();
    const daysInMonth = getDaysInMonth(currentYear, currentMonth);
    const firstDay = getFirstDayOfMonth(currentYear, currentMonth);
    const monthName = calDate.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });

    const selectedDateTransactions = selectedDate && monthSales
        ? monthSales.filter(sale => sale.sale_date.startsWith(selectedDate))
        : [];

    return (
        <div className="p-6 relative">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-gray-900 dark:text-white">Bulan: <span className="text-[#48CFA4]">{monthName}</span></h3>
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-2 md:gap-3 text-center">
                {['Min', 'Sen', 'Sel', 'Rab', 'Kam', 'Jum', 'Sab'].map(day => (
                    <div key={day} className="text-xs font-extrabold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider py-2">{day}</div>
                ))}
                {Array.from({ length: firstDay }).map((_, i) => (
                    <div key={`empty-${i}`} className="h-24 md:h-28 rounded-2xl bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24]/30"></div>
                ))}
                {Array.from({ length: daysInMonth }).map((_, i) => {
                    const dayNum = i + 1;
                    const dateStr = `${currentYear}-${String(currentMonth + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(dayNum).padStart(2, '0')}`;
                    const hasData = calendarData && calendarData[dateStr];

                    return (
                        <button 
                            key={dayNum} 
                            disabled={!hasData}
                            onClick={() => hasData && setSelectedDate(dateStr)}
                            className={`h-24 md:h-28 rounded-2xl border p-2 md:p-3 flex flex-col justify-between transition-all w-full text-left
                                ${hasData 
                                    ? 'cursor-pointer hover:scale-[1.03] hover:shadow-lg hover:border-[#48CFA4] border-[#48CFA4]/40 bg-[#EAF5F0]/40 dark:bg-[#253939]/40' 
                                    : 'cursor-default border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E]'
                                }`}
                        >
                            <div className="flex justify-between items-start w-full">
                                <span className={`text-sm font-extrabold ${hasData ? 'text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#67E2B4]' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>{dayNum}</span>
                                {hasData && <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#48CFA4]"></span>}
                            </div>
                            {hasData && (
                                <div className="mt-auto w-full">
                                    <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 dark:text-[#48CFA4] font-bold truncate mb-0.5">{hasData.transactions} Struk</div>
                                    <div className="text-xs font-extrabold text-gray-900 dark:text-white truncate">{formatRupiah(hasData.revenue)}</div>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </button>
                    );
                })}
            </div>

            {/* MODAL TRANSAKSI KALENDER */}
            {selectedDate && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-gray-900/40 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-[24px] w-full max-w-2xl shadow-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 flex flex-col max-h-[80vh] animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
                        <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24]/50 rounded-t-[24px]">
                            <div>
                                <h3 className="font-extrabold text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-white">Rincian Tanggal <span className="text-[#48CFA4]">{selectedDate}</span></h3>
                            </div>
                            <button onClick={() => setSelectedDate(null)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white dark:bg-[#2A343D] border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500 hover:text-red-500 cursor-pointer">
                                ✕
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        <div className="overflow-y-auto p-6 custom-scrollbar space-y-3">
                            {selectedDateTransactions.map(tx => (
                                <div key={tx.id} className="flex justify-between items-center p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 bg-white dark:bg-[#151E24]">
                                    <div>
                                        <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white font-mono">{tx.invoice_number}</h4>
                                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-medium">Kasir: {tx.user?.name || '-'}</p>
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="text-right">
                                        <div className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 dark:text-[#48CFA4]">{formatRupiah(tx.total_amount)}</div>
                                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">{tx.payment_method}</span>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: resources/js/Pages/Reports/Components/ReportStockLedger.jsx
