import React from 'react';

export default function ReportStockLedger({ stockMutations = [] }) {
    const formatDate = (dateString) => {
        if (!dateString) return '-';
        return new Date(dateString).toLocaleString('id-ID', {
            day: '2-digit',
            month: 'short',
            year: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit'
        });
    };

    return (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 uppercase bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24]/50 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                    <tr>
                        <th className="px-6 py-3.5">Waktu Transaksi</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-3.5">Nama Obat & SKU</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-3.5 text-center">Tipe Mutasi</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-3.5 text-center">Jumlah</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-3.5">No. Referensi / Struk</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-3.5">Keterangan Sumber</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100 dark:divide-gray-800/60 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">
                    {stockMutations.length > 0 ? (
                        stockMutations.map((item, index) => (
                            <tr key={index} className="hover:bg-gray-50/50 dark:hover:bg-gray-800/20 transition-colors">
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                                    {formatDate(item.date)}
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4">
                                    <div className="font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{item.medicine_name}</div>
                                    <div className="text-xs font-mono text-gray-400">{item.sku}</div>
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                    {item.type === 'IN' ? (
                                        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-900/40 dark:text-emerald-400 text-[11px] font-black rounded-lg inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                                            ↓ IN (Masuk)
                                        </span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-900/40 dark:text-amber-400 text-[11px] font-black rounded-lg inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                                            ↑ OUT (Keluar)
                                        </span>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center font-mono font-bold text-sm">
                                    <span className={item.type === 'IN' ? 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400' : 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400'}>
                                        {item.type === 'IN' ? `+${item.quantity}` : `-${item.quantity}`}
                                    </span>
                                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 ml-1 font-normal">{item.uom}</span>
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">
                                    {item.reference}
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                                    {item.description}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        ))
                    ) : (
                        <tr>
                            <td colSpan="6" className="px-6 py-12 text-center text-gray-400 text-sm font-bold">
                                Belum ada riwayat mutasi stok pada periode ini.
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    )}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    );
}

import React from 'react';

export default function ReportTable({ salesList, formatRupiah }) {
    return (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                <thead className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-500 text-xs uppercase font-bold">
                    <tr>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Waktu</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Invoice</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Kasir</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Metode</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Total</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100 dark:divide-gray-800">
                    {salesList && salesList.length > 0 ? salesList.map((sale) => (
                        <tr key={sale.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24] transition-colors">
                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-xs text-gray-500 font-medium">{sale.sale_date}</td>
                            <td className="px-6 py-4 font-mono font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{sale.invoice_number}</td>
                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 font-medium">{sale.user?.name || '-'}</td>
                            <td className="px-6 py-4">
                                <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase">{sale.payment_method}</span>
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-6 py-4 font-extrabold text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{formatRupiah(sale.total_amount)}</td>
                        </tr>
                    )) : (
                        <tr>
                            <td colSpan="5" className="px-6 py-10 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium">Tidak ada data transaksi pada periode ini.</td>
                        </tr>
                    )}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    );
}

=========================================================
FILE: resources/js/Pages/Reports/Index.jsx
=========================================================
import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import ExportModal from './Components/ExportModal';
import ReportTable from './Components/ReportTable';
import ReportCalendar from './Components/ReportCalendar';
import ReportStockLedger from './Components/ReportStockLedger';

const formatRupiah = (number) => new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', { style: 'currency', currency: 'IDR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(number);

export default function Index({ 
    auth, 
    filters, 
    summary, 
    salesList, 
    calendarData, 
    monthSales, 
    calendarMonth, 
    currentDate,
    stockMutations,
    medicinesList 
}) {
    const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState(filters?.start_date || currentDate);
    const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState(filters?.end_date || currentDate);
    const [selectedMedicineId, setSelectedMedicineId] = useState(filters?.medicine_id || '');
    const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState('table'); // 'table' | 'calendar' | 'ledger'
    const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState(null);
    
    const [isExportModalOpen, setIsExportModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [exportFormat, setExportFormat] = useState('pdf');
    const [selectedReportType, setSelectedReportType] = useState('sales');
    const [notification, setNotification] = useState(null);

    const showNotify = (msg) => {
        setNotification(msg);
        setTimeout(() => setNotification(null), 3000);
    };

    const handleFilter = (period = 'custom') => {
        let params = { period };

        if (period !== 'custom' && period !== 'all') {
            const today = new Date();
            const formatD = (d) => `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
            
            if (period === 'daily') {
                setStartDate(formatD(today));
                setEndDate(formatD(today));
                params = { ...params, start_date: formatD(today), end_date: formatD(today) };
            } 
        } else if (period === 'custom') {
            params = { 
                ...params, 
                start_date: startDate, 
                end_date: endDate,
                medicine_id: selectedMedicineId 
            };
        }
        
        router.get(route('reports.index'), params, { preserveState: true });
    };

    const formatDate = (date) => {
        const year = date.getFullYear();
        const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
        const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
        return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
    };

    const setPresetDate = (preset) => {
        const today = new Date();
        if (preset === 'hari_ini') {
            const dateStr = formatDate(today);
            setStartDate(dateStr); 
            setEndDate(dateStr);
        } else if (preset === 'minggu_ini') {
            const firstDay = new Date(today);
            firstDay.setDate(today.getDate() - today.getDay() + 1);
            const lastDay = new Date(today);
            lastDay.setDate(today.getDate() - today.getDay() + 7);
            setStartDate(formatDate(firstDay)); 
            setEndDate(formatDate(lastDay));
        } else if (preset === 'bulan_ini') {
            const firstDay = new Date(today.getFullYear(), today.getMonth(), 1);
            const lastDay = new Date(today.getFullYear(), today.getMonth() + 1, 0);
            setStartDate(formatDate(firstDay)); 
            setEndDate(formatDate(lastDay));
        } else if (preset === 'tahun_ini') {
            const firstDay = new Date(today.getFullYear(), 0, 1);
            const lastDay = new Date(today.getFullYear(), 11, 31);
            setStartDate(formatDate(firstDay)); 
            setEndDate(formatDate(lastDay));
        }
    };

    const handleExecuteExport = () => {
        setIsExportModalOpen(false);

        const exportUrl = route('reports.export', {
            type: selectedReportType,
            format: exportFormat,
            start_date: startDate,
            end_date: endDate,
            medicine_id: selectedMedicineId
        });

        if (exportFormat === 'pdf') {
            showNotify(`Menyiapkan Cetak PDF Laporan ${selectedReportType.toUpperCase()}...`);
            
            const oldIframe = document.getElementById('print-iframe');
            if (oldIframe) oldIframe.remove();

            const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
            iframe.id = 'print-iframe';
            iframe.style.display = 'none';
            iframe.src = exportUrl;
            document.body.appendChild(iframe);

        } else if (exportFormat === 'excel') {
            showNotify(`Memproses file Excel Laporan ${selectedReportType.toUpperCase()}...`);
            window.location.href = exportUrl;
        }
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Laporan Apotek">
            <Head title="Laporan & Rekapitulasi" />

            <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-500 pb-10 relative">
                
                {notification && (
                    <div className="fixed top-5 right-5 z-50 bg-[#1E272E] text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-[#48CFA4]/40 flex items-center gap-3 animate-in slide-in-from-top-5">
                        <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#48CFA4] animate-pulse"></span>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold">{notification}</p>
                    </div>
                )}

                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4">
                    <div>
                        <h2 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-gray-900 dark:text-white">Laporan & Audit Mutasi Stok</h2>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">Medisync — Pantau arus kas, laba rugi, dan kartu mutasi stok obat.</p>
                    </div>

                    <button 
                    onClick={() => setIsExportModalOpen(true)}
                    className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-md text-sm font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all duration-200 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white hover:bg-[#3bb890] shadow-sm dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#2d4747] dark:border dark:border-[#30413B] dark:shadow-none cursor-pointer">
                        <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M4 16v1a3 3 0 003 3h10a3 3 0 003-3v-1m-4-4l-4 4m0 0l-4-4m4 4V4"></path></svg>Export Laporan</button>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-4 rounded-[20px] border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 shadow-sm flex flex-col xl:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4">
                    <div className="flex gap-1.5 w-full xl:w-auto overflow-x-auto custom-scrollbar pb-2 xl:pb-0">
                        {[{ key: 'daily', label: 'Harian' }, { key: 'weekly', label: 'Mingguan' }, { key: 'monthly', label: 'Bulanan' }, { key: 'yearly', label: 'Tahunan' }, { key: 'all', label: 'Seluruhnya' }].map((item) => (
                            <button 
                                key={item.key} 
                                onClick={() => handleFilter(item.key)}
                                className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition-all whitespace-nowrap ${filters?.period === item.key ? 'bg-[#EAF5F0] dark:bg-[#253939] text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#67E2B4]' : 'bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100'}`}
                            >
                                {item.label}
                            </button>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 w-full xl:w-auto">
                        {activeView === 'ledger' && (
                            <select 
                                value={selectedMedicineId} 
                                onChange={e => setSelectedMedicineId(e.target.value)}
                                className="px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 font-medium"
                            >
                                <option value="">-- Semua Obat Master --</option>
                                {(medicinesList || []).map(m => (
                                    <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.sku} - {m.name}</option>
                                ))}
                            </select>
                        )}
                        <input type="date" value={startDate} onChange={e => setStartDate(e.target.value)} className="px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200" />
                        <span className="text-gray-400 text-xs">s/d</span>
                        <input type="date" value={endDate} onChange={e => setEndDate(e.target.value)} className="px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200" />
                        <button onClick={() => handleFilter('custom')} className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-800 dark:bg-white text-white dark:text-gray-900 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl hover:opacity-95">Cari</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 shadow-sm">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Omzet (Pendapatan)</p>
                        <h4 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-900 dark:text-white mt-1">{formatRupiah(summary.total_sales)}</h4>
                    </div>
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 shadow-sm">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Total HPP (Modal)</p>
                        <h4 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-orange-500 mt-1">{formatRupiah(summary.total_hpp)}</h4>
                    </div>
                    <div className="bg-[#48CFA4] p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm text-white relative overflow-hidden">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase opacity-90 relative z-10">Laba Bersih</p>
                        <h4 className="text-xl font-extrabold mt-1 relative z-10">{formatRupiah(summary.profit)}</h4>
                    </div>
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 shadow-sm">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Total Transaksi</p>
                        <h4 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-900 dark:text-white mt-1">{summary.total_transactions} Struk</h4>
                    </div>
                    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 shadow-sm">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Stok Menipis</p>
                        <h4 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-red-500 mt-1">{summary.low_stock} Item</h4>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-[24px] border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 shadow-sm overflow-hidden mt-6">
                    <div className="border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-center px-6 py-4 gap-4 bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-[#151E24]/30">
                        <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
                            {activeView === 'table' && 'Tabel Transaksi Penjualan'}
                            {activeView === 'calendar' && 'Kalender Omzet Penjualan'}
                            {activeView === 'ledger' && 'Kartu Stok & Mutasi Obat (Audit Trail)'}
                        </h3>
                        
                        <div className="flex bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#151E24] p-1.5 rounded-xl gap-1">
                            <button 
                                onClick={() => setActiveView('table')} 
                                title="Tabel Penjualan"
                                className={`px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer ${activeView === 'table' ? 'bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] text-gray-900 dark:text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'}`}
                            >
                                <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2.5" d="M3 10h18M3 14h18m-9-4v8m-7 0h14a2 2 0 002-2V8a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v8a2 2 0 002 2z"/></svg>
                                Transaksi
                            </button>

                            <button 
                                onClick={() => setActiveView('calendar')} 
                                title="Kalender Omzet"
                                className={`px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer ${activeView === 'calendar' ? 'bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] text-[#48CFA4] shadow-sm' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'}`}
                            >
                                <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2.5" d="M8 7V3m8 4V3m-9 8h10M5 21h14a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z"/></svg>
                                Kalender
                            </button>

                            <button 
                                onClick={() => setActiveView('ledger')} 
                                title="Kartu Stok Obat"
                                className={`px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer ${activeView === 'ledger' ? 'bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] text-amber-500 shadow-sm' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'}`}
                            >
                                <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2.5" d="M9 17v-2m3 2v-4m3 4v-6m2 10H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z"/></svg>
                                Mutasi Stok
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {activeView === 'table' && <ReportTable salesList={salesList} formatRupiah={formatRupiah} />}
                    
                    {activeView === 'calendar' && (
                        <ReportCalendar 
                            calendarMonth={calendarMonth}
                            calendarData={calendarData}
                            selectedDate={selectedDate}
                            setSelectedDate={setSelectedDate}
                            monthSales={monthSales}
                            formatRupiah={formatRupiah}
                        />
                    )}

                    {activeView === 'ledger' && (
                        <ReportStockLedger stockMutations={stockMutations} />
                    )}
                </div>

                <ExportModal 
                    isOpen={isExportModalOpen}
                    onClose={() => setIsExportModalOpen(false)}
                    selectedReportType={selectedReportType}
                    setSelectedReportType={setSelectedReportType}
                    startDate={startDate}
                    setStartDate={setStartDate}
                    endDate={endDate}
                    setEndDate={setEndDate}
                    exportFormat={exportFormat}
                    setExportFormat={setExportFormat}
                    handleExecuteExport={handleExecuteExport}
                    setPresetDate={setPresetDate}
                />

            </div>
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

const WarningIcon = () => (
    <svg className="w-6 h-6 text-red-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-3L13.732 4c-.77-1.333-2.694-1.333-3.464 0L3.34 16c-.77 1.333.192 3 1.732 3z" /></svg>
);

export default function ConfirmModal({ isOpen, title, message, onClose, onConfirm }) {
    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in">
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-w-sm w-full mx-4 text-center border dark:border-gray-800">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/30 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                    <WarningIcon />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100 mb-1">{title}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-6 leading-relaxed">{message}</p>
                
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button 
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs transition-all"
                    >
                        Batal
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={onConfirm}
                        className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white rounded-xl font-bold text-xs shadow-lg shadow-red-600/30 transition-all"
                    >
                        Ya, Lanjutkan
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

=========================================================
FILE: resources/js/Pages/Sales/Index.jsx
=========================================================
import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import SalesFilter from './SalesFilter';
import SalesTable from './SalesTable';
import ReceiptModal from './ReceiptModal';
import ConfirmModal from './ConfirmModal';

export default function Index({ sales, isTrash }) {
    const [selectedSale, setSelectedSale] = useState(null);
    const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState([]);
    const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('all');
    const [customDate, setCustomDate] = useState('');
    const [confirmModal, setConfirmModal] = useState({ show: false, title: '', message: '', action: null });

    const formatRupiah = (number) => {
        return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', { style: 'currency', currency: 'IDR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(number || 0);
    };

    const filteredSales = useMemo(() => {
        if (!sales?.data) return [];
        if (filterType === 'all') return sales.data;

        const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        const yesterdayDate = new Date();
        yesterdayDate.setDate(yesterdayDate.getDate() - 1);
        const yesterday = yesterdayDate.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

        return sales.data.filter(sale => {
            const saleDateOnly = sale.sale_date.slice(0, 10);
            if (filterType === 'today') return saleDateOnly === today;
            if (filterType === 'yesterday') return saleDateOnly === yesterday;
            if (filterType === 'custom' && customDate) return saleDateOnly === customDate;
            return true;
        });
    }, [sales, filterType, customDate]);

    const handleSelectAll = (e) => {
        if (e.target.checked) {
            setSelectedIds(filteredSales.map(sale => sale.id));
        } else {
            setSelectedIds([]);
        }
    };

    const handleSelectOne = (id) => {
        if (selectedIds.includes(id)) {
            setSelectedIds(selectedIds.filter(item => item !== id));
        } else {
            setSelectedIds([...selectedIds, id]);
        }
    };

    const handleDelete = (id) => {
        setConfirmModal({
            show: true,
            title: 'Pindahkan ke Sampah?',
            message: 'Apakah Anda yakin ingin memindahkan transaksi penjualan ini ke sampah?',
            action: () => router.delete(route('sales.destroy', id), {
                onSuccess: () => { setConfirmModal({ show: false }); setSelectedIds([]); }
            })
        });
    };

    const handleRestore = (id) => {
        setConfirmModal({
            show: true,
            title: 'Pulihkan Transaksi?',
            message: 'Pulihkan transaksi ini kembali ke daftar riwayat penjualan aktif?',
            action: () => router.post(route('sales.restore', id), {}, {
                onSuccess: () => { setConfirmModal({ show: false }); setSelectedIds([]); }
            })
        });
    };

    const handleForceDelete = (id) => {
        setConfirmModal({
            show: true,
            title: 'Hapus Permanen?',
            message: 'PERINGATAN: Transaksi akan dihapus secara permanen dari database dan tidak dapat dikembalikan!',
            action: () => router.delete(route('sales.force-delete', id), {
                onSuccess: () => { setConfirmModal({ show: false }); setSelectedIds([]); }
            })
        });
    };

    const handleBulkAction = (actionType) => {
        if (selectedIds.length === 0) return;

        let title = '';
        let message = '';
        let actionCallback = null;

        if (actionType === 'delete') {
            title = 'Pindahkan Terpilih ke Sampah?';
            message = `Anda akan memindahkan ${selectedIds.length} transaksi terpilih ke sampah.`;
            actionCallback = () => {
                router.post(route('sales.bulk-destroy'), { ids: selectedIds }, {
                    onSuccess: () => {
                        setConfirmModal({ show: false });
                        setSelectedIds([]);
                    }
                });
            };
        } else if (actionType === 'restore') {
            title = 'Pulihkan Terpilih?';
            message = `Pulihkan ${selectedIds.length} transaksi terpilih kembali ke aktif.`;
            actionCallback = () => {
                router.post(route('sales.bulk-restore'), { ids: selectedIds }, {
                    onSuccess: () => {
                        setConfirmModal({ show: false });
                        setSelectedIds([]);
                    }
                });
            };
        } else if (actionType === 'force-delete') {
            title = 'Hapus Permanen Terpilih?';
            message = `PERINGATAN: ${selectedIds.length} transaksi akan dihapus permanen secara massal!`;
            actionCallback = () => {
                selectedIds.forEach(id => {
                    router.delete(route('sales.force-delete', id), { preserveScroll: true });
                });
                setConfirmModal({ show: false });
                setSelectedIds([]);
            };
        }

        setConfirmModal({
            show: true,
            title,
            message,
            action: actionCallback
        });
    };

    const printHtmlContent = (htmlContent, title = 'Cetak Struk') => {
        const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
        iframe.style.position = 'fixed';
        iframe.style.right = '0';
        iframe.style.bottom = '0';
        iframe.style.width = '0px';
        iframe.style.height = '0px';
        iframe.style.border = 'none';

        document.body.appendChild(iframe);

        const iframeDoc = iframe.contentWindow || iframe.contentDocument.document || iframe.contentDocument;
        iframeDoc.document.open();
        iframeDoc.document.write(`
            <html>
                <head>
                    <title>${title}</title>
                    <style>
                        @page { size: 58mm auto; margin: 0; }
                        body { font-family: 'Courier New', Courier, monospace; font-size: 11px; width: 58mm; margin: 0; padding: 5px; color: #000; }
                    </style>
                </head>
                <body>${htmlContent}</body>
            </html>
        `);
        iframeDoc.document.close();

        setTimeout(() => {
            iframe.contentWindow.focus();
            iframe.contentWindow.print();

            iframe.contentWindow.onafterprint = () => {
                if (document.body.contains(iframe)) {
                    document.body.removeChild(iframe);
                }
            };

            setTimeout(() => {
                if (document.body.contains(iframe)) {
                    document.body.removeChild(iframe);
                }
            }, 2000);
        }, 300);
    };

    const handleBulkPrint = () => {
        if (selectedIds.length === 0) return;
        const salesToPrint = filteredSales.filter(sale => selectedIds.includes(sale.id));
        
        let combinedHtml = '';
        salesToPrint.forEach(sale => {
            let itemsHtml = sale.details.map(i => `
                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                    <div>${i.medicine?.name || 'Obat'} (${i.quantity}x)</div>
                    <div>${formatRupiah(i.subtotal)}</div>
                </div>
            `).join('');

            combinedHtml += `
                <div style="page-break-after: always; margin-bottom: 20px;">
                    <div style="text-align: center; border-bottom: 1px dashed #000; padding-bottom: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px;">
                        <div style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 13px;">APOTEK MEDISYNC</div>
                        <div>Nota: ${sale.invoice_number}</div>
                        <div>${new Date(sale.sale_date).toLocaleString('id-ID')}</div>
                        <div>Kasir: ${sale.user?.name || 'Admin'}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="margin-bottom: 8px;">${itemsHtml}</div>
                    <div style="border-top: 1px dashed #000; padding-top: 8px; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; font-weight: bold;"><span>Total:</span> <span>${formatRupiah(sale.total_amount)}</span></div>
                        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;"><span>Metode:</span> <span>${sale.payment_method}</span></div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="text-align: center; font-size: 10px;">TERIMA KASIH - LEKAS SEMBUH KEMBALI</div>
                </div>
            `;
        });

        printHtmlContent(combinedHtml, 'Cetak Struk Massal');
    };

    const executePrintSingle = () => {
        const printContents = document.getElementById('modal-printable-receipt')?.innerHTML || '';
        if (printContents) {
            printHtmlContent(printContents, 'Cetak Struk');
        }
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Riwayat Penjualan">
            <Head title="Riwayat Penjualan" />

            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 overflow-hidden">
                <SalesFilter 
                    isTrash={isTrash}
                    selectedIds={selectedIds}
                    filterType={filterType}
                    setFilterType={setFilterType}
                    customDate={customDate}
                    setCustomDate={setCustomDate}
                    onBulkPrint={handleBulkPrint}
                    onBulkAction={handleBulkAction}
                />

                <SalesTable 
                    sales={filteredSales}
                    isTrash={isTrash}
                    selectedIds={selectedIds}
                    onSelectAll={handleSelectAll}
                    onSelectOne={handleSelectOne}
                    onPrint={(sale) => setSelectedSale(sale)}
                    onDelete={handleDelete}
                    onRestore={handleRestore}
                    onForceDelete={handleForceDelete}
                />
            </div>

            <ConfirmModal 
                isOpen={confirmModal.show}
                title={confirmModal.title}
                message={confirmModal.message}
                onClose={() => setConfirmModal({ show: false })}
                onConfirm={confirmModal.action}
            />

            <ReceiptModal 
                selectedSale={selectedSale}
                onClose={() => setSelectedSale(null)}
                onPrint={executePrintSingle}
            />
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

=========================================================
FILE: resources/js/Pages/Sales/ReceiptModal.jsx

export default function ReceiptModal({ selectedSale, onClose, onPrint }) {
    if (!selectedSale) return null;

    const formatRupiah = (number) => {
        return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', { style: 'currency', currency: 'IDR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(number || 0);
    };

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm">
            <div className="bg-white text-black p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-w-sm w-full mx-4 font-mono text-xs">
                
                {/* AREA YANG AKAN DICETAK (Menggunakan Table agar posisinya stabil sejajar kiri-kanan) */}
                <div id="modal-printable-receipt" className="bg-white text-black p-2" style={{ fontFamily: 'Courier New, Courier, monospace', fontSize: '11px', width: '100%' }}>
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', paddingBottom: '8px', borderBottom: '1px dashed #000', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                        <div style={{ fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: '13px' }}>APOTEK MEDISYNC</div>
                        <div>Nota: {selectedSale.invoice_number}</div>
                        <div>{new Date(selectedSale.sale_date).toLocaleString('id-ID')}</div>
                        <div>Kasir: {selectedSale.user?.name || 'Admin'}</div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div style={{ paddingBottom: '8px', borderBottom: '1px dashed #000', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                        <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: '11px' }}>
                            <tbody>
                                {selectedSale.details.map((item, idx) => (
                                    <tr key={idx}>
                                        <td style={{ paddingBottom: '4px', verticalAlign: 'top' }}>
                                            {item.medicine?.name || 'Obat'} ({item.quantity}x)
                                        </td>
                                        <td style={{ paddingBottom: '4px', textAlign: 'right', verticalAlign: 'top', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                                            {formatRupiah(item.subtotal)}
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                ))}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ paddingBottom: '8px', borderBottom: '1px dashed #000', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                        <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: '11px' }}>
                            <tbody>
                                <tr>
                                    <td style={{ fontWeight: 'bold', paddingBottom: '2px' }}>Total Tagihan:</td>
                                    <td style={{ fontWeight: 'bold', textAlign: 'right', paddingBottom: '2px' }}>{formatRupiah(selectedSale.total_amount)}</td>
                                </tr>
                                <tr>
                                    <td style={{ paddingBottom: '2px' }}>Metode Bayar:</td>
                                    <td style={{ textAlign: 'right', paddingBottom: '2px' }}>{selectedSale.payment_method}</td>
                                </tr>
                                <tr>
                                    <td style={{ paddingBottom: '2px' }}>Total Dibayar:</td>
                                    <td style={{ textAlign: 'right', paddingBottom: '2px' }}>{formatRupiah(selectedSale.paid_amount)}</td>
                                </tr>
                                <tr>
                                    <td style={{ paddingBottom: '2px' }}>Kembalian:</td>
                                    <td style={{ textAlign: 'right', paddingBottom: '2px' }}>{formatRupiah(selectedSale.change_amount)}</td>
                                </tr>
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '10px', paddingTop: '4px' }}>
                        TERIMA KASIH - LEKAS SEMBUH KEMBALI
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* TOMBOL AKSI MODAL */}
                <div className="flex gap-2 pt-4 border-t mt-2">
                    <button 
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="flex-1 py-2 bg-gray-200 text-gray-800 rounded-xl font-sans font-bold"
                    >
                        Tutup
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={onPrint}
                        className="flex-1 py-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl font-sans font-bold shadow-md"
                    >
                        Cetak Ulang Struk
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

import { Link } from '@inertiajs/react';

const Icons = {
    Trash: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16" /></svg>,
    TrashSolid: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M3 6h18M19 6v14a2 2 0 01-2 2H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V6m3 0V4a2 2 0 012-2h4a2 2 0 012 2v2M10 11v6M14 11v6" /></svg>,
    Restore: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 2A8.001 8.001 0 004.582 9m0 0H9m11 11v-5h-.581m0 0a8.003 8.003 0 01-15.357-2m15.357 2H15" /></svg>,
    Print: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M17 17h2a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h6a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2zm8-12V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4h10z" /></svg>,
    Back: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M10 19l-7-7m0 0l7-7m-7 7h18" /></svg>,
    Calendar: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-[#48CFA4]" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="18" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><line x1="16" y1="2" x2="16" y2="6"></line><line x1="8" y1="2" x2="8" y2="6"></line><line x1="3" y1="10" x2="21" y2="10"></line></svg>,
    Filter: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-[#48CFA4]" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><polygon points="22 3 2 3 10 12.46 10 19 14 21 14 12.46 22 3"></polygon></svg>,
};

export default function SalesFilter({ isTrash, selectedIds, filterType, setFilterType, customDate, setCustomDate, onBulkPrint, onBulkAction }) {
    return (
        <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 flex flex-col xl:flex-row justify-between items-start xl:items-center gap-4">
            <div>
                <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">
                    {isTrash ? 'Sampah Riwayat Penjualan (Trash)' : 'Daftar Transaksi Kasir'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Kelola riwayat transaksi, filter tanggal, dan aksi massal.</p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                {/* FILTER WAKTU & TANGGAL */}
                {!isTrash && (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        {/* Dropdown Preset Waktu */}
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 shadow-sm">
                            <Icons.Filter />
                            <select 
                                value={filterType} 
                                onChange={(e) => setFilterType(e.target.value)}
                                className="bg-transparent border-none text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 focus:ring-0 cursor-pointer pr-4"
                            >
                                <option value="all" className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Semua Riwayat</option>
                                <option value="today" className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Hari Ini</option>
                                <option value="yesterday" className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Kemarin</option>
                                <option value="custom" className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Pilih Tanggal Spesifik</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>

                        {/* Input Tanggal Khusus (Hanya muncul jika memilih 'custom') */}
                        {filterType === 'custom' && (
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 shadow-sm animate-in fade-in">
                                <Icons.Calendar />
                                <input 
                                    type="date" 
                                    value={customDate}
                                    onChange={(e) => setCustomDate(e.target.value)}
                                    className="bg-transparent border-none text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 focus:ring-0 cursor-pointer"
                                />
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* TOMBOL AKSI MASSAL JIKA ADA ITEM DICENTANG */}
                {selectedIds.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/30 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 animate-in fade-in">
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">{selectedIds.length} Dipilih:</span>
                        
                        <button 
                            onClick={onBulkPrint}
                            className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-[#48CFA4] hover:bg-[#3AB890] text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm transition-all"
                        >
                            <Icons.Print /> Cetak Pilihan
                        </button>

                        {isTrash ? (
                            <>
                                <button 
                                    onClick={() => onBulkAction('restore')}
                                    className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 transition-all"
                                >
                                    <Icons.Restore /> Pulihkan
                                </button>
                                <button 
                                    onClick={() => onBulkAction('force-delete')}
                                    className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 transition-all"
                                >
                                    <Icons.TrashSolid /> Hapus Permanen
                                </button>
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <button 
                                onClick={() => onBulkAction('delete')}
                                className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 transition-all"
                            >
                                <Icons.Trash /> Hapus Pilihan
                            </button>
                        )}
                    </div>
                )}

                {isTrash ? (
                    <Link 
                        href={route('sales.history')} 
                        title="Kembali ke Riwayat"
                        className="p-2.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 rounded-xl transition-all flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm"
                    >
                        <Icons.Back />
                    </Link>
                ) : (
                    <Link 
                        href={route('sales.trash')} 
                        title="Lihat Sampah"
                        className="p-2.5 bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/20 dark:text-red-400 rounded-xl transition-all flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm"
                    >
                        <Icons.Trash />
                    </Link>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

const Icons = {
    Trash: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16" /></svg>,
    TrashSolid: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M3 6h18M19 6v14a2 2 0 01-2 2H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V6m3 0V4a2 2 0 012-2h4a2 2 0 012 2v2M10 11v6M14 11v6" /></svg>,
    Restore: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M4 4v5h.582m15.356 2A8.001 8.001 0 004.582 9m0 0H9m11 11v-5h-.581m0 0a8.003 8.003 0 01-15.357-2m15.357 2H15" /></svg>,
    Print: () => <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M17 17h2a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h6a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2zm8-12V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4h10z" /></svg>,
};

export default function SalesTable({ sales, isTrash, selectedIds, onSelectAll, onSelectOne, onPrint, onDelete, onRestore, onForceDelete }) {
    const formatRupiah = (number) => {
        return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', { style: 'currency', currency: 'IDR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(number || 0);
    };

    return (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                <thead className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] text-gray-500 uppercase text-xs">
                    <tr>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4 w-10">
                            <input 
                                type="checkbox" 
                                onChange={onSelectAll}
                                checked={sales?.length > 0 && selectedIds.length === sales.length}
                                className="rounded border-gray-300 text-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-[#48CFA4] w-4 h-4 cursor-pointer"
                            />
                        </th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">No. Invoice</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Waktu</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Kasir</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Metode Bayar</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Total Tagihan</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center">Aksi</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100 dark:divide-gray-800">
                    {!sales || sales.length === 0 ? (
                        <tr>
                            <td colSpan="7" className="px-6 py-10 text-center text-gray-400">Belum ada data transaksi.</td>
                        </tr>
                    ) : (
                        sales.map((sale) => (
                            <tr key={sale.id} className={`hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24] ${selectedIds.includes(sale.id) ? 'bg-emerald-50/50 dark:bg-emerald-950/20' : ''}`}>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4">
                                    <input 
                                        type="checkbox" 
                                        checked={selectedIds.includes(sale.id)}
                                        onChange={() => onSelectOne(sale.id)}
                                        className="rounded border-gray-300 text-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-[#48CFA4] w-4 h-4 cursor-pointer"
                                    />
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 font-mono font-bold text-[#48CFA4]">{sale.invoice_number}</td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">{new Date(sale.sale_date).toLocaleString('id-ID')}</td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{sale.user?.name || 'Admin'}</td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4">
                                    <span className="px-2 py-1 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase">{sale.payment_method}</span>
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 font-mono font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{formatRupiah(sale.total_amount)}</td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                                        <button 
                                            onClick={() => onPrint(sale)}
                                            title="Cetak Struk"
                                            className="p-2 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-xl transition-all"
                                        >
                                            <Icons.Print />
                                        </button>

                                        {isTrash ? (
                                            <>
                                                <button 
                                                    onClick={() => onRestore(sale.id)}
                                                    title="Pulihkan Transaksi"
                                                    className="p-2 bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/30 dark:text-blue-400 rounded-xl transition-all"
                                                >
                                                    <Icons.Restore />
                                                </button>
                                                <button 
                                                    onClick={() => onForceDelete(sale.id)}
                                                    title="Hapus Permanen"
                                                    className="p-2 bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 rounded-xl transition-all"
                                                >
                                                    <Icons.TrashSolid />
                                                </button>
                                            </>
                                        ) : (
                                            <button 
                                                onClick={() => onDelete(sale.id)}
                                                title="Hapus / Masukkan ke Sampah"
                                                className="p-2 bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 rounded-xl transition-all"
                                            >
                                                <Icons.Trash />
                                            </button>
                                        )}
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        ))
                    )}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: resources/js/Pages/Suppliers/Create.jsx

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import PhoneInput from '@/Components/PhoneInput';

export default function Create({ supplierTypes = [] }) {
    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors } = useForm({
        supplier_type_id: '',
        code: '',
        name: '',
        contact_person: '',
        phone: '',
        email: '',
        address: '',
    });

    const handleTypeChange = (e) => {
        const typeId = e.target.value;
        const selected = supplierTypes.find(t => t.id == typeId);
        
        if (selected) {
            const randomNum = Math.floor(100 + Math.random() * 900);
            setData(prev => ({
                ...prev,
                supplier_type_id: typeId,
                code: `${selected.prefix_code}-${randomNum}`
            }));
        } else {
            setData('supplier_type_id', typeId);
        }
    };

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        post(route('suppliers.store'));
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Tambah Supplier Baru">
            <Head title="Tambah Supplier" />

            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 p-8 max-w-3xl mx-auto transition-colors duration-300">
                <div className="mb-8">
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Informasi Perusahaan</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Masukkan detail lengkap pemasok atau pabrik farmasi.</p>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-6">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Tipe Pemasok (Referensi)</label>
                            <select 
                                value={data.supplier_type_id} 
                                onChange={handleTypeChange}
                                required
                                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors"
                            >
                                <option value="">-- Pilih Tipe Pemasok --</option>
                                {supplierTypes.map((type) => (
                                    <option key={type.id} value={type.id}>
                                        {type.name} ({type.prefix_code})
                                    </option>
                                ))}
                            </select>
                            <InputError message={errors.supplier_type_id} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Kode Pemasok</label>
                            <input 
                                type="text" 
                                value={data.code} 
                                onChange={e => setData('code', e.target.value)} 
                                required 
                                placeholder="Pilih Tipe Pemasok Dulu"
                                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#11181C] border-transparent rounded-xl text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors font-mono font-bold text-[#48CFA4] uppercase" 
                            />
                            <InputError message={errors.code} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>
                        
                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nama Perusahaan</label>
                            <input type="text" value={data.name} onChange={e => setData('name', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" />
                            <InputError message={errors.name} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Kontak Person</label>
                            <input type="text" value={data.contact_person} onChange={e => setData('contact_person', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" />
                            <InputError message={errors.contact_person} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nomor Telepon</label>
                            <PhoneInput value={data.phone} onChange={(value) => setData('phone', value)} />
                            <InputError message={errors.phone} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div className="md:col-span-2">
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Alamat Email</label>
                            <input type="email" value={data.email} onChange={e => setData('email', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" />
                            <InputError message={errors.email} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div className="md:col-span-2">
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Alamat Lengkap</label>
                            <textarea value={data.address} onChange={e => setData('address', e.target.value)} rows="3" className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors"></textarea>
                            <InputError message={errors.address} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-6 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 mt-8">
                        <Link href={route('suppliers.index')} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors">Batal</Link>
                        <button type="submit" disabled={processing} className="px-6 py-3 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-[#3bb890] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50">
                            {processing ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan Data'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

=========================================================
FILE: resources/js/Pages/Suppliers/Edit.jsx

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import PhoneInput from '@/Components/PhoneInput';

export default function Edit({ supplierEdit, supplierTypes = [] }) {
    const { data, setData, put, processing, errors } = useForm({
        supplier_type_id: supplierEdit.supplier_type_id || '',
        code: supplierEdit.code || '',
        name: supplierEdit.name || '',
        contact_person: supplierEdit.contact_person || '',
        phone: supplierEdit.phone || '',
        email: supplierEdit.email || '',
        address: supplierEdit.address || '',
        is_active: supplierEdit.is_active,
    });

    const handleTypeChange = (e) => {
        const typeId = e.target.value;
        const selected = supplierTypes.find(t => t.id == typeId);
        
        if (selected) {
            const parts = data.code.split('-');
            const uniqueNumber = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1] : data.code;
            
            setData(prev => ({
                ...prev,
                supplier_type_id: typeId,
                code: `${selected.prefix_code}-${uniqueNumber}`
            }));
        } else {
            setData('supplier_type_id', typeId);
        }
    };

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        put(route('suppliers.update', supplierEdit.id));
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Perbarui Data Supplier">
            <Head title="Edit Supplier"/>

            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 p-8 max-w-3xl mx-auto transition-colors duration-300">
                <div className="mb-8">
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Informasi Perusahaan</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Ubah detail pemasok atau nonaktifkan jika kerja sama telah berakhir.</p>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-6">
                    
                    <div className="p-4 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <div>
                            <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Status Kemitraan</h4>
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Matikan jika perusahaan ini tidak lagi menjadi penyuplai obat.</p>
                        </div>
                        <label className="relative inline-flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                            <input type="checkbox" checked={data.is_active} onChange={e => setData('is_active', e.target.checked)} className="sr-only peer" />
                            <div className="w-11 h-6 bg-gray-200 peer-focus:outline-none rounded-full peer dark:bg-gray-700 peer-checked:after:translate-x-full peer-checked:after:border-white after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-[2px] after:left-[2px] after:bg-white after:border-gray-300 after:border after:rounded-full after:h-5 after:w-5 after:transition-all peer-checked:bg-[#48CFA4]"></div>
                        </label>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                        
                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Tipe Pemasok (Referensi)</label>
                            <select 
                                value={data.supplier_type_id} 
                                onChange={handleTypeChange}
                                required
                                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors"
                            >
                                <option value="">-- Pilih Tipe Pemasok --</option>
                                {supplierTypes.map((type) => (
                                    <option key={type.id} value={type.id}>
                                        {type.name} ({type.prefix_code})
                                    </option>
                                ))}
                            </select>
                            <InputError message={errors.supplier_type_id} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Kode Pemasok</label>
                            <input 
                                type="text" 
                                value={data.code} 
                                onChange={e => setData('code', e.target.value)} 
                                required 
                                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-[#11181C] border-transparent rounded-xl text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors uppercase font-mono font-bold text-[#48CFA4]" 
                            />
                            <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mt-1">Otomatis berubah menyesuaikan tipe pemasok.</p>
                            <InputError message={errors.code} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>
                        
                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nama Perusahaan</label>
                            <input type="text" value={data.name} onChange={e => setData('name', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" />
                            <InputError message={errors.name} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Kontak Person</label>
                            <input type="text" value={data.contact_person} onChange={e => setData('contact_person', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" />
                            <InputError message={errors.contact_person} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nomor Telepon</label>
                            <PhoneInput value={data.phone} onChange={(value) => setData('phone', value)} />
                            <InputError message={errors.phone} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div className="md:col-span-2">
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Alamat Email</label>
                            <input type="email" value={data.email} onChange={e => setData('email', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" />
                            <InputError message={errors.email} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div className="md:col-span-2">
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Alamat Lengkap</label>
                            <textarea value={data.address} onChange={e => setData('address', e.target.value)} rows="3" className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors"></textarea>
                            <InputError message={errors.address} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-6 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 mt-8">
                        <Link href={route('suppliers.index')} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors">Batal</Link>
                        <button type="submit" disabled={processing} className="px-6 py-3 bg-[#48CFA4] text-white rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-[#3bb890] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50">
                            {processing ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan Perubahan'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}   

=========================================================
FILE: resources/js/Pages/Suppliers/Index.jsx
