import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, router } from '@inertiajs/react'; 
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import ConfirmModal from '@/Components/ConfirmModal';
import { useState } from 'react';

const EditIcon = () => (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z"></path>
    </svg>
);

const DeleteIcon = () => (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
    </svg>
);

export default function Index({ suppliers }) {
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [supplierToDelete, setSupplierToDelete] = useState(null);
    const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);

    const confirmDelete = (supplier) => {
        setSupplierToDelete(supplier);
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    const executeDelete = () => {
        setIsDeleting(true);
        router.delete(route('suppliers.destroy', supplierToDelete.id), {
            preserveScroll: true,
            onSuccess: () => {
                setIsDeleting(false);
                setIsModalOpen(false);
            },
            onError: () => {
                setIsDeleting(false);
                setIsModalOpen(false);
            },
        });
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Direktori Supplier">
            <Head title="Data Supplier" />

            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Daftar Pemasok Resmi</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Kelola data distributor dan pabrik farmasi.</p>
                </div>
                <PrimaryButton as="link" href={route('suppliers.create')}>
                    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M12 4v16m8-8H4"></path></svg>
                    Tambah Supplier
                </PrimaryButton>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors duration-300">
                <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                    <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 tracking-wider">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Kode</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Nama Perusahaan</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Tipe (Referensi)</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Telepon</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Status</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Aksi</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        {!suppliers || suppliers.length === 0 ? (
                            <tr>
                                <td colSpan="6" className="px-6 py-16 text-center text-gray-400">Belum ada data supplier yang didaftarkan.</td>
                            </tr>
                        ) : (
                            suppliers.map((supplier) => (
                                <tr key={supplier.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 dark:border-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24] transition-colors">
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">{supplier.code}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 font-bold text-blue-600 dark:text-[#6FD8E8]">{supplier.name}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5">
                                        <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/30 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
                                            {supplier.supplier_type ? `${supplier.supplier_type.name}` : '-'}
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 font-mono tracking-wide">{supplier.phone || '-'}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                        {supplier.is_active ? 
                                            <span className="bg-[#EAF5F0] dark:bg-[#253939] text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#67E2B4] text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">Aktif</span> : 
                                            <span className="bg-red-50 text-red-600 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">Nonaktif</span>
                                        }
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                        <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-3">
                                            <Link href={route('suppliers.edit', supplier.id)} className="p-2 text-[#48CFA4] bg-[#EAF5F0] hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#48CFA4] dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200">
                                                <EditIcon />
                                            </Link>
                                            <button onClick={() => confirmDelete(supplier)} className="p-2 text-red-500 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-500 dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200">
                                                <DeleteIcon />
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            ))
                        )}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            <ConfirmModal 
                isOpen={isModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={executeDelete}
                isProcessing={isDeleting}
                title="Hapus Data Supplier?"
                message={
                    <>Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus data pemasok <span className="font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200">{supplierToDelete?.name}</span>? Tindakan ini tidak dapat dibatalkan.</>
                }
            />
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function Create({ roles, branches }) {
    const { data, setData, post, processing, errors } = useForm({
        name: '',
        email: '',
        password: '',
        password_confirmation: '',
        role_id: '',
        branch_id: '',
    });

    const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        post(route('users.store'));
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Buat Akun Karyawan">
            <Head title="Buat Akun Baru" />

            <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto">
                <div className="mb-8">
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Kredensial Akses</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Pastikan alamat email valid. Karyawan dapat mengubah passwordnya sendiri nanti.</p>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors duration-300">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                        
                        <div className="md:col-span-2">
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nama Lengkap Karyawan <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                            <input 
                                type="text" 
                                value={data.name} 
                                onChange={e => setData('name', e.target.value)} 
                                required 
                                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                            />
                            <InputError message={errors.name} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div className="md:col-span-2">
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Alamat Email Karyawan <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                            <input 
                                type="email" 
                                value={data.email} 
                                onChange={e => setData('email', e.target.value)} 
                                required 
                                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                            />
                            <InputError message={errors.email} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Password Sementara <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                            <input 
                                type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                                value={data.password} 
                                onChange={e => setData('password', e.target.value)} 
                                required 
                                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                            />
                            <InputError message={errors.password} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Konfirmasi Password <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                            <input 
                                type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                                value={data.password_confirmation} 
                                onChange={e => setData('password_confirmation', e.target.value)} 
                                required 
                                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" 
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div className="md:col-span-2 flex justify-end">
                            <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                <input type="checkbox" checked={showPassword} onChange={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="w-4 h-4 text-[#48CFA4] rounded border-gray-300 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]" />
                                <span className="ml-2 text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">Tampilkan Password</span>
                            </label>
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Hak Akses (Role) <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                            <select 
                                value={data.role_id} 
                                onChange={e => setData('role_id', e.target.value)} 
                                required 
                                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors uppercase"
                            >
                                <option value="">-- PILIH ROLE --</option>
                                {roles.map(r => <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.name}</option>)}
                            </select>
                            <InputError message={errors.role_id} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Penempatan Cabang <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                            <select 
                                value={data.branch_id} 
                                onChange={e => setData('branch_id', e.target.value)} 
                                required 
                                className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors"
                            >
                                <option value="">-- PILIH CABANG --</option>
                                {branches.map(b => <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>{b.name}</option>)}
                            </select>
                            <InputError message={errors.branch_id} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-8 mt-4 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <Link href={route('users.index')} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center">
                            Batal
                        </Link>
                        <PrimaryButton type="submit" disabled={processing} className="px-8 text-base">
                            {processing ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Buat Akun Karyawan'}
                        </PrimaryButton>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

FILE: resources/js/Pages/Users/Edit.jsx

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, useForm } from '@inertiajs/react';
import InputError from '@/Components/InputError';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function Edit({ userEdit, roles, branches }) {
    const { data, setData, put, processing, errors } = useForm({
        name: userEdit.name,
        email: userEdit.email,
        password: '',
        password_confirmation: '',
        role_id: userEdit.role_id,
        branch_id: userEdit.branch_id,
        is_active: userEdit.is_active,
    });

    const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);

    const submit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        put(route('users.update', userEdit.id));
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Edit Akun Karyawan">
            <Head title="Edit Akun" />

            <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto">
                <div className="mb-8">
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Perbarui Kredensial</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Kosongkan kolom password jika tidak ingin menggantinya.</p>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors duration-300">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                        
                        {/* Status Aktif */}
                        <div className="md:col-span-2 p-4 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                            <div>
                                <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Status Karyawan Aktif</h4>
                                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Matikan sakelar ini jika karyawan sudah *resign* untuk mencabut akses sistem.</p>
                            </div>
                            <label className="relative inline-flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                <input type="checkbox" checked={data.is_active} onChange={e => setData('is_active', e.target.checked)} className="sr-only peer" />
                                <div className="w-11 h-6 bg-gray-200 peer-focus:outline-none rounded-full peer dark:bg-gray-700 peer-checked:after:translate-x-full peer-checked:after:border-white after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-[2px] after:left-[2px] after:bg-white after:border-gray-300 after:border after:rounded-full after:h-5 after:w-5 after:transition-all peer-checked:bg-[#48CFA4]"></div>
                            </label>
                        </div>

                        <div className="md:col-span-2">
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Nama Lengkap</label>
                            <input type="text" value={data.name} onChange={e => setData('name', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" />
                            <InputError message={errors.name} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div className="md:col-span-2">
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Email Akses</label>
                            <input type="email" value={data.email} onChange={e => setData('email', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:bg-white dark:focus:bg-[#212E36] focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 transition-colors" />
                            <InputError message={errors.email} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        {/* Dropdown Role & Branch (Persis seperti Create) */}
                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Hak Akses (Role)</label>
                            <select value={data.role_id} onChange={e => setData('role_id', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 uppercase">
                                {roles.map(r => <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.name}</option>)}
                            </select>
                            <InputError message={errors.role_id} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Cabang</label>
                            <select value={data.branch_id} onChange={e => setData('branch_id', e.target.value)} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">
                                {branches.map(b => <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>{b.name}</option>)}
                            </select>
                            <InputError message={errors.branch_id} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div className="md:col-span-2 pt-4">
                            <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-red-500 mb-4 border-b pb-2">Ubah Password (Opsional)</h4>
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Password Baru</label>
                            <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} value={data.password} onChange={e => setData('password', e.target.value)} placeholder="Biarkan kosong jika tidak diubah" className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100" />
                            <InputError message={errors.password} className="mt-2" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-2">Konfirmasi Password Baru</label>
                            <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} value={data.password_confirmation} onChange={e => setData('password_confirmation', e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-transparent rounded-xl focus:border-[#48CFA4] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48CFA4]/20 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100" />
                        </div>

                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-8 mt-4 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800">
                        <Link href={route('users.index')} className="px-6 py-3 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors flex items-center">Batal</Link>
                        <PrimaryButton type="submit" disabled={processing} className="px-8 text-base">
                            {processing ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan Perubahan'}
                        </PrimaryButton>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

FILE: resources/js/Pages/Users/Index.jsx

import AuthenticatedLayout from '@/Layouts/AuthenticatedLayout';
import { Head, Link, usePage, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import PrimaryButton from '@/Components/PrimaryButton';
import ConfirmModal from '@/Components/ConfirmModal'; // Import komponen baru kita
import { useState } from 'react';

const EditIcon = () => (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z"></path>
    </svg>
);

const DeleteIcon = () => (
    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
    </svg>
);

export default function Index({ users }) {
    const currentUser = usePage().props.auth.user;
    
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [userToDelete, setUserToDelete] = useState(null);
    const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);

    const confirmDelete = (user) => {
        setUserToDelete(user);
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    const executeDelete = () => {
        setIsDeleting(true);
        router.delete(route('users.destroy', userToDelete.id), {
            preserveScroll: true,
            onSuccess: () => {
                setIsDeleting(false);
                setIsModalOpen(false);
            },
            onError: () => setIsDeleting(false),
        });
    };

    return (
        <AuthenticatedLayout header="Manajemen Akun Pengguna">
            <Head title="Manajemen Akun" />

            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-100">Daftar Pengguna Sistem</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Kelola akses kredensial karyawan (Apoteker, Kasir, Admin).</p>
                </div>
                <PrimaryButton as="link" href={route('users.create')}>
                    <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M12 4v16m8-8H4"></path></svg>
                    Buat Akun Baru
                </PrimaryButton>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-white dark:bg-[#1E272E] rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 transition-colors duration-300">
                <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                    <thead className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase bg-gray-50 dark:bg-[#151E24] border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-800 tracking-wider">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Nama Karyawan</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Alamat Email</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Hak Akses (Role)</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold">Lokasi Cabang</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-center">Aksi</th> 
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        {users.map((u) => (
                            <tr key={u.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 dark:border-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-[#151E24] transition-colors">
                                <td className="px-6 py-5 font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                                    {u.name}
                                    {!u.is_active && (
                                        <span className="text-[10px] bg-red-100 text-red-600 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold">Nonaktif</span>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-5 font-medium text-blue-600 dark:text-[#6FD8E8]">{u.email}</td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-5">
                                    <span className="bg-[#EAF5F0] dark:bg-[#253939] text-[#246A55] dark:text-[#67E2B4] text-[11px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full uppercase">
                                        {u.role?.name || 'User'}
                                    </span>
                                </td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-5 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">{u.branch?.name || '-'}</td>
                                <td className="px-6 py-5 text-center">
                                    {u.id !== currentUser.id ? (
                                        <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-3">
                                            <Link href={route('users.edit', u.id)} className="p-2 text-[#48CFA4] bg-[#EAF5F0] hover:bg-[#48CFA4] hover:text-white dark:bg-[#253939] dark:text-[#67E2B4] dark:hover:bg-[#48CFA4] dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200">
                                                <EditIcon />
                                            </Link>
                                            <button onClick={() => confirmDelete(u)} className="p-2 text-red-500 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-500 dark:hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-all duration-200">
                                                <DeleteIcon />
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    ) : (
                                        <span className="text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-600 text-xs italic font-medium">Akun Anda</span>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        ))}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            {/* Pemanggilan Komponen Modal Universal */}
            <ConfirmModal 
                isOpen={isModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={executeDelete}
                isProcessing={isDeleting}
                title="Hapus Akun Karyawan?"
                message={
                    <>
                        Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus akses permanen untuk <span className="font-bold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200">{userToDelete?.name}</span>? Data yang dihapus tidak dapat dipulihkan.
                    </>
                }
            />

        </AuthenticatedLayout>
    );
}

FILE: resources/js/Utils/printHelper.js
=========================================================
export const printReceipt = (htmlContent) => {
    const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
    iframe.style.position = 'fixed';
    iframe.style.right = '0';
    iframe.style.bottom = '0';
    iframe.style.width = '0px';
    iframe.style.height = '0px';
    iframe.style.border = 'none';

    document.body.appendChild(iframe);

    const doc = iframe.contentWindow.document;
    doc.open();
    doc.write(`
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html>
            <head>
                <title>Cetak Struk Transaksi</title>
                <style>
                    body { font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; margin: 0; padding: 10px; }
                    .text-center { text-align: center; }
                    .bold { font-weight: bold; }
                    @page { size: auto; margin: 0mm; }
                </style>
            </head>
            <body>
                ${htmlContent}
            </body>
        </html>
    `);
    doc.close();

    iframe.contentWindow.focus();
    
    setTimeout(() => {
        iframe.contentWindow.print();
        
        // Listener event setelah dialog cetak ditutup
        iframe.contentWindow.onafterprint = () => {
            if (document.body.contains(iframe)) {
                document.body.removeChild(iframe);
            }
        };

        setTimeout(() => {
            if (document.body.contains(iframe)) {
                document.body.removeChild(iframe);
            }
        }, 2000);
    }, 250);
};
